<b>Psalms 1</b><br><b>1</b> Ubusisiwe umuntu ongahambi ngeseluleko sababi a, ongemi endleleni yezoni, ongahlali esihlalweni b sabagconayo c; <b>2</b> kodwa intokozo yakhe isemlayweni weNkosi d, uzindla ngomlayo wayo emini lebusuku e. <b>3</b> Njalo uzakuba njengesihlahla esihlanyelwe emifuleni yamanzi, esithela isithelo saso ngesikhathi saso f, ohlamvu laso kaliyikubuna g; lakho konke akwenzayo kuzaphumelela h. <b>4</b> Ababi kabanjalo, kodwa banjengamakhoba umoya owaphephulayo i. 
<b>5</b> Ngakho ababi kabayikuma ekwahlulelweni, lezoni enhlanganweni yabalungileyo j. <b>6</b> Ngoba iNkosi iyayazi indlela yabalungileyo k, kodwa indlela yababi izabhubha l. 
<b>Psalms 2</b><br><b>1</b> Kungani a izizwe zixokozela b, labantu benakana okuyize? <b>2</b> Amakhosi omhlaba azimisela c, lababusi benza ugobe ndawonye d bemelene leNkosi njalo bemelene logcotshiweyo wayo e, besithi f: <b>3</b> Asiqamule izibopho zabo, silahle intambo zabo zisuke kithi g. <b>4</b> Ohlezi emazulwini h uzahleka i, iNkosi izabahleka usulu j , k. <b>5</b> Khona izakhuluma kubo entukuthelweni yayo, ibethuse ngokuvutha kolaka lwayo. <b>6</b> Kube kanti mina ngibekile iNkosi yami phezu kweZiyoni, intaba yami engcwele. 
<b>7</b> Ngizalandisa ngesimiso: INkosi ithe kimi: Wena uyiNdodana yami, lamuhla ngikuzele l. <b>8</b> Cela kimi, ngizanika izizwe zibe yilifa lakho, lemikhawulo yomhlaba ibe yimfuyo yakho m. <b>9</b> Uzazephula n ngentonga yensimbi o, uziphahlaze njengembiza yebumba p. <b>10</b> Ngakho-ke hlakaniphani makhosi, lilayeke bahluleli bomhlaba. <b>11</b> Ikhonzeni iNkosi ngokwesaba, lithokoze ngokuthuthumela q, <b>12</b> liyange r iNdodana s, hlezi ithukuthele, libhubhe endleleni, nxa ulaka lwayo seluvutha kancinyane t. Babusisiwe u bonke abaphephela kuyo v. 
<b>Psalms 3</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, zande kangakanani izitha zami a! Banengi abangivukelayo b. <b>2</b> Banengi abathi ngomphefumulo wami: Kakulasindiso lwakhe kuNkulunkulu. Sela* c. <b>3</b> Kodwa wena Nkosi, uyisihlangu sami d, udumo lwami, lomphakamisi wekhanda lami e. <b>4</b> Ngakhala ngelizwi lami eNkosini f, yasingiphendula g isentabeni yayo engcwele h. Sela*. <b>5</b> Mina ngalala phansi, ngajumeka; ngavuka, ngoba iNkosi yangisekela i. <b>6</b> Kangiyikwesaba izinkulungwane ezilitshumi zabantu abangihanqileyo bemelana lami j. 
<b>7</b> Vuka, Nkosi! Ngisindisa, Nkulunkulu wami. Ngoba zonke izitha zami uzitshayile emhlathini k, wephule amazinyo ababi l. <b>8</b> Usindiso ngolweNkosi. Isibusiso sakho siphezu kwabantu bakho. Sela* m. 
<b>Psalms 4</b><br><b>1</b> Ngiphendula ekukhaleni kwami, Nkulunkulu wokulunga kwami. Ekucindezelweni wangenzela indawo ebanzi. Woba lomusa kimi, uzwe umkhuleko wami a. <b>2</b> Madodana abantu, koze kube nini liphendula udumo lwami lube lihlazo, lithanda okuyize, lidinga amanga? Sela* b. <b>3</b> Kodwa yazini ukuthi iNkosi izehlukanisele c owesaba uNkulunkulu d; iNkosi iyangizwa lapho ngikhala kuyo. <b>4</b> Thuthumelani, lingoni e; khulumani f lenhliziyo yenu embhedeni wenu, lithule. Sela* g. 
<b>5</b> Nikelani imihlatshelo yokulunga h, lithembe eNkosini i. <b>6</b> Banengi abathi: Ngubani ozasitshengisa okuhle? Nkosi, phakamisela j ukukhanya kobuso bakho phezu kwethu k. <b>7</b> Ubeke intokozo enhliziyweni yami l okwedlula isikhathi lapho amabele abo lewayini labo elitsha kwandile m. <b>8</b> Ngizacambalala n ngokuthula o, khonokho ngilale ubuthongo, ngoba wena Nkosi wedwa uyangihlalisa ngokulondolozeka p. 
<b>Psalms 5</b><br><b>1</b> Beka indlebe emazwini ami, Nkosi; unanzelele ukububula kwami a. <b>2</b> Lalela ilizwi lokukhala kwami, Nkosi yami loNkulunkulu wami b; ngoba ngikhuleka kuwe c. <b>3</b> Nkosi, ekuseni uzalizwa ilizwi lami d; ekuseni ngizahlela umkhuleko wami ngakuwe e, ngilinde f. <b>4</b> Ngoba kawusuye uNkulunkulu othokoza ebubini; omubi kayikuhlala lawe. <b>5</b> Abazikhukhumezayo kabayikuma phambi kwamehlo akho; uzonda bonke abenzi bobubi g. <b>6</b> Uzababhubhisa abaqamba amanga. INkosi iyamenyanya umuntu wegazi lenkohliso h. 
<b>7</b> Kodwa mina, ngizangena endlini yakho ngobukhulu besihawu sakho, ngikhothame i ngikhangele ethempelini lakho elingcwele, ngokukwesaba j. <b>8</b> Nkosi, ngikhokhelele ekulungeni kwakho ngenxa yezitha zami k, wenze indlela yakho iqonde phambi kwami l. <b>9</b> Ngoba kalikho iqiniso emlonyeni wazo, ingaphakathi yazo izinkohlakalo m; umphimbo wazo n ulingcwaba elivulekileyo o, zibamba amehlo ngolimi lwazo p. <b>10</b> Tshono uthi zilecala, Nkulunkulu, kaziwe ngamacebo azo; uzilahle ngenxa yobunengi beziphambeko zazo, ngoba zikuvukele. 
<b>11</b> Kodwa kabathabe q bonke abathembela kuwe r, bamemeze kokuphela ngentokozo, ngoba ubavikele; lalabo abathanda ibizo lakho kabajabule kuwe s. <b>12</b> Ngoba wena Nkosi, uzabusisa olungileyo, uzamhanqa ngomusa njengesihlangu esikhulu t. 
<b>Psalms 6</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, ungangikhuzi entukuthelweni yakho a, ungangijezisi ekuvutheni kolaka lwakho b. <b>2</b> Woba lomusa kimi, Nkosi, ngoba ngibuthakathaka c. Nkosi, ngisilisa mina, ngoba amathambo ami d ayakhathazeka e. <b>3</b> Lomphefumulo wami ukhathazekile kakhulu f: Kodwa wena, Nkosi, koze kube nini g? <b>4</b> Phenduka, Nkosi, khulula umphefumulo wami; ungisindise ngenxa yomusa wakho h. <b>5</b> Ngoba ekufeni kakukho ukukukhumbula; engcwabeni ngubani ozakubonga i? 
<b>6</b> Ngikhathele j yikububula kwami; ubusuku bonke ngintshezisa icansi lami, ngezinyembezi zami ngimanzise umbheda wami k. <b>7</b> Ilihlo lami lidliwe ngenxa yosizi, liyaguga ngenxa yezitha zami zonke l. <b>8</b> Sukani kimi m, lonke benzi bobubi n, ngoba iNkosi izwile ilizwi lokulila kwami o. <b>9</b> INkosi izwile ukuncenga kwami p; iNkosi izakwemukela umkhuleko wami q. <b>10</b> Zonke izitha zami zizayangeka zitshaywe luvalo kakhulu r; zizaphenduka, zihle ziyangeke s. 
<b>Psalms 7</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami, ngiyathembela kuwe a; ngisindisa kubo bonke abangizingelayo, ungikhulule b. <b>2</b> Hlezi adabule umphefumulo wami njengesilwane, edabudabula, kungekho okhululayo c. <b>3</b> Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami, uba ngikwenzile lokhu d, uba kukhona okubi ezandleni zami e, <b>4</b> uba ngiphindisele okubi kosekuthuleni lami f (kuphela ngaphanga oyisitha sami ngaphandle kwesizatho g), 
<b>5</b> isitha kasizingele umphefumulo wami, siwufice, siyinyathelele impilo yami emhlabathini, senze udumo lwami luhlale ethulini h. Sela*. <b>6</b> Vuka, Nkosi, ngolaka lwakho, ziphakamise i ngenxa yentukuthelo yezitha zami j; vuka ungimele k; ulayile isahlulelo l. <b>7</b> Ngakho inhlangano yezizwe izakuhanqa; buya ngaphezulu phezu kwayo. <b>8</b> INkosi izakwahlulela izizwe. Ngahlulela, Nkosi m, njengokulunga kwami n lanjengobuqotho bami obukimi o. 
<b>9</b> Ake buphele ububi bababi, kodwa umqinise olungileyo, ngoba ohlola inhliziyo lezinso p nguNkulunkulu olungileyo q. <b>10</b> Isihlangu sami sikuNkulunkulu osindisa abaqotho enhliziyweni r. <b>11</b> UNkulunkulu ngumahluleli olungileyo, njalo nguNkulunkulu othukuthelela omubi insuku zonke. <b>12</b> Uba engaphenduki, uzalola inkemba yakhe s; useligobile idandili lakhe t walilungisa u. <b>13</b> Usemlungisele izikhali zokufa, wenzela abazingeli v imitshoko yakhe etshisayo w. 
<b>14</b> Khangela, uhelelwa ngobubi x, akhulelwe yikona y, azale amanga z. <b>15</b> Agebhe umgodi awugubhe, abesewela egodini alenzileyo aa. <b>16</b> Ububi bakhe buzabuyela phezu kwekhanda lakhe, lodlame lwakhe lwehlele enkanda yakhe ab. <b>17</b> Ngizayidumisa iNkosi njengokulunga kwayo, ngihlabelele ibizo leNkosi ephezu konke ac. 
<b>Psalms 8</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, Nkosi yethu, lilenkazimulo kangakanani ibizo lakho emhlabeni wonke a! Omise ubukhosi bakho ngaphezu kwamazulu b. <b>2</b> Emlonyeni wezingane lowabamunyayo umisile amandla ngenxa yezitha zakho c, ukuze uthulise isitha lomphindiseli d. <b>3</b> Lapho ngikhangela amazulu akho, umsebenzi weminwe yakho, inyanga lenkanyezi owazimisayo e, <b>4</b> uyini umuntu ukuthi umkhumbule f, lendodana yomuntu ukuthi uyihambele g? <b>5</b> Kanti wamenza waba ngaphansi kancinyane kulengilosi, wamethwesa umqhele wobukhosi h lodumo i. 
<b>6</b> Wamenza wabusa phezu kwemisebenzi yezandla zakho j, konke wakubeka ngaphansi kwenyawo zakhe k, <b>7</b> izimvu lenkabi zonke, kanye lezinyamazana zeganga, <b>8</b> inyoni zamazulu, lezinhlanzi zolwandle, okudlula ezindleleni zenlwandle l. <b>9</b> Nkosi, Nkosi yethu, lilenkazimulo kangakani ibizo lakho emhlabeni wonke m! 
<b>Psalms 9</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizakudumisa, Nkosi, ngenhliziyo yami yonke, ngilandise zonke izimangaliso zakho a. <b>2</b> Ngizathaba ngithokoze kuwe b, ngihlabele indumiso ebizweni lakho c, wena oPhezukonke d. <b>3</b> Lapho izitha zami zibuyela emuva, zizakuwa, zibhubhe phambi kwakho. <b>4</b> Ngoba ulimele ilungelo lami lodaba lwami, wahlala esihlalweni sobukhosi, wahlulela ngokulungileyo e. <b>5</b> Uzikhuzile izizwe, wabhubhisa omubi f; walesula ibizo labo kuze kube nini lanini g. 
<b>6</b> Izitha ziphelile, izincithakalo eziphakade; uyidilizile imizi; ukukhunjulwa kwabo kubhubhe kanye labo h. <b>7</b> Kodwa iNkosi izahlala kuze kube phakade i, isihlalo sayo sobukhosi isilungisele isahlulelo j. <b>8</b> Yona-ke izawahlulela umhlaba ngokulunga k, ibagwebe abantu l ngokuqonda m , n. <b>9</b> Njalo iNkosi izakuba yisiphephelo socindezelweyo o, isiphephelo ngezikhathi zokuhlupheka p. <b>10</b> Labalaziyo ibizo lakho bazathemba kuwe, ngoba wena Nkosi, kawubatshiyanga abakudingayo q. 
<b>11</b> Hlabelani indumiso eNkosini ehlezi eZiyoni, limemezele phakathi kwabantu izenzo zayo r. <b>12</b> Nxa idinga igazi, iyabakhumbula, kayikhohlwa ukukhala kwabahlutshwayo s. <b>13</b> Woba lomusa kimi, Nkosi, ubone uhlupho lwami oluvela kwabangizondayo t, wena ongiphakamisayo emasangweni okufa u, <b>14</b> ukuze ngilandise yonke indumiso yakho emasangweni endodakazi yeZiyoni, ngithokoze v kusindiso lwakho w , x. <b>15</b> Izizwe zitshone emgodini ezawenzayo y, embuleni ezalithiyayo unyawo lwazo lubanjiwe z. 
<b>16</b> INkosi izazisile, yenza isahlulelo aa; omubi uthiywe emsebenzini wezandla zakhe. Higayoni. Sela*. <b>17</b> Abakhohlakeleyo bazabuyela esihogweni, zonke izizwe ezimkhohlwayo uNkulunkulu ab. <b>18</b> Ngoba oswelayo kayikukhohlakala kokuphela; ithemba ac labayanga ad kaliyikunyamalala phakade ae. <b>19</b> Vuka, Nkosi, umuntu kanganqobi; izizwe kazigwetshwe phambi kwakho. <b>20</b> Zitshayise ngovalo, Nkosi, izizwe kazikwazi ukuthi zingabantu nje. Sela* af. 
<b>Psalms 10</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, umeleni khatshana? Ucatshelani ezikhathini zokuhlupheka a? <b>2</b> Omubi ekuzigqajeni uyamzingelisisa umyanga b; kababanjwe c ngamagobe abawacebileyo d. <b>3</b> Ngoba omubi uyazikhukhumeza ngesifiso somphefumulo wakhe, uyasibusisa isihwaba, ayeyise iNkosi. <b>4</b> Omubi, ngokuzigqaja kobuso bakhe uthi: Kakubizi e; kakho uNkulunkulu f emicabangweni yakhe yonke. <b>5</b> Indlela zakhe ziqinile isikhathi sonke; izahlulelo zakho ziphakeme zikhatshana laye g; izitha zakhe zonke uyazivuthela h. 
<b>6</b> Uthi enhliziyweni yakhe i: Kangiyikunyikinywa j; ngoba esizukulwaneni lesizukulwana kangiyikuba sebunzimeni k. <b>7</b> Umlomo wakhe ugcwele ukuthuka lezinkohliso l lobuqili m; ngaphansi kolimi lwakhe n kukhona ukona lobubi o. <b>8</b> Uhlezi endaweni yokucathama yemizi, ezindaweni ezisithekileyo ubulala ongelacala p; amehlo akhe akhangele othithibeleyo ngasese q. <b>9</b> Uyacatsha ekusithekeni r njengesilwane s ebhalwini lwaso t; ucatshela ukumbamba ongumyanga, uyabamba ongumyanga ngokumhudulela embuleni lakhe u. 
<b>10</b> Uyaqutha, uyakhothama v, njalo umyanga awe ngokulamandla kwakhe w. <b>11</b> Uthi enhliziyweni yakhe: UNkulunkulu ukhohliwe x, ufihle ubuso bakhe y, kasoze abone lanini z. <b>12</b> Sukuma, Nkosi! Nkulunkulu, phakamisa isandla sakho, ungabakhohlwa abahluphekayo. <b>13</b> Kungani omubi edelela uNkulunkulu aa? Uthi enhliziyweni yakhe ab: Kawuyikukubiza ac. <b>14</b> Uyabona, ngoba wena uyakhangela ububi lenhlupheko, ukukubeka esandleni sakho ad. Umyanga ae uyazinikela kuwe af; wena ungumsizi wezintandane ag. 
<b>15</b> Yephula ingalo yokhohlakeleyo lomubi ah; dinga inkohlakalo yakhe ukuze ungabe usayithola ai. <b>16</b> UJehova yiNkosi kuze kube nini lanini; izizwe zibhubhile elizweni layo aj. <b>17</b> Nkosi, uzwile isifiso sabathobekileyo ak, uzaqinisa inhliziyo yabo, wenze indlebe yakho izwe, <b>18</b> ukwahlulela intandane al locindezelweyo am, ukuze umuntu wasemhlabeni angabe esesabeka an. 
<b>Psalms 11</b><br><b>1</b> Ngithembela eNkosini a. Lingatsho njani emphefumulweni wami: Balekela entabeni yenu njengenyoni b. <b>2</b> Ngoba, khangela, ababi bayagobisa idandili c, balungisa umtshoko wabo entanjeni d, ukuze batshoke emnyameni abaqotho ngenhliziyo e. <b>3</b> Nxa izisekelo zidilizwa f, angenzani olungileyo g? <b>4</b> INkosi h isethempelini layo elingcwele i; iNkosi, isihlalo sayo sobukhosi sisemazulwini j; amehlo ayo ayakhangela k, inkophe zayo ziyahlola, abantwana babantu. 
<b>5</b> INkosi iyamhlola olungileyo, kodwa omubi lothanda ubudlwangudlwangu, umphefumulo wayo uyamzonda l. <b>6</b> Uzanisa phezu kwababi imijibila, umlilo, lesolufa* m, lomoya otshisayo n, kuzakuba yisabelo senkezo yabo o. <b>7</b> Ngoba ilungile iNkosi, iyathanda ukulunga p; ubuso bayo buyabona oqotho q. 
<b>Psalms 12</b><br><b>1</b> Siza, Nkosi, ngoba owesaba uNkulunkulu kasekho, ngoba abathembekileyo banyamalele basuka ebantwaneni babantu a. <b>2</b> Bakhuluma okuyize, ngulowo lalowo lomakhelwane wakhe b; indebe ezilalisayo, bakhuluma nganhliziyombili c. <b>3</b> INkosi izaquma zonke indebe ezilalisayo d, ulimi olukhuluma izinto zokuzidla ▼▼ zokuzidla: Heb ezinkulu.; <b>4</b> abathi: Sizanqoba ngolimi lwethu, indebe zethu ngezethu; ngubani oyinkosi phezu kwethu? 
<b>5</b> Ngenxa yokubandezelwa f kwabayanga, ngenxa yokububula kwabaswelayo g, sengizavuka, kutsho iNkosi h. Ngizabeka ekulondolozekeni amvuthelayo i. <b>6</b> Amazwi eNkosi angamazwi ahlambulukileyo j, isiliva esizanywe esithandweni senhlabathi k, sihlanjululwe l kasikhombisa m. <b>7</b> Wena, Nkosi, uzabagcina, ubavikele kulesisizukulwana kuze kube nininini. <b>8</b> Ababi bayahambahamba inhlangothi zonke, lapho amanyala ephakanyiswa phakathi kwabantwana babantu. 
<b>Psalms 13</b><br><b>1</b> Koze kube nini, Nkosi, ungikhohlwa a kokuphela b? Koze kube nini ungifihlela ubuso bakho c? <b>2</b> Koze kube nini ngizicebisa emphefumulweni wami, ngilosizi enhliziyweni yami insuku zonke? Koze kube nini isitha sami siphakanyiswa phezu kwami? <b>3</b> Khangela, ungiphendule, Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami; khanyisa amehlo ami d hlezi ngilale ubuthongo bokufa e, <b>4</b> hlezi isitha sami sithi: Sengimehlule f; labangihluphayo bathokoze lapho nginyikinywa g. 
<b>5</b> Kodwa mina ngithembele emuseni wakho h, inhliziyo yami izathaba ngosindiso lwakho i , j. <b>6</b> Ngizayihlabelela iNkosi, ngoba ingenzele okuhle kakhulu k. 
<b>Psalms 14</b><br><b>1</b> Isithutha a sithi enhliziyweni yaso b: KakulaNkulunkulu c. Bonakele, benza imisebenzi eyenyanyekayo d; kakho owenza okuhle e. <b>2</b> INkosi yakhangela phansi isemazulwini phezu kwabantwana babantu f, ukubona ukuthi ukhona yini g oqedisisayo h, odinga uNkulunkulu i. <b>3</b> Baphambukile bonke, bebonke bonakele; kakho owenza okuhle, kakho loyedwa j. <b>4</b> Kabazi yini bonke abenza okubi k, abadla abantu bami njengoba besidla isinkwa l? Kabayibizi iNkosi m. 
<b>5</b> Lapho besaba lokwesaba n, ngoba uNkulunkulu usesizukulwaneni solungileyo o. <b>6</b> Liliyangisile icebo lomyanga p, ngoba iNkosi iyisiphephelo sakhe q. <b>7</b> Kungathi usindiso lukaIsrayeli lungavela eZiyoni r! Lapho iNkosi ibuyisa ukuthunjwa kwabantu bayo, uJakobe uzathaba, uIsrayeli athokoze s. 
<b>Psalms 15</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, ngubani ozahlala ethenteni lakho a? Ngubani ozakwakha entabeni yakho engcwele b? <b>2</b> Ngulowo ohamba ngobuqotho c, lowenza okulungileyo d, lokhuluma iqiniso enhliziyweni yakhe e, <b>3</b> ongahlebiyo f ngolimi lwakhe g, ongenzi okubi kumngane wakhe, longaphakamiseli umakhelwane wakhe ihlazo; <b>4</b> olahliweyo uyenyanyeka emehlweni akhe, kodwa uyabahlonipha abayesabayo iNkosi; ofunga ngokuzilimaza, kodwa kaphenduki, 
<b>5</b> onganiki imali yakhe ukuthi azuze inzuzo h, longemukeli umvuzo wokuthengwa ngongelacala i. Owenza lezizinto kayikunyikinywa kuze kube nininini j. 
<b>Psalms 16</b><br><b>1</b> Ngilondoloza, Nkulunkulu, ngoba ngithembela kuwe a. <b>2</b> Ngithe eNkosini: UyiNkosi yami, ukulunga kwami kakufiki kuwe b. <b>3</b> Kodwa kubangcwele abasemhlabeni, labahlekazi, ontokozo yami yonke ikubo c. <b>4</b> Zizakwanda insizi zabagijimela omunye unkulunkulu d; kangiyikunikela iminikelo yabo yokunathwayo yegazi e; lamabizo abo kangiyikuwaphatha endebeni zami f. <b>5</b> INkosi iyisabelo g selifa lami h lesenkezo yami i; wena uphethe isabelo sami j. 
<b>6</b> Imizila iwele kimi k ezindaweni ezithokozisayo l; yebo, ngilelifa elibukekayo. <b>7</b> Ngizayibonga iNkosi engelulekayo; lebusuku m izinso zami ziyangiqondisa n. <b>8</b> Ngibekile o iNkosi phambi kwami njalonjalo p; ngoba ingakwesokunene sami q, kangiyikunyikinywa r. <b>9</b> Ngakho inhliziyo yami iyathokoza, lodumo lwami luyajabula s, lenyama yami izahlala ivikelekile. <b>10</b> Ngoba kawutshiyanga umphefumulo wami esihogweni t, njalo kawuyikunikela u ongcwele wakho v ukuthi abone ukubola w. 
<b>11</b> Uzangazisa indlela yempilo x; ukugcwala kwentokozo kusebukhoneni bakho y, ngakwesokunene sakho kukhona izinjabulo phakade z. 
<b>Psalms 17</b><br><b>1</b> Zwana ukulunga, Nkosi, ulalele ukukhala kwami a; ubeke indlebe emkhulekweni wami ongaphumi ezindebeni ezilenkohliso. <b>2</b> Ukwahlulelwa kwami kakuphume ebukhoneni bakho b, amehlo akho kawabone izinto eziqondileyo c. <b>3</b> Uyilingile inhliziyo yami d, wangihambela e ebusuku f; ungihlolile, kawuficanga lutho; ngihlosile, umlomo wami kawuyikuphambuka g. <b>4</b> Mayelana lezenzo zabantu, ngelizwi lendebe zakho, mina ngizinqandile ezindleleni zomchithi, 
<b>5</b> ukusekela izinyathelo zami h emikhondweni yakho i, ukuze inyawo zami zingatsheleli. <b>6</b> Mina ngikubizile j, ngoba uzangiphendula, Nkulunkulu k; beka indlebe yakho kimi, uzwe inkulumo yami l. <b>7</b> Tshengisa ngokumangalisayo uthandolomusa wakho m, wena osindisa ngesandla sakho sokunene labo abathembela kuwe kwabavukelayo n. <b>8</b> Ngigcine njengenhlamvu yelihlo o; ungifihle emthunzini wempiko zakho p, <b>9</b> kubo ubuso bababi abangicindezelayo, izitha zempilo yami ezingihanqileyo. 
<b>10</b> Bayazivalela ngamahwahwa abo q; ngomlomo wabo bakhuluma ngokuzigqaja r. <b>11</b> Sebesihanqile emanyathelweni ethu s; amehlo abo bawamise besithobele emhlabathini t. <b>12</b> Unjengesilwane esikhuthalele ukudabula, lanjengesilwane esitsha esiquthileyo endaweni ezisithekileyo. <b>13</b> Vuka, Nkosi! Uhlangabeze ubuso bakhe, umphosele phansi u. Khulula umphefumulo wami komubi, oyinkemba yakho v, 
<b>14</b> kubo abantu, abayisandla sakho, Nkosi, ebantwini bomhlaba w, osabelo sabo sikulokhukuphila x, osisu sabo usigcwalisa ngokuligugu kwakho y; bagcwele abantwana, njalo batshiyele izingane zabo okuseleyo kwabo z. <b>15</b> Mina, ngizakhangela ubuso bakho ekulungeni aa, ngeneliswe ab yisimo sakho ac ekuphaphameni kwami ad. 
<b>Psalms 18</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizakuthanda, Nkosi, mandla ami c. <b>2</b> INkosi ilidwala lami, lenqaba yami, lomkhululi wami; uNkulunkulu wami, idwala lami, engizaphephela kulo, isihlangu sami, lophondo losindiso lwami, inqaba yami ephakemeyo d. <b>3</b> Ngizabiza iNkosi edumisekayo, ngakho ngizasindiswa ezitheni zami. <b>4</b> Izibopho zokufa zangizingelezela, lezikhukhula zobubi zangethusa. <b>5</b> Izibopho zesihogo zangigombolozela, imijibila yokufa yayiphambi kwami e. 
<b>6</b> Ekuhluphekeni kwami ngayibiza iNkosi, ngakhala kuNkulunkulu wami; walizwa ilizwi lami esethempelini lakhe, lokukhala kwami kweza phambi kwakhe, endlebeni zakhe f. <b>7</b> Khona umhlaba wazamazama, wanyikinyeka, lezisekelo zezintaba zaqhaqhazela, zazamazama, ngoba wayethukuthele g. <b>8</b> Kwenyuka intuthu emakhaleni akhe, lomlilo ophuma emlonyeni wakhe waqothula; amalahle avuthiswa yiwo h. <b>9</b> Wasekhothamisa amazulu, wehlela phansi, lomnyama onzima wawungaphansi kwenyawo zakhe. 
<b>10</b> Wasegada ikherubhi*, waphapha, wandiza phezu kwempiko zomoya i. <b>11</b> Wamisa umnyama waba yindawo yakhe yensitha, idumba lakhe inhlangothi zonke zakhe, amanzi amnyama, amayezi amnyama omkhathi j. <b>12</b> Ekukhazimuleni okuphambi kwakhe amayezi akhe amnyama edlula, isiqhotho lamalahle omlilo k. <b>13</b> INkosi yasiduma emazulwini, oPhezukonke wazwakalisa ilizwi lakhe, isiqhotho lamalahle omlilo l. <b>14</b> Yasithuma imitshoko yayo, yabachitha; lemibane eminengi, yabaphaphathekisa. 
<b>15</b> Khona kwabonakala imisele yamanzi, kwembulwa izisekelo zomhlaba ekusoleni kwakho m, Nkosi, ekuvutheleni komoya wamakhala akho. <b>16</b> Yathuma iphezulu, yangibamba, yangenyula emanzini amanengi. <b>17</b> Yangophula esitheni sami esilamandla, lakibo abangizondayo, ngoba babelamandla kulami n. <b>18</b> Babephambi kwami osukwini lwenhlupheko yami, kodwa iNkosi yaba yisisekelo sami o. <b>19</b> Yangikhuphela endaweni ebanzi, yangikhulula ngoba yayithokoza ngami. 
<b>20</b> INkosi yangivuza njengokulunga kwami; yangibuyisela njengokuhlanzeka kwezandla zami. <b>21</b> Ngoba ngizilondolozile indlela zeNkosi, kangisukanga kuNkulunkulu wami ngenkohlakalo. <b>22</b> Ngoba zonke izahlulelo zayo zaziphambi kwami, lezimiso zayo kangiziphambulanga kimi p. <b>23</b> Njalo ngangiqondile kuyo, ngizinqandile ebubini bami. <b>24</b> Ngakho iNkosi ingibuyisele njengokulunga kwami, njengokuhlanzeka kwezandla zami phambi kwamehlo ayo. <b>25</b> Kolesihawu uzaziveza ulesihawu; koqotho uzaziveza uqotho; 
<b>26</b> kohlanzekileyo uzaziveza uhlanzekile; lakophambukileyo uzaziveza uphambukile. <b>27</b> Ngoba wena uzabasindisa abantu abahlutshwayo; kodwa amehlo aphakemeyo uzawehlisela phansi q. <b>28</b> Ngoba wena uzalumathisa isibane sami; iNkosi, uNkulunkulu wami, izakhanyisa ubumnyama bami. <b>29</b> Ngoba ngawe ngigijimile ngadabula phakathi kwebutho, langoNkulunkulu wami ngeqe umthangala. <b>30</b> UNkulunkulu, indlela yakhe iphelele; ilizwi leNkosi lihloliwe; uyisihlangu kubo bonke abaphephela kuye. 
<b>31</b> Ngoba ngubani onguNkulunkulu ngaphandle kweNkosi? Njalo ngubani olidwala ngaphandle kukaNkulunkulu wethu? <b>32</b> NguNkulunkulu ongibhincisa amandla r , s, owenza indlela yami iphelele t. <b>33</b> Owenza inyawo zami zibe njengezezimpala, wangimisa ezingqongeni zami. <b>34</b> Ofundisela izandla zami impi, ukuze idandili lethusi ligotshiswe zingalo zami. <b>35</b> Unginikile lesihlangu sosindiso lwakho, lesandla sakho sokunene u singisekele v, lobumnene bakho bungikhulisile. 
<b>36</b> Wenze zaba banzi izinyathelo zami ngaphansi kwami, ukuze inyawo zami zingatsheleli. <b>37</b> Ngixotshene lezitha zami, ngazifica, kangibuyanga ngaze ngaziqeda. <b>38</b> Ngazigwaza, zaze zehluleka ukuvuka, zawa ngaphansi kwenyawo zami. <b>39</b> Ngoba ungibhincise amandla kusenzelwa impi, wabakhothamisela phansi kwami abangivukelayo. <b>40</b> Unginike intamo yezitha zami, labo abangizondayo ngababhubhisa. <b>41</b> Bakhala, kodwa engekho umsindisi; eNkosini, kodwa kayibaphendulanga w. 
<b>42</b> Ngasengibacholisisa njengothuli phambi komoya, ngabachitha njengodaka lwezitalada. <b>43</b> Wangikhulula ekuphikiseni kwabantu, wangimisa ngaba yinhloko yezizwe; abantu engangingabazi bangisebenzela. <b>44</b> Bangenela ukuzwa ngendlebe bazangilalela, abezizweni bazazithoba kimi ngokuzenzisa. <b>45</b> Abezizweni bazabuna, baphume ezinqabeni zabo bethuthumela. <b>46</b> INkosi iyaphila; njalo kalidunyiswe idwala lami, kaphakanyiswe uNkulunkulu wosindiso lwami. <b>47</b> UNkulunkulu onginika impindiselo, lowehlisela izizwe ngaphansi kwami x; 
<b>48</b> ongikhulula ezitheni zami; yebo, phezu kwabangivukelayo wangiphakamisa, emuntwini wodlakela wangikhulula. <b>49</b> Ngenxa yalokhu y ngizakudumisa, Nkosi, phakathi kwezizwe, ngihlabelele ibizo lakho indumiso z. <b>50</b> Iyanika inkosi yayo usindiso olukhulu, isenzela ogcotshiweyo wayo umusa, kuDavida, lenzalweni yakhe kuze kube nininini aa. 
<b>Psalms 19</b><br><b>1</b> Amazulu alandisa ngodumo lukaNkulunkulu, lomkhathi utshumayela umsebenzi wezandla zakhe a. <b>2</b> Usuku kusuku luphumisela inkulumo b, lobusuku kubusuku butshengisa ulwazi c. <b>3</b> Kakukho nkulumo, kawakho lamazwi, lapho ilizwi lawo elingezwakali khona. <b>4</b> Intambo yawo d iphumele emhlabeni wonke, lamazwi awo emkhawulweni womhlaba. Ulimisele ilanga ithente kuwo e. <b>5</b> Elinjengomyeni f ephuma ekamelweni lakhe g, lithokoza h njengeqhawe ligijima endleleni i. 
<b>6</b> Ukuphuma kwalo kusukela emkhawulweni wamazulu, lokuzingeleza kwalo kuze kube semikhawulweni yawo; njalo kakukho okufihliweyo ekutshiseni kwalo j. <b>7</b> Umlayo weNkosi uphelele, uphendula umphefumulo; ubufakazi beNkosi k buqinisile l, buhlakaniphisa ongelalwazi m; <b>8</b> izimiso zeNkosi zilungile, zithokozisa inhliziyo n; umthetho weNkosi uhlambulukile o, ukhanyisa amehlo p. <b>9</b> Ukuyesaba iNkosi kuhlanzekile, kumi kuze kube nininini q; izahlulelo zeNkosi ziliqiniso, zilungile ngokupheleleyo. 
<b>10</b> Ziyaloyiseka kakhulu kulegolide r, yebo kulegolide elicwengekileyo elinengi s; njalo zimnandi kuloluju t lolujuluka ohlangeni loluju u. <b>11</b> Futhi inceku yakho iyelulekwa ngazo; ekuzigcineni kulomvuzo omkhulu v. <b>12</b> Ngubani oqedisisa iziphosiso zakhe w? Ngikhulula kwezisithekileyo x. <b>13</b> Igcine inceku yakho lezonweni zokuzincoma y; kazingabusi phezu kwami. Khona ngizakuba ngoqondileyo, ngibe msulwa esiphambekweni esikhulu z. 
<b>14</b> Amazwi omlomo wami lezizindlo zenhliziyo yami kakwemukeleke phambi kwakho aa, Nkosi, dwala lami lomhlengi wami ab. 
<b>Psalms 20</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi ikuphendule osukwini lokuhlupheka; ibizo likaNkulunkulu kaJakobe likuvikele. <b>2</b> Kayikuthumele usizo oluvela endaweni engcwele a, ikusekele iseZiyoni b. <b>3</b> Kayikhumbule yonke iminikelo yakho, njalo yemukele umnikelo wakho wokutshiswa. Sela*. <b>4</b> Ikunike njengokwenhliziyo yakho, njalo igcwalise icebo lakho lonke c. <b>5</b> Simemeze ngentokozo esindisweni lwakho d, lebizweni likaNkulunkulu wethu siphakamise iziboniso e! INkosi igcwalise konke ukucela kwakho f. 
<b>6</b> Sengisazi ukuthi iNkosi isindisa ogcotshiweyo wayo g; izamphendula isemazulwini ayo angcwele ngamandla asindisayo esandla sayo sokunene. <b>7</b> Abanye bathembela enqoleni, labanye emabhizeni, kodwa thina sizakhumbula ibizo leNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu h. <b>8</b> Bona bakhothanyisiwe bawa, kodwa thina sisukumile simi i. <b>9</b> Jehova, sindisa; inkosi isiphendule osukwini lokukhala kwethu. 
<b>Psalms 21</b><br><b>1</b> Jehova, inkosi izajabula emandleni akho, lesindisweni lwakho izathaba kangakanani a! <b>2</b> Uyinikile isifiso senhliziyo yayo, kawuyincitshanga isicelo sendebe zayo b. Sela*. <b>3</b> Ngoba uyihlangabeze ngezibusiso zokuhle c, wayethwesa umqhele d wegolide elihle ekhanda layo e. <b>4</b> Yacela impilo kuwe, wayinika yona f, ubude bezinsuku kuze kube nini lanini g. <b>5</b> Lukhulu udumo lwayo ekusindiseni kwakho h, inkazimulo i lobukhosi j , k wakubeka phezu kwayo l. 
<b>6</b> Ngoba uyenze yabusiseka kakhulu kuze kube nininini, uyayithokozisa ngenjabulo ebukhoneni bakho m. <b>7</b> Ngoba inkosi ithembela kuJehova, langomusa woPhezukonke kayiyikunyikinywa n. <b>8</b> Isandla sakho sizazithola zonke izitha zakho, lesandla sakho sokunene sizabathola labo abakuzondayo o. <b>9</b> Uzabenza babe njengesithando somlilo p esikhathini solaka lwakho q. INkosi izabaginya ekuthukutheleni kwayo r, lomlilo uzabaqothula s. <b>10</b> Uzachitha isithelo sabo emhlabeni, lenzalo yabo phakathi kwabantwana babantu t. 
<b>11</b> Ngoba bezimisela okubi bemelene lawe, beceba ugobe, bazakwehluleka u. <b>12</b> Ngoba uzabafulathelisa, ngezintambo zedandili uzalungisa imitshoko imelane lobuso babo v. <b>13</b> Phakama, Nkosi, ngamandla akho! Sizahlabela sidumise amandla akho. 
<b>Psalms 22</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu wami, Nkulunkulu wami, ungitshiyeleni a na? Ukhatshana lokungisiza, lamazwi okubhonga kwami b? <b>2</b> Nkulunkulu wami, ngiyakhala emini, kodwa kawungiphenduli, lebusuku, kodwa kangilakho ukuthula c. <b>3</b> Kodwa wena ungcwele, ohlezi ezibongweni zikaIsrayeli d. <b>4</b> Obaba bethu bathemba kuwe, bathemba, wasubakhulula. <b>5</b> Bakhala kuwe, bakhululwa e; bathembela kuwe, kabayangekanga f. <b>6</b> Kodwa ngingumswenya, kangisuye muntu g, ihlazo labantu h, ngeyiswa ngabantu i. 
<b>7</b> Bonke abangibonayo bayangihleka usulu j, bakhamisa indebe, banikine ikhanda k, <b>8</b> besithi: Wathembela eNkosini l; kayimkhulule m, imophule, ngoba yathokoza kuye n. <b>9</b> Kodwa nguwe owangikhupha esiswini o , p, wangenza ngathemba ngisemabeleni kamama. <b>10</b> Ngalahlelwa phezu kwakho kusukela esizalweni q; kusukela esiswini sikamama unguNkulunkulu wami r. <b>11</b> Ungabi khatshana lami, ngoba uhlupho luseduze, ngoba kakho osizayo. <b>12</b> Izinkunzi ezinengi zingihanqile, ezilamandla s zeBashani t zingizingelezele u. 
<b>13</b> Zangikhamisela umlomo wazo, isilwane esiphangayo lesibhongayo v. <b>14</b> Ngithululiwe njengamanzi w, lamathambo ami wonke ehlukene; inhliziyo yami x injengengcino y, incibilikile phakathi kwemibilini yami z. <b>15</b> Amandla ami omile njengodengezi aa, lolimi lwami lunamathele emihlathini yami ab; ungibeke ethulini lokufa. <b>16</b> Ngoba izinja zingigombolozele ac; inhlangano yababi ingihanqile ad; izandla zami lenyawo zami bazibhobozile ae. <b>17</b> Ngingabala wonke amathambo ami; bona bayakhangela bayangijolozela af. 
<b>18</b> Babelana izigqoko zami, benza inkatho yokuphosa ngezembatho zami ag. <b>19</b> Kodwa, wena Nkosi, ungabi khatshana; mandla ami, phangisa ukungisiza ah. <b>20</b> Khulula umphefumulo wami kuyo inkemba, wona wodwa owami emandleni enja ai. <b>21</b> Ngisindisa emlonyeni wesilwane aj; ngoba empondweni zezinyathi ak ungiphendule al. <b>22</b> Ngizalandisa ibizo lakho am kubafowethu an, phakathi kwebandla ngizakudumisa. <b>23</b> Lina elesaba iNkosi, idumiseni ao; lonke nzalo kaJakobe, ihlonipheni; liyesabe, lonke nzalo kaIsrayeli ap. 
<b>24</b> Ngoba kayidelelanga kayinengwanga yinhlupheko yohluphekayo; njalo kayimfihlelanga ubuso bayo; kodwa lapho ekhala kuyo, yezwa aq. <b>25</b> Indumiso yami izakuba ngawe ebandleni elikhulu ar; ngizakhokha izifungo zami phambi kwabayesabayo as. <b>26</b> Abamnene bazakudla basuthe; bazayidumisa iNkosi labo abayidingayo at. Inhliziyo yenu au izaphila kuze kube phakade av. <b>27</b> Imikhawulo yonke yomhlaba izakhumbula, iphendukele eNkosini aw; lezizukulwana zonke zezizwe zizakhonza phambi kwakho ax. 
<b>28</b> Ngoba umbuso ungoweNkosi, njalo iyabusa phakathi kwezizwe ay. <b>29</b> Bonke abakhulupheleyo bomhlaba bazakudla bakhonze az; bonke abehlela othulini bazakhothama phambi kwayo ba, longagcini umphefumulo wakhe uphila. <b>30</b> Inzalo izayikhonza; izabalelwa eNkosini kuso isizukulwana bb. <b>31</b> Bazakuza bamemezele ukulunga kwayo bc ebantwini abazazalwa, ukuthi ikwenzile bd. 
<b>Psalms 23</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi ingumelusi wami a, kangiyikuswela b. <b>2</b> Iyangilalisa emadlelweni aluhlaza c; iyangiholela emanzini okuphumula d. <b>3</b> Ibuyisa umphefumulo wami, ingiholele endleleni zokulunga e ngenxa yebizo layo f. <b>4</b> Yebo, loba ngihamba esihotsheni g sethunzi lokufa h, kangiyikwesaba okubi i, ngoba wena ulami; intonga yakho lodondolo lwakho, khona kuyangiduduza j. <b>5</b> Uyalungisa itafula phambi kwami k ngaphambi kwezitha zami l; ugcoba ikhanda lami ngamafutha m, inkezo yami iyaphuphuma n. 
<b>6</b> Isibili okuhle lesihawu kuzangilandela insuku zonke zempilo yami; njalo ngizahlala endlini yeNkosi kuze kube nininini ▼▼ nininini, Heb sebudeni bezinsuku.. 
<b>Psalms 24</b><br><b>1</b> Umhlaba ungoweNkosi a, lokugcwala kwawo, ilizwe, labahlala kulo b. <b>2</b> Ngoba yona yawusekela c phezu kwezinlwandle, yawumisa phezu kwemifula d. <b>3</b> Ngubani ozakwenyukela entabeni yeNkosi e? Kumbe ngubani ozakuma endaweni yayo engcwele f? <b>4</b> Ngulowo g olezandla ezihlanzekileyo h lenhliziyo emhlophe i, ongaphakamiseli umphefumulo wakhe kokuyize longafungi ngenkohliso j. <b>5</b> Uzakwemukela isibusiso eNkosini, lokulunga kuNkulunkulu wosindiso lwakhe k. 
<b>6</b> Lesi yisizukulwana salabo abayidingayo, abadinga ubuso bakho, ngitsho Jakobe l. Sela*. <b>7</b> Phakamisani inhloko zenu, lina masango, phakanyiswani, lina minyango yaphakade m, ukuze kungene iNkosi yenkazimulo n. <b>8</b> Ingubani liNkosi yenkazimulo? INkosi elamandla leliqhawe, iNkosi elamandla empini. <b>9</b> Phakamisani inhloko zenu, lina masango, yebo, phakamani, lina minyango yaphakade, ukuze kungene iNkosi yenkazimulo. <b>10</b> Ingubani liNkosi yenkazimulo? INkosi yamabandla, yiyo eyiNkosi yenkazimulo. Sela*. 
<b>Psalms 25</b><br><b>1</b> Kuwe, Nkosi, ngiphakamisela umphefumulo wami a. <b>2</b> Nkulunkulu wami, ngithembela kuwe b; kangingayangeki c; izitha zami kazingathokozi phezu kwami d. <b>3</b> Yebo, kungayangeki loyedwa kulabo abakulindelayo e; kabayangeke labo f abaphambukayo kungelasizatho g. <b>4</b> Ngazisa izindlela zakho, Nkosi h, ungifundise imikhondo yakho i. <b>5</b> Ngikhokhelela eqinisweni lakho, ungifundise, ngoba unguNkulunkulu wosindiso lwami j; ngilindele wena usuku lonke. 
<b>6</b> Khumbula izihawu zakho, Nkosi, lobubele bakho, ngoba kwakukhona kusukela phakade k. <b>7</b> Ungazikhumbuli izono zobutsha bami leziphambeko zami l; ngokomusa wakho wena ungikhumbule, ngenxa yokulunga kwakho, Nkosi m. <b>8</b> INkosi ilungile iqondile n; ngakho-ke izazifundisa izoni ngendlela o. <b>9</b> Izabakhokhela abathobekileyo ekwahlulelweni p, ibafundise abathobekileyo indlela yayo q. <b>10</b> Zonke indlela zeNkosi zingumusa leqiniso kulabo abalondoloza isivumelwano sayo lezifakazelo zayo. 
<b>11</b> Ngenxa yebizo lakho, Nkosi, thethelela isiphambeko sami, ngoba sikhulu r. <b>12</b> Nguwuphi umuntu owesaba iNkosi? Izamfundisa ngendlela ezayikhetha s. <b>13</b> Umphefumulo wakhe uzahlala ebuhleni t, lenzalo yakhe u izalidla ilifa lelizwe v. <b>14</b> Imfihlo yeNkosi ingeyabayesabayo w, izabazisa isivumelwano sayo. <b>15</b> Amehlo ami ahlala eseNkosini x, ngoba yiyo ezakhupha inyawo zami embuleni y. <b>16</b> Phendukela kimi, ube lomusa kimi z, ngoba ngilesizungu, ngihluphekile aa. 
<b>17</b> Inkathazo zenhliziyo yami zanda, ungikhuphe ezinhluphekweni zami. <b>18</b> Khangela inhlupheko yami losizi lwami, uthethelele zonke izono zami ab. <b>19</b> Khangela izitha zami ngoba zinengi, ziyangizonda ngenzondo elesihluku. <b>20</b> Londoloza umphefumulo wami, ungikhulule; kangingayangeki ac, ngoba ngiphephela kuwe ad. <b>21</b> Ubuqotho lokuqonda kakungilondoloze, ngoba ngilindele wena. <b>22</b> Nkulunkulu, hlenga uIsrayeli kuzo zonke inkathazo zakhe ae. 
<b>Psalms 26</b><br><b>1</b> Ngahlulela, Nkosi a, ngoba mina ngihambe ngobuqotho bami b, njalo ngithembele eNkosini c, angiyikutshelela d. <b>2</b> Ngihlola, Nkosi, ungilinge e, uhlole f izinso zami lenhliziyo yami g. <b>3</b> Ngoba uthandolomusa wakho luphambi kwamehlo ami, njalo ngihambe eqinisweni lakho h. <b>4</b> Kangihlalanga i labantu benkohliso j, kangihambelani labazenzisayo. <b>5</b> Ngiyizondile inhlangano yabenzi bobubi k, kangihlali lababi l. <b>6</b> Ngizageza izandla zami ngokungelacala, ngizingelezele ilathi lakho, Nkosi m. 
<b>7</b> Ukuze ngizwakalise ngelizwi lokubonga n, lokuze ngilandise zonke izenzo zakho ezimangalisayo o. <b>8</b> Nkosi, ngiyayithanda inhlalo yendlu yakho p, lendawo yokuhlala yenkazimulo yakho q. <b>9</b> Ungabuthi umphefumulo wami kanye lezoni, kumbe impilo yami kanye labantu abomele igazi. <b>10</b> Ozandla zabo zilobubi, lesandla sabo sokunene sigcwele izivalamlomo r. <b>11</b> Kodwa mina ngizahamba ngobuqotho bami; ngihlenga, ube lomusa kimi s. <b>12</b> Unyawo lwami lumi endaweni elingeneyo t; ngizayidumisa iNkosi emabandleni u. 
<b>Psalms 27</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi iyikukhanya kwami a losindiso lwami b; ngizakwesaba bani c? INkosi ingamandla empilo yami; ngizakwethuka bani d? <b>2</b> Lapho ababi, izitha zami labamelana lami, besondela kimi ukudla inyama yami, bona bakhubeka bawa e. <b>3</b> Loba ibutho lingamisa inkamba limelene lami, inhliziyo yami kayiyikwesaba; loba impi ingangivukela, kulokhu ngizathemba f. 
<b>4</b> Into yinye engiyicela eNkosini, yiyo engizayidinga g, yikuthi ngihlale endlini yeNkosi insuku zonke zempilo yami h, ukuze ngibone ubuhle beNkosi, ngibuzisise ethempelini layo i. <b>5</b> Ngoba izangifihla edumbeni layo ngosuku lobubi j, ingifihle ekusithekeni kwethente layo k, ingiphakamisele edwaleni l. <b>6</b> Khathesi-ke ikhanda lami lizaphakanyiswa phezu kwezitha zami ezingihanqileyo m; ngakho ngizanikela iminikelo yokuthaba ethenteni layo; ngizahlabela, yebo ngihlabele indumiso eNkosini. 
<b>7</b> Zwana, Nkosi, ilizwi lami lapho ngikhala n, njalo ungihawukele, ungiphendule o. <b>8</b> Uthe: Dingani ubuso bami. Inhliziyo yami yathi kuwe: Ubuso bakho, Nkosi, ngizabudinga p. <b>9</b> Ungangifihleli ubuso bakho q; ungayixotshi inceku yakho ngokuthukuthela; ubulusizo lwami; ungangitshiyi, ungangideli, Nkulunkulu wosindiso lwami r. <b>10</b> Lapho umama lobaba bengitshiya, khona iNkosi izangemukela s. <b>11</b> Ngifundisa indlela yakho, Nkosi, ungikhokhele endleleni eqondileyo t, ngenxa yezitha zami. 
<b>12</b> Unganginikeli entandweni yezitha zami u, ngoba abafakazi bamanga bangivukele v, lovutha isihluku w. <b>13</b> Ngangizaphela ngaphandle kokuthi ngakholwa ukuthi ngizabona okuhle kweNkosi elizweni labaphilayo x. <b>14</b> Lindela iNkosi y, ube lamandla, lenhliziyo yakho iqine, yebo, lindela iNkosi z. 
<b>Psalms 28</b><br><b>1</b> Ngiyakhala kuwe, Nkosi; dwala lami a, ungangithuleli b, hlezi nxa ungithulela, ngifanane c labehlela emgodini d , e. <b>2</b> Zwana ilizwi lokuncenga kwami lapho ngikhala kuwe f, lapho ngiphakamisela izandla zami g endaweni yakho h yelizwi engcwele i. <b>3</b> Ungangihuduli kanye lababi njalo kanye labenzi bobubi j, abakhuluma ukuthula labomakhelwane babo, kodwa ububi busenhliziyweni yabo k. <b>4</b> Ubanike njengokwenza kwabo, lanjengobubi bezenzo zabo; ubanike njengokomsebenzi wezandla zabo l, ubabuyisele isenzo sabo m. 
<b>5</b> Ngoba kabayinanzi imisebenzi yeNkosi lokwenza kwezandla zayo, izababhidliza ingabakhi n. <b>6</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, ngoba ilizwile ilizwi lokuncenga kwami o. <b>7</b> INkosi ingamandla ami lesihlangu sami p; inhliziyo yami ithembela kuyo, ngiyasizwa; ngakho inhliziyo yami iyathokoza, ngiyidumise ngengoma yami q. <b>8</b> INkosi ingamandla abo, njalo iyinqaba yezinsindiso zogcotshiweyo wayo. <b>9</b> Sindisa abantu bakho r, ubusise ilifa lakho s, beluse, ubaphakamise kuze kube nininini. 
<b>Psalms 29</b><br><b>1</b> Inikeni iNkosi, lina madodana abalamandla, inikeni iNkosi udumo lamandla a. <b>2</b> Inikeni iNkosi b udumo lwebizo layo c, likhothame eNkosini ebuhleni bobungcwele d. <b>3</b> Ilizwi leNkosi liphezu kwamanzi; uNkulunkulu wenkazimulo uyaduma e; iNkosi iphezu kwamanzi amanengi f. <b>4</b> Ilizwi leNkosi lilamandla, ilizwi leNkosi lilobukhosi. <b>5</b> Ilizwi leNkosi lephula imisedari, yebo, iNkosi yephula imisedari yeLebhanoni g. <b>6</b> Yenza yona-ke iqolotshe njengethole h, iLebhanoni leSiriyoni i njengethole lenyathi j. 
<b>7</b> Ilizwi leNkosi lidabula amalangabi omlilo. <b>8</b> Ilizwi leNkosi lizamazamisa inkangala, iNkosi izamazamisa inkangala yeKadeshi k. <b>9</b> Ilizwi leNkosi lizalisa izimpala l, lihlubule amahlathi; lethempelini layo konke kuthi: Udumo. <b>10</b> UJehova uhlezi phezu kukazamcolo m, yebo, uJehova uhlezi eyiNkosi kuze kube nininini n. <b>11</b> INkosi izanika abantu bayo amandla; iNkosi ibusise abantu bayo ngokuthula o. 
<b>Psalms 30</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizakuphakamisa, Nkosi, ngoba ungenyusile a, njalo kawuthokozisanga izitha zami ngami b. <b>2</b> Nkosi Nkulunkulu wami, ngakhalela kuwe c, wangisilisa d. <b>3</b> Nkosi, uwukhuphulile umphefumulo wami e engcwabeni f, ungigcinile ngiphila, ukuze ngingehleli emgodini g. <b>4</b> Hlabelelani eNkosini, lina bangcwele bayo h, libonge ekukhunjulweni kobungcwele bayo. <b>5</b> Ngoba ukuthukuthela kwayo kungokwesikhatshana i, impilo isemuseni wayo j. Ukulila kungahlala kusihlwa k, kanti ekuseni kulentokozo l. 
<b>6</b> Mina-ke ngathi ekuphumeleleni kwami m: Kangiyikunyikinywa laphakade n. <b>7</b> Nkosi, ngomusa wakho wenzile intaba yami iqine; wabufihla ubuso bakho, ngahlupheka o. <b>8</b> Ngakhala kuwe, Nkosi; ngancenga eNkosini p. <b>9</b> Kulenzuzo bani egazini lami ekutshoneni kwami emgodini? Uthuli luzakudumisa na? Luzatshumayela iqiniso lakho yini q? <b>10</b> Zwana, Nkosi, ube lomusa kimi; Nkosi, woba ngumsizi wami r. <b>11</b> Uphendule ukukhala kwami kwaba yikusina s; ukhuphile isaka lami, wangibhincisa intokozo t, 
<b>12</b> ukuze udumo lwami luhlabele indumiso kuwe, lungathuli; Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami, ngizakubonga kuze kube nininini. 
<b>Psalms 31</b><br><b>1</b> Kuwe, Nkosi, ngiyathemba a, kangingayangeki lanini b, ngikhulule ngokulunga kwakho c. <b>2</b> Beka kimi indlebe yakho, ngikhulule masinyane, ube lidwala elilamandla kimi d, indlu yezinqaba yokungisindisa e. <b>3</b> Ngoba wena ulidwala lami lenqaba yami f; ngakho ngenxa yebizo lakho g ngikhokhela h ungiqondise i. <b>4</b> Ngikhupha j embuleni abangithiye ngalo ensitha k, ngoba uyinqaba yami l. <b>5</b> Esandleni sakho ngibeka umoya wami m; ungihlengile, Nkosi, Nkulunkulu weqiniso n. 
<b>6</b> Ngibazondile o labo abananzelela okuyize kwamanga p; kodwa mina ngithembela eNkosini q. <b>7</b> Ngizathaba ngithokoze emuseni wakho, ngoba ubonile uhlupho lwami, wazile umphefumulo wami ezinkathazweni r. <b>8</b> Njalo kawungivalelanga esandleni sesitha s, umise inyawo zami endaweni ebanzi t. <b>9</b> Woba lomusa kimi, Nkosi u, ngoba ngilohlupho v; ilihlo lami liqedwa lusizi, umphefumulo wami lesisu sami w. 
<b>10</b> Ngoba impilo yami iphela ngosizi, leminyaka yami ngokububula; amandla ami ayadeda ngenxa yeziphambeko zami x, lamathambo ami ayaqedwa y. <b>11</b> Sengilihlazo phakathi kwazo zonke izitha zami, kodwa ikakhulu kwabakhelene lami z, sengiyikwesabeka kwabangejwayeleyo aa; abangibonayo ngaphandle bayangibalekela ab. <b>12</b> Bakhohlwe ngami njengofileyo enhliziyweni; nginjengesitsha esifileyo ac. <b>13</b> Ngoba ngizwile ukunyunda kwabanengi ad, ukwesaba inhlangothi zonke ae , af, becebisana bemelene lami, beceba ukuthatha impilo yami. 
<b>14</b> Kodwa mina ngithemba kuwe, Nkosi, ngithi: UnguNkulunkulu wami ag. <b>15</b> Izikhathi zami zisesandleni sakho ah; ngikhulula esandleni sezitha zami ai lakwabangizingelayo aj. <b>16</b> Khanyisa ubuso bakho phezu kwenceku yakho; ungisindise ngothando lwakho ak. <b>17</b> Nkosi, kangingayangeki, ngoba ngikubizile al; kabayangeke ababi, bathule engcwabeni am. <b>18</b> Kazithuliswe indebe zamanga, ezikhuluma kalukhuni zimelene lolungileyo ngokuzigqaja langokweyisa an. 
<b>19</b> Kukhulu kangakanani ukulunga kwakho, okubekele labo abakwesabayo ao, okwenzela labo abathembela kuwe, phambi kwabantwana babantu ap! <b>20</b> Uzabafihla ekusithekeni kobukhona bakho basuke kumagobe omuntu aq, ubafihle ethenteni ar , as ekuphikisaneni kwenlimi at. <b>21</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, ngoba ingenzele uthando olumangalisayo emzini oyinqaba au. <b>22</b> Ngoba mina ngathi ekuphangiseni kwami av: Ngiqunyiwe aw ngasuswa phambi kwamehlo akho ax. Loba kunjalo uzwile ilizwi lokuncenga kwami ekukhaleni kwami kuwe ay. 
<b>23</b> Ithandeni iNkosi lonke bangcwele bayo az. INkosi ilondoloza abathembekileyo, iyamphindisela kakhulukazi owenze ngokuzigqaja. <b>24</b> Qinani, izaqinisa inhliziyo yenu ba, lina lonke elithembela eNkosini bb. 
<b>Psalms 32</b><br><b>1</b> Ubusisiwe lowo osiphambeko sakhe sithethelelwe, osono sakhe sisitshekelwe a. <b>2</b> Ubusisiwe umuntu iNkosi engambaleli ububi b, lokungekho inkohliso emoyeni wakhe c. <b>3</b> Lapho ngathula, amathambo ami aguga d ngenxa yokubhonga kwami usuku lonke e. <b>4</b> Ngoba emini lebusuku isandla sakho sasinzima phezu kwami f; ubumanzi bami baguqulelwa ekomeni kwehlobo. Sela* g. 
<b>5</b> Ngakwazisa isono sami, kangifihlanga isiphambeko sami h, ngathi: Ngizavuma ngeziphambeko zami eNkosini. Wena wasuthethelela icala lesono sami. Sela* i. <b>6</b> Ngenxa yalokhu ngulowo lalowo owesaba uNkulunkulu uzakhuleka kuwe j ngesikhathi ongatholwa ngaso k. Isibili, esikhukhuleni samanzi amakhulu kawayikufinyelela kuye. <b>7</b> Wena uyindawo yami yokucatsha l, uyangilondoloza ekuhluphekeni m, ungiphahle ngamahubo okukhululwa. Sela* n. <b>8</b> Ngizakulaya ngikufundise endleleni ozahamba ngayo, ngizakweluleka, ilihlo lami libe phezu kwakho. 
<b>9</b> Ungabi njengebhiza, njengembongolo engelakuqedisisa o, omlomo wayo onqandwa ngomkhala langetomu, hlezi kusondele kuwe p. <b>10</b> Zinengi izinhlupheko zomubi q, kodwa othembela eNkosini, umusa uzamphahla r. <b>11</b> Jabulani eNkosini, lithabe, balungileyo s; memezani ngokuthaba t, lonke baqotho enhliziyweni u. 
<b>Psalms 33</b><br><b>1</b> Thokozani eNkosini, balungileyo a. Ukudumisa kubafanele abaqotho b. <b>2</b> Dumisani iNkosi ngechacho c, lihlabelele kuyo ngogubhu d olulentambo ezilitshumi e. <b>3</b> Ihlabeleleni ingoma entsha f, litshaye kuhle ngenhlokomo g. <b>4</b> Ngoba ilizwi leNkosi liqotho, lomsebenzi wayo wonke ngowobuqotho h. <b>5</b> Ithanda ukulunga lesahlulelo i; umhlaba ugcwele umusa weNkosi j. <b>6</b> Ngelizwi leNkosi amazulu enziwa k, lalo lonke ibutho lawo l ngomoya womlomo wayo m. 
<b>7</b> Ibuthelela amanzi olwandle njengenqumbi n, ifake inziki eziphaleni o. <b>8</b> Umhlaba wonke kawuyesabe iNkosi, bonke abakhileyo bomhlaba kabathuthumele ngayo. <b>9</b> Ngoba yona yakhuluma, kwaba khona; yona yalaya, kwema p. <b>10</b> INkosi ichitha icebo lezizwe, iphuthisa imicabango yabantu q. <b>11</b> Icebo leNkosi limi kuze kube nininini, imicabango yenhliziyo yayo ezizukulwaneni ngezizukulwana r. <b>12</b> Sibusisiwe isizwe esiNkulunkulu waso yiNkosi s, abantu ebakhethileyo baba yilifa layo t. 
<b>13</b> INkosi iyakhangela phansi isemazulwini, ibone bonke abantwana babantu u. <b>14</b> Isendaweni emisiweyo yokuhlala kwayo v ikhangela bonke abakhileyo bomhlaba w. <b>15</b> Ibumba inhliziyo yabo bonke, inanzelele zonke izenzo zabo x , y. <b>16</b> Kayikho inkosi esindiswa yibunengi bebutho, iqhawe kalikhululwa ngamandla amakhulu z. <b>17</b> Ibhiza liyize ekunqobeni, njalo kalikhululi ngobukhulu bamandla alo aa. <b>18</b> Khangela, ilihlo leNkosi liphezu kwabayesabayo ab, kwalabo abathembela emuseni wayo ac, 
<b>19</b> ukukhulula umphefumulo wabo ekufeni ad, lokubaphilisa endlaleni ae. <b>20</b> Umphefumulo wethu af ulindela iNkosi ag; ilusizo lwethu lesihlangu sethu ah. <b>21</b> Ngoba inhliziyo yethu izathokoza kuyo ai, ngoba sithembele ebizweni layo elingcwele aj. <b>22</b> Umusa wakho, Nkosi, kawube phezu kwethu, njengokulindela kwethu kuwe ak. 
<b>Psalms 34</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizayibonga iNkosi ngesikhathi sonke; ukudunyiswa kwayo kuzahlala kusemlonyeni wami d. <b>2</b> Umphefumulo wami e uzazincoma eNkosini f; abathobekileyo bazakuzwa bathokoze g. <b>3</b> Ikhuliseni iNkosi kanye lami, asiliphakamise ibizo layo kanyekanye. <b>4</b> Ngayidinga iNkosi h, yangiphendula, yasingikhulula kukho konke ukwesaba kwami i. <b>5</b> Bakhangela kuyo, bakhazimula, lobuso babo kabubanga lenhloni. <b>6</b> Lo umyanga wakhala, leNkosi yezwa j, yasimsindisa kuzo zonke inkathazo zakhe k. 
<b>7</b> Ingilosi yeNkosi l imisa inkamba izingelezele labo abayesabayo, ibakhulule m. <b>8</b> Nambithanini libone n ukuthi iNkosi ilungile o. Ubusisiwe lowomuntu p othembela kuyo q. <b>9</b> Yesabeni iNkosi, bangcwele bayo, ngoba kakulakuswela kwabayesabayo. <b>10</b> Izilwane ezintsha ziyaswela zilambe r, kodwa labo abayidingayo iNkosi kabasweli lutho oluhle s. <b>11</b> Wozani, bantwana, lingilalele, ngizalifundisa ukwesaba iNkosi t. <b>12</b> Ngubani umuntu ofisa impilo u, othanda izinsuku ukuthi abone okuhle v? 
<b>13</b> Londa ulimi lwakho kokubi w, lendebe zakho ekukhulumeni inkohliso x. <b>14</b> Suka kokubi, wenze okuhle y, dinga ukuthula, kunxwanele z. <b>15</b> Amehlo eNkosi akwabalungileyo aa, lezindlebe zayo kukukhala kwabo ab. <b>16</b> Ubuso beNkosi bumelene labenza okubi ac, ukuquma ukukhunjulwa kwabo kusuke emhlabeni ad. <b>17</b> Bakhala ae, iNkosi yezwa af; iyabakhulula kuzo zonke izinhlupheko zabo ag. <b>18</b> INkosi iseduze labo ah abalenhliziyo ezephukileyo ai, iyabasindisa abalomoya odabukileyo. 
<b>19</b> Zinengi inhlupheko zolungileyo aj, kodwa iNkosi iyamkhulula kuzo zonke ak. <b>20</b> Iyawagcina wonke amathambo akhe, kakwaphulwa lelilodwa kuwo al. <b>21</b> Okubi kuzambulala okhohlakeleyo am; labazonda olungileyo bazalahlwa. <b>22</b> INkosi iyahlenga umphefumulo wenceku zayo; njalo bonke abathemba kuyo kabayikulahlwa an. 
<b>Psalms 35</b><br><b>1</b> Phikisana, Nkosi a, labaphikisana lami, ulwe labalwa lami b. <b>2</b> Xhakalaza ihawu lesihlangu esikhulu c, usukumele usizo lwami d. <b>3</b> Hwatsha lomkhonto, uvimbe indlela umelane labangizingelayo; tshono emphefumulweni wami ukuthi: Ngilusindiso lwakho. <b>4</b> Kabayangeke e , f , g babe lehlazo labo abadinga umphefumulo wami h. Kabahlehliselwe emuva i , j babe lenhloni labo abaceba ububi ngami k. <b>5</b> Kababe njengamakhoba phambi komoya l, lengilosi yeNkosi kayibadudule. 
<b>6</b> Indlela yabo kayibe mnyama itshelele, lengilosi yeNkosi ibaxotshe m. <b>7</b> Ngoba kungelasizatho bangifihlele imbule labo emgodini, kungelasizatho bawugebhele umphefumulo wami n. <b>8</b> Ukubhujiswa kakumjume o, lembule lakhe alifihlileyo kalimbambe yena, awele kulo, ekubhujisweni p , q. <b>9</b> Kodwa umphefumulo wami uzathokoza eNkosini, ujabule esindisweni lwayo r. <b>10</b> Wonke amathambo ami azakuthi s: Nkosi, ngubani onjengawe, okhulula umyanga kulowo olamandla kulaye t, yebo, umyanga loswelayo komphangayo u? 
<b>11</b> Abafakazi bamanga basukuma, bangibuza engingakwaziyo v. <b>12</b> Bangibuyisela okubi endaweni yokuhle, umphefumulo wami w ulahlekelwa. <b>13</b> Kanti mina, ekuguleni kwabo isembatho sami sasilisaka x, ngathobisa umphefumulo wami ngokuzila ukudla, lomkhuleko wami wabuyela esifubeni sami y. <b>14</b> Ngahamba ngokungathi ungumngane ngokungathi ungumfowethu; ngikhotheme z kwezimnyama njengolilela unina aa. 
<b>15</b> Kodwa ekukhubekeni kwami bathokoza, babuthana ndawonye, abalahliweyo babuthana ndawonye bemelene lami, njalo ngangingakwazi; badabula kabemanga ab. <b>16</b> Kanye lababi abaklolodayo edilini ac, bangigedlela amazinyo abo ad. <b>17</b> Nkosi, uzabukela kuze kube nini? Buyisa umphefumulo wami ekubhubhiseni kwabo, wona wodwa owami emabhongweni ezilwane ae. <b>18</b> Ngizakubonga ebandleni elikhulu, ngikudumise phakathi kwabantu abanengi af. 
<b>19</b> Bangajabuli ngami abayizitha zami ngamanga ag, kabangangificeli ah ilihlo labo ai abangizonda aj ngeze ak , al. <b>20</b> Ngoba kabakhulumi ukuthula, kodwa baceba amazwi enkohliso bemelene labachumileyo emhlabeni. <b>21</b> Kanti bangikhamisela kakhulu umlomo wabo am bathi: Aha, aha, ilihlo lethu likubonile an! <b>22</b> Nkosi, usukubonile ao; ungathuli ap. Nkosi, ungabi khatshana lami aq. <b>23</b> Zivuse ar, uvukele as ilungelo lami at, udaba lwami, Nkulunkulu wami leNkosi yami. 
<b>24</b> Ngahlulela, Nkosi Nkulunkulu wami, ngokulunga kwakho au; kabangathokozi ngami av. <b>25</b> Kabangatsho enhliziyweni yabo ukuthi: Hiya, yisifiso sethu aw! Bangatsho ukuthi: Simginyile ax. <b>26</b> Kabayangeke babe lenhloni ndawonye abajabula ngosizi lwami ay; bembathiswe ngenhloni lehlazo az labo abazikhukhumeza ngami ba. <b>27</b> Kabamemeze ngenjabulo bathabe abaloyisa ukulunga kwami bb, yebo kabatsho njalonjalo bcukuthi: Kayikhuliswe iNkosi bd ethokoza be ngokuhlala kuhle kwenceku yayo bf. 
<b>28</b> Lolimi lwami luzakhuluma ukulunga kwakho, usuku lonke indumiso yakho bg. 
<b>Psalms 36</b><br><b>1</b> Kulenkulumo yesiphambeko kokhohlakeleyo; ngaphakathi kwenhliziyo yami ngathi: Kakukho ukwesaba uNkulunkulu phambi kwamehlo akhe b. <b>2</b> Ngoba uyazikhohlisa emehlweni akhe c, ububi bakhe buze butholakale buzondeka d. <b>3</b> Amazwi omlomo wakhe ayibubi e lenkohliso f; uyekele inhlakanipho ukwenza okuhle g. <b>4</b> Uceba h okubi embhedeni wakhe i, azimise endleleni engalunganga j, kakwali okubi k. <b>5</b> Nkosi, umusa wakho usemazulwini, uthembeko lwakho luze lufike emayezini l. 
<b>6</b> Ukulunga kwakho kunjengezintaba zikaNkulunkulu, izahlulelo zakho ziyinziki enkulu m; uyalondoloza umuntu lenyamazana, Nkosi n. <b>7</b> Luligugu kangakanani uthandolomusa wakho, Nkulunkulu! Ngakho abantwana babantu baphephela emthunzini wempiko zakho o. <b>8</b> Bazasutha kakhulu ngamanono p endlu yakho q, ubanathise okomfula r wentokozo yakho s. <b>9</b> Ngoba ukuwe umthombo wempilo t; ekukhanyeni kwakho sizabona ukukhanya u. <b>10</b> Luqhubele phambili uthandolomusa wakho kulabo abakwaziyo v, lokulunga kwakho kubo abaqotho enhliziyweni w. 
<b>11</b> Unyawo lwabazigqajayo lungezi kimi, lesandla sababi singangixotshi. <b>12</b> Nampo abenzi x bobubi sebewile y, bawiselwe phansi, kabalakuvuka z. 
<b>Psalms 37</b><br><b>1</b> Ungazikhathazi ngababi a, ungabi lomhawu ngabenzi bobubi b. <b>2</b> Ngoba bazaqunywa masinyane c njengotshani, babune njengohlaza d. <b>3</b> Themba eNkosini e, wenze okuhle, uhlale elizweni, uzondle ngothembeko f. <b>4</b> Zithokozise eNkosini g, njalo izakunika iziloyiso zenhliziyo yakho h. <b>5</b> Nikela indlela yakho eNkosini i, uthembe kuyo; yona izakwenza j. <b>6</b> Izaveza ukulunga kwakho njengokukhanya k, lelungelo lakho njengemini enkulu l. 
<b>7</b> Thula eNkosini, uyilindele m; ungazikhathazi n ngophumelelayo ngendlela yakhe o, ngomuntu owenza amacebo amabi. <b>8</b> Yekela intukuthelo, utshiye ulaka p; ungazikhathazi langokwenza okubi q. <b>9</b> Ngoba ababi bazaqunywa r, kodwa labo abalindele iNkosi bona bazakudla ilifa lelizwe s. <b>10</b> Kuseyisikhatshana nje, omubi angabe esaba khona t; loba uhlola indawo yakhe uyabe engasekho u. <b>11</b> Kodwa abamnene v bazakudla ilifa lelizwe w, bazithokozise ngobunengi bokuthula x. 
<b>12</b> Omubi uyaceba emelene lolungileyo, amgedlele amazinyo akhe y. <b>13</b> INkosi izamhleka z, ngoba iyabona ukuthi usuku lwakhe luyeza aa. <b>14</b> Ababi sebehwatshe inkemba, bagobise idandili labo ab, ukumlahla phansi umyanga loswelayo, ukubulala abaqotho ngendlela ac. <b>15</b> Inkemba yabo izangena kweyabo inhliziyo, kwephulwe amadandili abo ad. <b>16</b> Okuncinyane alakho olungileyo ae kungcono kulenotho yababi abanengi af. <b>17</b> Ngoba ingalo zababi zizakwephulwa ag, kodwa iNkosi iyabasekela abalungileyo ah. 
<b>18</b> INkosi iyazazi izinsuku zabaqotho ai, lelifa labo lizakuba sephakadeni aj. <b>19</b> Kabayikuyangeka ngesikhathi esibi, langensuku zendlala bazasutha ak. <b>20</b> Kodwa ababi bazabhubha; lezitha zeNkosi zizanyamalala njengamafutha amawundlu, zinyamalale entuthwini al. <b>21</b> Omubi uyeboleka, angabuyiseli am, kodwa olungileyo uyahawukela an, aphe ao. <b>22</b> Ngoba ababusiswe yiyo ap bazakudla ilifa lelizwe aq, kodwa abaqalekiswe yiyo bazaqunywa ar. <b>23</b> Izinyathelo zomuntu olungileyo ziqondiswa yiNkosi, iyathokoza endleleni yakhe as. 
<b>24</b> Loba esiwa at, kayikulahlwa phansi au, ngoba iNkosi isekela isandla sakhe av. <b>25</b> Ngangimutsha, sengimdala, kanti kangibonanga olungileyo etshiyiwe, lenzalo yakhe aw idinga ukudla ax. <b>26</b> Usuku lonke ulomusa, uyeboleka, lenzalo yakhe iyisibusiso ay. <b>27</b> Suka ebubini, wenze okuhle az; uhlale kuze kube nininini ba. <b>28</b> Ngoba iNkosi ithanda isahlulelo bb, kayiyikubatshiya abangcwele bayo bc. Bazalondolozwa kuze kube nininini; kodwa inzalo yababi izaqunywa bd. 
<b>29</b> Abalungileyo bazakudla ilifa lelizwe be, bahlale kulo kuze kube nininini. bf <b>30</b> Umlomo wolungileyo ukhuluma inhlakanipho bg, lolimi lwakhe lukhuluma isahlulelo bh. <b>31</b> Umlayo kaNkulunkulu wakhe usenhliziyweni yakhe bi; izinyathelo zakhe kazitsheleli bj. <b>32</b> Omubi uyamqaphela olungileyo, udinga ukumbulala bk. <b>33</b> INkosi kayiyikumyekela esandleni sakhe, kayiyikumlahla lapho esahlulelwa bl. <b>34</b> Lindela eNkosini, ugcine indlela yayo bm, izakuphakamisa ukuthi udle ilifa lelizwe bn; uzabona lapho ababi bequnywa bo. 
<b>35</b> Ngambona omubi bp elamandla amakhulu bq, eziqhelisa njengesihlahla semvelo esiluhlaza br. <b>36</b> Kube kanti wedlula bs, khangela-ke, wayengasekho bt; yebo ngamdinga, kodwa katholakalanga. <b>37</b> Nanzelela opheleleyo, umbone oqotho, ngoba ukuphela kwalowomuntu kuyikuthula bu. <b>38</b> Kodwa abaphambukayo bazabhujiswa kanyekanye; ukuphela kwababi kuzaqunywa bv. <b>39</b> Kodwa usindiso lwabalungileyo luvela eNkosini bw, inqaba yabo esikhathini sokuhlupheka bx. 
<b>40</b> Njalo iNkosi izabasiza ibakhulule; izabakhulula kwababi by, ibasindise, ngoba baphephela kuyo bz. 
<b>Psalms 38</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, ungangikhuzi entukuthelweni yakho, ungangijezisi ekuvutheni kolaka lwakho a. <b>2</b> Ngoba imitshoko yakho ihlabe yatshona kimi b, lesandla sakho sehle nzima phezu kwami c. <b>3</b> Kakukho ukuphila enyameni yami ngenxa yentukuthelo yakho d; kakukho ukuthula emathanjeni ami ngenxa yesono sami e. <b>4</b> Ngoba iziphambeko zami zedlule phezu kwekhanda lami f, njengomthwalo onzima zinzima kakhulu kimi g. <b>5</b> Izilonda zami zilevumba zilobomvu ngenxa yobuthutha bami h. 
<b>6</b> Ngigungubele, ngagotshiselwa phansi kakhulu i, usuku lonke ngihamba j ngikwezimnyama k. <b>7</b> Ngoba ukhalo lwami lugcwele okutshileyo, njalo kakukho ukuphila enyameni yami l. <b>8</b> Ngibuthakathaka ngichobozekile kakhulukazi m, ngiyabhonga ngenxa yokububula kwenhliziyo yami n. <b>9</b> Nkosi, sonke isiloyiso sami siphambi kwakho, lokububula kwami kakufihlakalanga kuwe o. <b>10</b> Inhliziyo yami iyatshaya, amandla ami angitshiyile; lokukhanya kwamehlo ami lakho kakukho kimi p. 
<b>11</b> Abangithandayo labangane bami q bema ummango lenhlupheko yami r, labakwethu s bema khatshana lami t. <b>12</b> Labo abadinga impilo yami bayangithiya u, labo abadinga ukoniwa kwami bakhuluma v izincithakalo w, bezindla inkohliso usuku lonke x. <b>13</b> Kodwa mina nginjengesacuthe, kangizwa y, lanjengesimungulu, esingavuli umlomo waso z. <b>14</b> Yebo, nginjengomuntu ongezwayo aa, okungekho ukuphikisa emlonyeni wakhe ab. <b>15</b> Ngoba kuwe, Nkosi, ngiyathemba; wena uzaphendula, Nkosi Nkulunkulu wami. 
<b>16</b> Ngoba ngathi: Hlezi bathokoze ngami ac, lapho unyawo lwami lutshelela ad, bazikhukhumeze bemelene lami ae. <b>17</b> Ngoba sengiseduze lokuwa, lobuhlungu bami buphambi kwami njalonjalo af. <b>18</b> Ngoba ngizakwazisa ububi bami ag, ngizakhathazeka ngesono sami ah. <b>19</b> Kodwa izitha zami ziphilile zilamandla ai, labangizonda ngeze sebandile aj. <b>20</b> Abaphindisela okubi esikhundleni sokuhle ak bayizitha zami al, ngoba nginxwanela okulungileyo am. <b>21</b> Ungangitshiyi, Nkosi; Nkulunkulu wami, ungabi khatshana lami an. 
<b>22</b> Phangisa ungisize, Nkosi, nsindiso yami ao. 
<b>Psalms 39</b><br><b>1</b> Ngathi: Ngizaqaphelisa izindlela zami a, ukuze ngingoni ngolimi lwami b; ngizagcina umlomo wami ngetomu, omubi esesekhona phambi kwami c. <b>2</b> Ngaba yisimungulu ngokuthula d; ngathula kokuhle e; lokuhlupheka kwami kwavuswa. <b>3</b> Inhliziyo yami yatshisa phakathi kwami f, ngisacabanga umlilo wavutha; ngakhuluma ngolimi lwami g, <b>4</b> ngathi: Nkosi, ngazisa isiphetho sami lesilinganiso sensuku zami ukuthi siyini h, ukuze ngazi ukuthi ngiyedlula kangakanani i. 
<b>5</b> Khangela, wenze insuku zami zaba yibubanzi besandla j, lobudala bami k bunjengento engesilutho phambi kwakho l; isibili ngulowo lalowomuntu ekumeni kwakhe uyize nje. Sela* m. <b>6</b> Isibili, umuntu uhambahamba esesithunzini; isibili n, bayakhathazeka ngeze o; uyabuthelela, kodwa kazi ukuthi ngubani ozakubutha p. <b>7</b> Pho-ke, ngilindeleni, Nkosi? Ithemba lami likuwe q. <b>8</b> Ngikhulula kuzo zonke iziphambeko zami. Ungangenzi ihlazo lesithutha r. <b>9</b> Ngaba yisimungulu, kangivulanga umlomo wami s, ngoba wakwenza wena t. 
<b>10</b> Susa uswazi lwakho kimi, ngoba ngiyaqedwa yisidutshulo sesandla sakho u. <b>11</b> Lapho ulaya umuntu ngokukhuza ngenxa yesiphambeko v, wenza ubuhle bakhe buncibilike w njengenundu x; isibili ngulowo lalowomuntu uyize. Sela* y. <b>12</b> Zwana umkhuleko wami, Nkosi, ubeke indlebe ekukhaleni kwami z; ungathuleli izinyembezi zami aa, ngoba ngingowemzini kuwe ab, ohlala njengowezizwe ac, njengabo bonke obaba ad. <b>13</b> Ungangihwaqeli ae ukuze ngihlumelele af, ngingakasuki, ngingabe ngisaba khona ag. 
<b>Psalms 40</b><br><b>1</b> Ngalindela lokuyilindela iNkosi a; yangithobela, yezwa ukukhala kwami b. <b>2</b> Yangenyula emgodini ohlokomayo, exhaphozini c lodaka d, yabeka inyawo zami phezu kwedwala e, yaqinisa izinyathelo zami f. <b>3</b> Yafaka ingoma entsha emlonyeni wami, indumiso kuNkulunkulu wethu g. Abanengi bazabona h besabe i, bathembe eNkosini j. <b>4</b> Ubusisiwe umuntu oyenzayo iNkosi ibe lithemba lakhe k, ongaphendukeli kwabazigqajayo l, lalabo abaphendukela emangeni m. 
<b>5</b> Minengi wena oyenzileyo, Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami, imisebenzi yakho emangalisayo n lemicabango yakho kithi; ingelandiswe ngokulandelana kuwe o. Ngingayilandisa ngiyitsho, minengi kulokuthi ibalwe p. <b>6</b> Umhlatshelo lomnikelo kawukufisi; izindlebe zami uzivulile; umnikelo wokutshiswa lomnikelo wesono kawuwubizi q. <b>7</b> Ngasengisithi: Khangela, ngiyeza; emqulwini wogwalo r kulotshiwe ngami s. <b>8</b> Ngiyathokoza ukwenza intando yakho, Nkulunkulu wami t, lomlayo wakho uphakathi kwemibilini yami u. 
<b>9</b> Ngitshumayele ukulunga ebandleni elikhulu v; khangela, kangibambanga w indebe zami x, Nkosi, wena uyakwazi y. <b>10</b> Ukulunga kwakho kangikufihlanga phakathi kwenhliziyo yami; ngikhulumile ngobuqotho bakho langosindiso lwakho; kangifihlanga uthandolomusa wakho leqiniso lakho ebandleni elikhulu z. <b>11</b> Wena Nkosi, ungangigodleli isihawu sakho aa; uthandolomusa wakho leqiniso lakho kakungilondoloze njalonjalo ab. 
<b>12</b> Ngoba ububi obungebalwe bungihanqile ac, iziphambeko zami zingibambile, ukuze ngingabi lakho ukubona ad; zinengi okwedlula inwele zekhanda lami ae; ngakho inhliziyo yami ingitshiyile af. <b>13</b> Thokoza, Nkosi, ukungikhulula ag. Nkosi, phangisa ukungisiza ah. <b>14</b> Kabayangeke babe lenhloni ndawonye abadinga umphefumulo wami ukuwuchitha; kababuyiselwe emuva bayangiswe abangifisela okubi ai. <b>15</b> Kabachitheke kube ngumvuzo wehlazo labo aj abathi kimi: Aha, aha ak! 
<b>16</b> Kabathabe bajabule kuwe bonke abakudingayo; abathanda usindiso lwakho kabatsho njalonjalo al bathi: Kayikhuliswe iNkosi am! <b>17</b> Kodwa ngingumyanga ngiyaswela an; iNkosi iyangikhumbula ao; ungumsizi wami lomkhululi wami; Nkulunkulu wami, ungalibali. 
<b>Psalms 41</b><br><b>1</b> Ubusisiwe onanzelela obuthakathaka a. INkosi izamkhulula ngosuku lobubi b. <b>2</b> INkosi izamlondoloza, imgcine ephila; uzabusiswa emhlabeni; kawuyikumnikela entandweni yezitha zakhe. <b>3</b> INkosi izamsekela embhedeni wokugula kwakhe; wena uzaguqula lonke icansi lakhe emkhuhlaneni wakhe c. <b>4</b> Mina ngathi: Nkosi, woba lomusa kimi d, silisa umphefumulo wami, ngoba ngonile kuwe e. <b>5</b> Izitha zami zikhuluma okubi ngami zisithi: Uzakufa nini, lebizo lakhe lichitheke? 
<b>6</b> Lalapho efika ukungibona, ukhuluma inkohliso; inhliziyo yakhe izibuthela okubi, esephumile phandle akhulume f. <b>7</b> Bonke abangizondayo banyenyezelana bemelene lami, bangisongele ukungona. <b>8</b> Into embi inamathele kuye; njalo njengoba elele phansi kasayikuvuka g. <b>9</b> Yebo, indoda yobungane bami engiyithembayo h, ebikade isidla isinkwa sami, ingiphakamisele isithende imelene lami i. <b>10</b> Kodwa, wena Nkosi, woba lomusa kimi, ungilulamise ukuze ngibaphindisele j. 
<b>11</b> Ngalokhu ngiyazi ukuthi uyangithanda, ngoba isitha sami kasithabanga phezu kwami. <b>12</b> Kodwa mina, ungisekele ebuqothweni bami, wangimisa phambi kwakho kuze kube nininini k. <b>13</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, kusukela phakade kuze kube phakade l. Ameni, loAmeni m. 
<b>Psalms 42</b><br><b>1</b> Njengalokhu indluzele inxwanele izifula zamanzi, ngokunjalo umphefumulo wami unxwanela wena, Nkulunkulu a. <b>2</b> Umphefumulo wami womela uNkulunkulu b, uNkulunkulu ophilayo c. Ngizafika nini ngibonakale phambi kobuso bukaNkulunkulu d? <b>3</b> Inyembezi zami beziyikudla kwami emini lebusuku e, lapho besithi kimi usuku lonke: Ungaphi uNkulunkulu wakho f? 
<b>4</b> Ngikhumbula lezizinto ngathulula umphefumulo wami phakathi kwami g, ngoba ngadlula lexuku, ngaya labo endlini kaNkulunkulu ngelizwi lentokozo lendumiso, ixuku eligubha umkhosi h. <b>5</b> Udanelani mphefumulo wami i? Ukhathazekelani j phakathi kwami k? Themba kuNkulunkulu l, ngoba ngisezamdumisa, ngosizo lobuso bakhe m. <b>6</b> Nkulunkulu wami, umphefumulo wami udanile phakathi kwami; ngakho ngizakukhumbula ngiselizweni leJordani n lelamaHermoni, ngisentabeni iMizari o. 
<b>7</b> Inziki ibiza inziki p ekuhlokomeni kwezimpophoma zakho q; wonke r amagagasi akho lamaza akho adlule phezu kwami s. <b>8</b> Emini iNkosi izalaya uthandolomusa wayo t, lebusuku ingoma yayo izakuba lami; umkhuleko wami kuNkulunkulu wempilo yami u. <b>9</b> Ngizakuthi kuNkulunkulu, idwala lami: Ungikhohlweleni v? Ngihambelani ngikwezimnyama ngenxa yocindezelo lwesitha w? <b>10</b> Njengokuchoboza emathanjeni ami, izitha zami ziyangiklolodela, lapho zisithi kimi usuku lonke: Ungaphi uNkulunkulu wakho x? 
<b>11</b> Udaneleni mphefumulo wami? Njalo ukhathazekelani phakathi kwami? Themba kuNkulunkulu, ngoba ngisezamdumisa, usindiso lobuso bami, loNkulunkulu wami y. 
<b>Psalms 43</b><br><b>1</b> Ngahlulela, Nkulunkulu a, umele udaba lwami esizweni esingesabi uNkulunkulu b; ungikhulule emuntwini wenkohliso longalunganga. <b>2</b> Ngoba wena unguNkulunkulu wesiphephelo sami c; ungilahleleni d? Ngihambelani ngikwezimnyama ngenxa yocindezelo lwesitha e? <b>3</b> Thumela ukukhanya kwakho leqiniso lakho, kungikhokhele, kungise entabeni yakho engcwele f lemathabhanekeleni akho g. 
<b>4</b> Khona ngizakuya elathini likaNkulunkulu, kuNkulunkulu injabulo yentokozo yami; yebo ngizakudumisa ngechacho, Nkulunkulu, Nkulunkulu wami. <b>5</b> Udanelani mphefumulo wami? Njalo ukhathazekelani phakathi kwami? Themba kuNkulunkulu, ngoba ngisezamdumisa, ukusiliswa kobuso bami, loNkulunkulu wami h. 
<b>Psalms 44</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, sizwile ngendlebe zethu, obaba basitshelile a, umsebenzi owawenza ensukwini zabo, ensukwini zendulo b. <b>2</b> Wena ngesandla sakho wazixotsha izizwe elifeni c, wabahlanyela bona d; wazihlupha izizwe, kodwa wabasabalalisa bona e. <b>3</b> Ngoba kabadlanga ilifa lelizwe ngenkemba yabo, lengalo yabo kayibasindisanga f, kodwa isandla sakho sokunene, lengalo yakho, lokukhanya kobuso bakho g, ngoba wawubathanda h. <b>4</b> Wena uyiNkosi yami, Nkulunkulu i; laya ukukhululwa kukaJakobe j. 
<b>5</b> Ngawe sizadudula izitha zethu k; ngebizo lakho sizabanyathelela phansi abasivukelayo l. <b>6</b> Ngoba kangithembeli m kulo idandili lami n, lenkemba yami kayiyikungisindisa. <b>7</b> Kodwa usisindisile ezitheni zethu, wayangisa abasizondayo o. <b>8</b> KuNkulunkulu siyazincoma usuku lonke; lebizo lakho sizalidumisa kuze kube nininini. Sela* p. <b>9</b> Kodwa ulahlile wasiyangisa q, njalo kawuphumi lamabutho ethu r. <b>10</b> Usenze ukuthi sibuyele emuva sisuka esitheni; labasizondayo baziphangela s. 
<b>11</b> Usinikele njengezimvu zokudliwa t, wasichithachitha phakathi kwezizwe u. <b>12</b> Uthengisile abantu bakho ngokungelanotho v, awuzuzanga lutho ngentengo yabo w. <b>13</b> Usenze x saba lihlazo kwabakhelene lathi y , z, inhlekisa aa lendumazo kwabasiphahlileyo ab , ac. <b>14</b> Usenze saba yisiga phakathi kwezizwe ad, ukunikinwa kwekhanda phakathi kwabantu ae. <b>15</b> Usuku lonke ukudunyazwa kwami kuphambi kwami, lehlazo lobuso bami lingigubuzele af, <b>16</b> ngenxa yelizwi lomgconi lomhlambazi ag, ngenxa yesitha lomphindiseli ah. 
<b>17</b> Konke lokhu kusehlele, kube kanti kasikukhohlwanga ai, kasenzanga ngenkohliso esivumelwaneni sakho aj. <b>18</b> Inhliziyo yethu kayibuyelanga emuva ak, lezinyathelo zethu kaziphambukanga emkhondweni wakho al, <b>19</b> lanxa usichobozile am endaweni yamaganyana an, usembese ngethunzi lokufa ao. <b>20</b> Uba besilikhohliwe ibizo likaNkulunkulu wethu, loba selulela izandla zethu ap kunkulunkulu wezizweni aq, <b>21</b> uNkulunkulu ubengayikukuhlola lokhu yini? Ngoba uyayazi imfihlo yenhliziyo ar. 
<b>22</b> Yebo, ngenxa yakho siyabulawa usuku lonke, kuthiwa sinjengezimvu zokuhlatshwa as. <b>23</b> Phaphama, ulaleleni, Nkosi at? Vuka, ungalahli kokuphela au. <b>24</b> Ufihlelani ubuso bakho, ukhohlwe ukuhlupheka kwethu locindezelo lwethu av? <b>25</b> Ngoba umphefumulo wethu ukhothamele othulini, isisu sethu sinamathela emhlabathini aw. <b>26</b> Sukumela usizo lwethu, usihlenge ngenxa yomusa wakho. 
<b>Psalms 45</b><br><b>1</b> Inhliziyo yami iphuphuma ngendaba enhle; ngikhuluma ngezinto engizenzele inkosi; ulimi lwami lulusiba a lombhali ophangisayo b. <b>2</b> Umuhle kulabantwana babantu; umusa uthululwe endebeni zakho; ngenxa yalokho uNkulunkulu ukubusisile kuze kube phakade c. <b>3</b> Bhinca inkemba yakho d emlenzeni e, qhawe f, udumo lwakho lobukhosi bakho g. <b>4</b> Lebukhosini bakho gada uphumulele, ngenxa yeqiniso lobumnene bokulunga h; lesandla sakho sokunene sikufundise izinto ezesabekayo i. 
<b>5</b> Imitshoko yakho ibukhali enhliziyweni yezitha zenkosi; izizwe zizakuwa ngaphansi kwakho j. <b>6</b> Isihlalo sakho sobukhosi, Nkulunkulu, ngesaphakade laphakade; intonga yakho yobukhosi iyintonga yobuqotho k. <b>7</b> Uthanda ukulunga, njalo uzonda ububi l; ngenxa yalokho uNkulunkulu, uNkulunkulu wakho m, ukugcobile n ngamafutha entokozo ngaphezu kwabanakwenu o. <b>8</b> Izembatho zakho zonke ziqholiwe ngemure* p lenhlaba lekhasiya* q; ezigodlweni zempondo zendlovu abakwenze wathokoza ngazo. 
<b>9</b> Amadodakazi amakhosi aphakathi kwabesifazana bakho abadumileyo r; indlovukazi s imi esandleni sokunene sakho t yembethe igolide u leOfiri v. <b>10</b> Zwana, ndodakazi, unakane, ubeke indlebe yakho; ukhohlwe abantu bakini lendlu kayihlo w; <b>11</b> khona inkosi izalangatha ubuhle bakho; ngoba iyinkosi yakho x, njalo khothama kuyo y. <b>12</b> Lendodakazi yeTire ilesipho z, labanothileyo ebantwini, bazancenga ubuso bakho aa. <b>13</b> Indodakazi yenkosi iyakhazimula yonke ab ngaphakathi, isembatho sayo singesegolide elelukiweyo ac. 
<b>14</b> Izalethwa enkosini ad yembethe isembatho esifekethisiweyo ae; izintombi eziyilandelayo, abangane bayo, zizalethwa kuwe. <b>15</b> Zizalethwa ngokuthaba lenjabulo, zizangena esigodlweni senkosi. <b>16</b> Esikhundleni saboyihlo kuzakuba ngamadodana akho, uzawabeka abe yiziphathamandla emhlabeni wonke af. <b>17</b> Ibizo lakho ngizalenza likhunjulwe ezizukulwaneni zonke ag; ngenxa yalokho izizwe zizakudumisa kuze kube nini lanini. 
<b>Psalms 46</b><br><b>1</b> UNkulunkulu uyisiphephelo sethu lamandla ethu a, uyatholwa elusizo kakhulu b ekuhluphekeni c. <b>2</b> Ngenxa yalokhu kasiyikwesaba ekuguqukeni komhlaba, lanxa izintaba zidilikela phakathi kwenlwandle. <b>3</b> Amanzi alo kawahlokome, akhaphazele, izintaba zizamazame ngokukhukhumala kwalo. Sela* d. <b>4</b> Kulomfula e, izifula zawo zithokozisa umuzi kaNkulunkulu f, indawo engcwele yamathabhanekele oPhezukonke. <b>5</b> UNkulunkulu uphakathi kwawo g, kawuyikunyikinywa h; uNkulunkulu uzawusiza ngovivi lokusa. 
<b>6</b> Izizwe ziyaxokozela, imibuso izamazame i; wakhupha ilizwi lakhe j, umhlaba wancibilika k. <b>7</b> INkosi yamabandla ilathi, uNkulunkulu kaJakobe uyinqaba yethu. Sela* l. <b>8</b> Wozani libone imisebenzi yeNkosi, incithakalo eyenzileyo emhlabeni m. <b>9</b> Yenza izimpi ziphele kuze kube semkhawulweni womhlaba n; yephula idandili, iqumaqume umkhonto o, itshise izinqola emlilweni p. <b>10</b> Thulani, lazi ukuthi mina nginguNkulunkulu q, ngizaphakanyiswa phakathi kwezizwe, ngiphakanyiswe emhlabeni r. 
<b>11</b> INkosi yamabandla ilathi, uNkulunkulu kaJakobe uyinqaba yethu. Sela* s. 
<b>Psalms 47</b><br><b>1</b> Tshayani isandla, zizwe zonke a, hlokomani kuNkulunkulu ngelizwi lentokozo b. <b>2</b> Ngoba iNkosi, oPhezukonke, iyesabeka, yiNkosi enkulu phezu komhlaba wonke c. <b>3</b> Izakwehlisela abantu ngaphansi kwethu, lezizwe ngaphansi kwenyawo zethu d. <b>4</b> Izasikhethela ilifa lethu e, udumo lukaJakobe emthandayo. Sela* f. <b>5</b> UNkulunkulu wenyukile ngokuhlokoma, iNkosi ngokukhala kophondo g. <b>6</b> Hlabelani indumiso kuNkulunkulu, hlabelelani indumiso. Hlabelani indumiso eNkosini yethu, hlabelelani indumiso. 
<b>7</b> Ngoba uNkulunkulu uyiNkosi yomhlaba wonke h; hlabelelani indumiso ngokuqedisisa i. <b>8</b> UNkulunkulu uyabusa phezu kwezizwe; uNkulunkulu uhlezi esihlalweni sobukhosi sobungcwele bakhe j. <b>9</b> Iziphathamandla zabantu zibuthene ndawonye, abantu bakaNkulunkulu kaAbrahama k. Ngoba izihlangu zomhlaba zingezikaNkulunkulu; uphakeme kakhulu l. 
<b>Psalms 48</b><br><b>1</b> Yinkulu iNkosi, ingeyokudunyiswa kakhulu a emzini kaNkulunkulu wethu b, intaba yobungcwele bayo c. <b>2</b> Yinhle ngokuphakama d, yintokozo emhlabeni wonke intaba yeZiyoni e, enhlangothini zenyakatho f, umuzi weNkosi enkulu g. <b>3</b> Ezigodlweni zawo uNkulunkulu uyaziwa ukuthi uyinqaba h. <b>4</b> Ngoba, khangela, amakhosi abuthana, adlula kanyekanye i. <b>5</b> Wona awubona, ngokunjalo amangala j, atshaywa luvalo, aphaphatheka k. <b>6</b> Ukuthuthumela kwawabamba lapho l, umhelo m njengowesifazana obelethayo n. 
<b>7</b> Ngomoya wempumalanga o uphahlaza imikhumbi p yeTarshishi q. <b>8</b> Njengesikuzwileyo ngokunjalo sikubonile emzini weNkosi yamabandla, emzini kaNkulunkulu wethu r; uNkulunkulu uzawumisa kuze kube nininini. Sela* s. <b>9</b> Sizindle ngothandolomusa wakho, Nkulunkulu, phakathi kwethempeli lakho t. <b>10</b> Njengalo ibizo lakho, Nkulunkulu u, injalo indumiso yakho kuze kube semikhawulweni yomhlaba v; isandla sakho sokunene sigcwele ukulunga. <b>11</b> Kayithokoze intaba yeZiyoni w, amadodakazi akoJuda athabe, ngenxa yezahlulelo zakho x. 
<b>12</b> Ibhodeni iZiyoni, liyizingeleze, libale imiphotshongo yayo, <b>13</b> liqaphelise umthangala wayo y, lidabule lihlole izigodlo zayo z, ukuze lilandisele isizukulwana esilandelayo aa. <b>14</b> Ngoba lo uNkulunkulu unguNkulunkulu wethu kuze kube nini lanini; yena uzasikhokhela kuze kube sekufeni ab. 
<b>Psalms 49</b><br><b>1</b> Zwanini lokhu lonke bantu a. Bekani indlebe lonke bakhileyo bomhlaba b, <b>2</b> bonke abantukazana labaphakemeyo, abanothileyo labayanga, ndawonye c. <b>3</b> Umlomo wami uzakhuluma izinhlakanipho, lokuzindla kwenhliziyo yami kuzakuba yikuqedisisa d. <b>4</b> Ngizabeka indlebe yami kuso isaga e, ngizavula ilibho lami ngechacho f. <b>5</b> Ngizakwesabelani ensukwini zobubi g, nxa ububi babagwenxi bami buzangihanqa h. <b>6</b> Abathemba ingcebo yabo, abazincoma ngobunengi benotho yabo i, 
<b>7</b> umuntu kasoze ahlenge lokuhlenga umfowabo j, kasoze amnike uNkulunkulu k inhlawulo yakhe l, <b>8</b> (ngoba uhlengo lomphefumulo wabo luligugu, futhi luyaphela laphakade m) <b>9</b> ukuze ahlale ephila phakade, angaboni ukubola n. <b>10</b> Ngoba ubona ukuthi abahlakaniphileyo bayafa o, lokuthi isithutha p kanye lesiphukuphuku bayabhubha q, batshiyele inotho yabo abanye r. <b>11</b> Umcabango wabo wangaphakathi uyikuthi izindlu zabo zizakuba ngezaphakade, izindawo zabo zokuhlala zibe ngezezizukulwana s, babize amazwe ngamabizo abo t. 
<b>12</b> Kodwa umuntu elodumo kayikuma u, uyafanana lenyamazana ezibhubhayo v. <b>13</b> Le indlela yabo iyibuthutha babo; kube kanti ababalandelayo bathokoza ngomlomo wabo. Sela* w. <b>14</b> Njengezimvu bamiselwe ingcwaba, ukufa kuzabelusa; labaqotho bazababusa ekuseni x, isimo sabo ngesokuqedwa lingcwaba ukuze bangabi lendawo yokuhlala y. <b>15</b> Kodwa uNkulunkulu uzahlenga umphefumulo wami emandleni engcwaba z, ngoba uzangemukela. Sela* aa. <b>16</b> Ungesabi nxa umuntu enotha, nxa udumo lwendlu yakhe lusanda; 
<b>17</b> ngoba ekufeni kwakhe kayikuthatha lutho, udumo lwakhe kaluyikwehla lumlandele ab. <b>18</b> Lanxa empilweni yakhe wabusisa umphefumulo wakhe, njalo bakudumisa nxa uzenzela okuhle ac, <b>19</b> uzakuya esizukulwaneni saboyise ad, kabayikubona ukukhanya kuze kube phakade ae. <b>20</b> Umuntu olodumo, engaqedisisi, unjengezinyamazana ezibhubhayo af. 
<b>Psalms 50</b><br><b>1</b> USomandla, uNkulunkulu, iNkosi, ukhulumile a, ubiza umhlaba kusukela ekuphumeni kwelanga kuze kube sekutshoneni kwalo b. <b>2</b> EseZiyoni, ukuphelela kobuhle c, uNkulunkulu ukhanyile d. <b>3</b> UNkulunkulu wethu uzakuza, kayikuthula; umlilo phambi kwakhe uzaqothula, enhlangothini zonke zakhe kuzakuba lesiphepho esikhulu e. <b>4</b> Uzabiza amazulu ephezulu, lomhlaba, ukwahlulela abantu bakhe f. <b>5</b> Buthelani kimi abangcwele bami g, abenza isivumelwano lami ngomhlatshelo h. 
<b>6</b> Lamazulu azatshumayela ukulunga kwakhe i, ngoba uNkulunkulu ngokwakhe ungumahluleli. Sela* j. <b>7</b> Zwanini bantu bami, ngizakhuluma k; Israyeli, ngizafakaza ngimelene lawe l. NginguNkulunkulu, uNkulunkulu wakho m. <b>8</b> Kangikusoli ngenxa yemihlatshelo yakho n; leminikelo yakho yokutshiswa ihlala iphambi kwami o. <b>9</b> Kangiyikuthatha ijongosi elivela emzini wakho p, lezimpongo ezivela ezibayeni zakho q. <b>10</b> Ngoba ngeyami yonke inyamazana yehlathi, izinkomo ezisentabeni eziyizinkulungwane. 
<b>11</b> Ngiyazazi izinyoni zonke zezintaba, lezilo zommango zikimi r. <b>12</b> Uba bengilambile, bengingayikukutshela, ngoba umhlaba ngowami lokugcwala kwawo s. <b>13</b> Ngizayidla yini inyama yezinkunzi t, kumbe nginathe igazi lezimpongo? <b>14</b> Nikela kuNkulunkulu u umhlatshelo wokubonga v, ukhokhe izithembiso zakho koPhezukonke w. <b>15</b> Ungibize osukwini lokuhlupheka x, ngizakukhulula y, ubusungidumisa z. <b>16</b> Kodwa komubi uNkulunkulu uthi: Ulani lokuthi utshumayele izimiso zami, kumbe uthathe isivumelwano sami emlonyeni wakho aa? 
<b>17</b> Ngoba wena uzonda ukuqondiswa ab, ulahla amazwi ami emva kwakho ac. <b>18</b> Uba ubona isela, uthokozelana lalo, lesabelo sakho sisezifebeni ad. <b>19</b> Umlomo wakho uyawunikela ebubini, lolimi lwakho luqambe inkohliso ae. <b>20</b> Uhlala ukhulume umelene lomfowenu, uhlebe indodana kanyoko af. <b>21</b> Lezizinto uzenzile, ngathula ag; wacabanga ukuthi nginjengawe kanye ah. Ngizakukhuza ai, ngikuhlele phambi kwamehlo akho aj. <b>22</b> Kuqapheleni-ke lokhu, lina elikhohlwa uNkulunkulu ak, hlezi ngilidabule, kungabi khona okukhululayo al. 
<b>23</b> Onikela umnikelo wokubonga am uyangidumisa an; lolungisa indlela yakhe ngizambonisa usindiso lukaNkulunkulu. 
<b>Psalms 51</b><br><b>1</b> Ngihawukela, Nkulunkulu a, ngokothandolomusa wakho b; ngobunengi besihawu sakho yesula iziphambeko zami c. <b>2</b> Ungigezisise ebubini bami, ungihlambulule esonweni sami d. <b>3</b> Ngoba mina ngiyazivuma iziphambeko zami e, lesono sami sihlezi siphambi kwami f. <b>4</b> Ngonile kuwe, kuwe wedwa g, ngenze okubi emehlweni akho h, ukuze kuthiwe ulungile ekukhulumeni kwakho, uhlanzeke ekwahluleleni kwakho i. <b>5</b> Khangela, ngazalelwa ebubini, lomama wangomulela esonweni j. 
<b>6</b> Khangela, uthanda iqiniso endaweni ezingaphakathi; lekusithekeni ungenza ngazi inhlakanipho k. <b>7</b> Ngihlanze ngehisope*, ngizahlambuluka l; ngigezisa m, ngizakuba mhlophe kuleliqhwa elikhithikileyo n. <b>8</b> Ngizwise intokozo lenjabulo, ukuze athabe amathambo oowachobozileyo p. <b>9</b> Fihla ubuso bakho ezonweni zami q, wesule zonke iziphambeko zami r. <b>10</b> Nkulunkulu, dala kimi inhliziyo ehlambulukileyo s, njalo uvuselele t umoya oqinileyo phakathi kwami u. 
<b>11</b> Ungangilahli v ebusweni bakho w, njalo ungasusi umoya wakho ongcwele kimi x. <b>12</b> Buyisela kimi intokozo yosindiso lwakho, ungisekele ngomoya ovumayo y. <b>13</b> Khona ngizafundisa abaphambukayo indlela zakho, lezoni zizaphendukela kuwe z. <b>14</b> Ngikhulula ecaleni legazi, Nkulunkulu aa, Nkulunkulu wosindiso lwami ab, ulimi lwami luzahlabelela kakhulu ngokulunga kwakho ac. <b>15</b> Nkosi, vula indebe zami, lomlomo wami uzamemezela indumiso yakho.
