<b>16</b> Ngoba kawuthandi umhlatshelo, ngoba bengizawunika; kawuthokozi ngomnikelo wokutshiswa ad. <b>17</b> Imihlatshelo kaNkulunkulu ingumoya odabukileyo ae; inhliziyo edabukileyo af leyephukileyo ag, Nkulunkulu, kawuyikuyidelela. <b>18</b> Yenzele iZiyoni okuhle ngokuthanda kwakho ah; uyakhe imiduli yeJerusalema ai. <b>19</b> Khona uzajabulela aj iminikelo yokulunga ak, umnikelo wokutshiswa, lomnikelo wonke; khona bezanikela amajongosi phezu kwelathi lakho al. 
<b>Psalms 52</b><br><b>1</b> Uzincomelani ngokubi, qhawe? Umusa kaNkulunkulu ukulo lonke usuku a. <b>2</b> Ulimi lwakho b luceba konke ukuganga c, njengensingo ebukhali d, lwenza inkohliso e. <b>3</b> Uthanda okubi kulokuhle; amanga kulokukhuluma iqiniso. Sela*. <b>4</b> Uthanda wonke amazwi okuginya, limi lwenkohliso. <b>5</b> UNkulunkulu laye uzakubhidliza phakade, akususe, akuhluthune ethenteni f, akusiphune elizweni labaphilayo. Sela* g. <b>6</b> Abalungileyo bazakubona h besabe i, bazamhleka j, besithi: 
<b>7</b> Khangelani, umuntu ongamenzanga uNkulunkulu abe yinqaba yakhe, kodwa wathembela ebunengini benotho yakhe k, eqinile ekugangeni kwakhe l. <b>8</b> Kodwa nginjengesihlahla somhlwathi esiluhlaza endlini kaNkulunkulu. Ngiyathemba emuseni kaNkulunkulu phakade laphakade m. <b>9</b> Ngizakudumisa kuze kube phakade ngoba ukwenzile. Ngizalindela ibizo lakho, ngoba lihle n phambi kwabangcwele bakho o. 
<b>Psalms 53</b><br><b>1</b> Isithutha sithi enhliziyweni yaso: KakulaNkulunkulu a. Bonakele, benza ububi obunengekayo; kakho owenza okuhle b. <b>2</b> UNkulunkulu wakhangela phansi esemazulwini phezu kwabantwana babantu, ukubona ukuthi ukhona yini oqedisisayo, odinga uNkulunkulu c. <b>3</b> Bonke ngamunye wabo d babuyele emuva, bonke bonakele e; kakho owenza okuhle, kakho loyedwa. <b>4</b> Kabazi yini bonke abenzi bobubi, abadla abantu bami njengoba besidla isinkwa? Kababizi uNkulunkulu f. 
<b>5</b> Lapho besaba lokwesaba, kungekho okwesabekayo g. Ngoba uNkulunkulu h uchithachithe i , j amathambo akhe omisa inkamba emelane lawe k; ubayangisile, ngoba uNkulunkulu ubadelile. <b>6</b> Kungathi insindiso zikaIsrayeli zingavela eZiyoni! Lapho uNkulunkulu ebuyisa ukuthunjwa kwabantu bakhe, uJakobe uzathaba, uIsrayeli athokoze. 
<b>Psalms 54</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, ngisindisa ngebizo lakho, ungahlulele ngamandla akho. <b>2</b> Nkulunkulu, zwana umkhuleko wami, ubeke indlebe emazwini omlomo wami a. <b>3</b> Ngoba abemzini bangivukele, abalolunya badinga umphefumulo wami, kababekanga uNkulunkulu phambi kwabo. Sela* b. <b>4</b> Khangela, uNkulunkulu ungumsizi wami c; iNkosi ilalabo abasekela umphefumulo wami d. <b>5</b> Izaphindisela ububi ezitheni zami. Baqume e ngeqiniso lakho f. 
<b>6</b> Ngizanikela umhlatshelo kuwe ngokukhululeka g, ngizadumisa ibizo lakho, Nkosi, ngoba lihle h. <b>7</b> Ngoba ungikhulule ekuhluphekeni konke, lelihlo lami libonile isiloyiso sami ezitheni zami i. 
<b>Psalms 55</b><br><b>1</b> Beka indlebe a, Nkulunkulu, emkhulekweni wami b, ungazifihli ekunxuseni kwami c. <b>2</b> Lalela kimi d, ungiphendule e; ngiyazulazula ekukhalazeni kwami f, ngenza umsindo g, <b>3</b> ngenxa yelizwi lesitha, ngenxa yembandezelo yomubi; ngoba bangethwesa ububi, langentukuthelo bayangizonda h. <b>4</b> Inhliziyo yami iyafutha phakathi kwami, lezethuso zokufa ziwele phezu kwami i. <b>5</b> Uvalo j lokuthuthumela kungifikele k, lokwesaba okukhulu kungigubuzele l.

Sela* ag. Ngoba bengelazinguquko, ngakho kabamesabi uNkulunkulu ah. <b>20</b> Welule isandla sakhe kwabaxolelene laye, wasingcolisa isivumelwano sakhe ai.

<b>Psalms 65</b><br><b>1</b> Ukulinda ngokuthula kuyindumiso kuwe, Nkulunkulu, eZiyoni, lesifungo sizagcwaliswa kuwe a. <b>2</b> Wena ozwa umkhuleko b, yonke inyama izakuza kuwe c. <b>3</b> Izinto zobubi zingehlule d. <b>Psalms 66</b><br><b>1</b> Mhubeleni ngentokozo uNkulunkulu, mhlaba wonke a. <b>2</b> Hlabelani ubukhosi bebizo lakhe, lenze udumo lwakhe lube lenkazimulo b. <b>3</b> Tshonini kuNkulunkulu lithi: Yeka ukwesabeka kwezenzo zakho! <b>12</b> Wenze abantu bagade phezu kwekhanda lethu q; sadabula emlilweni lasemanzini r, kodwa wasikhuphela endaweni elokunengi. <b>13</b> Ngizangena endlini yakho ngileminikelo yokutshiswa s; ngizakhokha kuwe izifungo zami t, <b>14</b> indebe zami eziziphumisileyo, lomlomo wami ozikhulumileyo ngisekuhluphekeni u. <b>15</b> Ngizanikela kuwe iminikelo yokutshiswa yezinonileyo, kanye lempepha yezinqama; ngizanikela inkomo kanye lezimpongo. Sela*. <b>16</b> Wozani lizwe lina lonke elimesabayo uNkulunkulu, ngizalandisa akwenzele umphefumulo wami v. 
<b>17</b> Ngakhala kuye ngomlomo wami, waphakanyiswa ngaphansi kolimi lwami w. <b>18</b> Uba benginanze ububi enhliziyweni yami x, ibingayikuzwa iNkosi y. <b>19</b> Qotho uNkulunkulu uzwile z, walalela ilizwi lomkhuleko wami aa. <b>20</b> Kabongwe uNkulunkulu, ongabuyiselanga emuva umkhuleko wami, kumbe umusa wakhe kimi. 
<b>Psalms 67</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, woba lomusa kithi usibusise a, ukhanyise ubuso bakho phezu kwethu. Sela* b. <b>2</b> Ukuze indlela yakho yaziwe emhlabeni, usindiso lwakho ezizweni zonke c. <b>3</b> Kazikudumise izizwe, Nkulunkulu, kabakudumise abantu bonke d. <b>4</b> Izizwe kazijabule zihlabele ngentokozo, ngoba uzabahlulela abantu ngokuqonda, uhole izizwe emhlabeni. Sela* e. <b>5</b> Kazikudumise izizwe, Nkulunkulu, kabakudumise abantu bonke f. <b>6</b> Umhlaba uzathela izithelo zawo; uNkulunkulu, uNkulunkulu wethu uzasibusisa g. 
<b>7</b> UNkulunkulu uzasibusisa, layo yonke imikhawulo yomhlaba izamesaba h. 
<b>Psalms 68</b><br><b>1</b> UNkulunkulu kavuke a, zihlakazeke izitha zakhe b, labamzondayo babaleke phambi kwakhe. <b>2</b> Njengokuphetshethwa kwentuthu baphephethe c; njengokuncibilikiswa kwengcino phambi komlilo, ababi kabachithwe phambi kukaNkulunkulu d. <b>3</b> Kodwa abalungileyo kabajabule, bathabe phambi kukaNkulunkulu, njalo bathokoze ngentokozo e. <b>4</b> Hlabelelani kuNkulunkulu, hlabelelani indumiso ebizweni lakhe f, limphakamise lowo ogade esedlula emagcekeni enkangala g ngebizo lakhe uJehova, njalo thokozani phambi kwakhe h. 
<b>5</b> Unguyise wezintandane lomahluleli wabafelokazi, uNkulunkulu endaweni yakhe yokuhlala engcwele i. <b>6</b> UNkulunkulu uhlalisa j ababodwa emulini k, ukhupha ababotshiweyo ebasa empumelelweni l, kodwa abalenkani m bahlala elizweni elomileyo n. <b>7</b> Nkulunkulu, ekuphumeni kwakho phambi kwabantu bakho o, ekuhambeni kwakho p enkangala; Sela* q: <b>8</b> Umhlaba wazamazama, lawo amazulu athontisa phambi kukaNkulunkulu r, le iSinayi, phambi kukaNkulunkulu, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli s. 
<b>9</b> Walinisa izulu elikhulu, Nkulunkulu; waliqinisa wena ilifa lakho selikhathele t. <b>10</b> Ibandla lakho lahlala kulo u; wamlungisela ngobuhle bakho oswelayo, Nkulunkulu v. <b>11</b> INkosi yanika ilizwi; abesifazana ababika izindaba babelixuku elikhulu. <b>12</b> Amakhosi amabutho ayabaleka w, ayabaleka. Owesifazana ohlezi ekhaya x wayaba impango y. <b>13</b> Lanxa lilala phakathi kwezibaya zezimvu z , ▼▼kumbe izimbiza., lizakuba njengempiko zejuba ezisitshekelwe ngesiliva ab, lezinsiba zalo ngegolide eliliganu ac. 
<b>14</b> Lapho uSomandla echitha amakhosi kulo ad, kwaba mhlophe njengeliqhwa elikhithikileyo eZalimoni ae. <b>15</b> Intaba yeBashani yintaba kaNkulunkulu, intaba elezingqonga, intaba yeBashani. <b>16</b> Likhangelelani ngomhawu, lina zintaba zezingqonga af? Lintaba ag uNkulunkulu uyiloyisele ukuhlala kwakhe. Yebo, iNkosi izahlala kuyo kuze kube nininini ah. <b>17</b> Izinqola zikaNkulunkulu ai ziyizinkulungwane ezingamatshumi amabili, izinkulungwane zezinkulungwane aj, iNkosi iphakathi kwazo, iSinayi endaweni engcwele.

Sela*. <b>20</b> UNkulunkulu wethu unguNkulunkulu wezinsindiso; lakuJehova iNkosi aq kulokuphuma ekufeni ar. <b>21</b> Kodwa uNkulunkulu uzaphohloza ikhanda lezitha zakhe, ukhakhayi olulenwele lwalowo ohamba ezonweni zakhe as. 
<b>22</b> INkosi yathi: Ngizababuyisa bevela eBashani at, ngibabuyise bevela enzikini yolwandle au, <b>23</b> ukuze inyawo zakho uzigxamuze egazini lezitha av, inlimi zezinja zakho kulo aw. <b>24</b> Zibonile izindwendwe zakho, Nkulunkulu; izindwendwe zikaNkulunkulu wami, iNkosi yami, endaweni engcwele. <b>25</b> Ngaphambili kuhamba abahlabelayo ax, ngemuva abatshayi bamachacho ay, phakathi izintombi zitshaya izigubhu az. <b>26</b> Bongani uNkulunkulu emabandleni ba, iNkosi, kuvela emthonjeni kaIsrayeli bb. 
<b>27</b> Nango uBhenjamini omncinyane umbusi wabo bc, iziphathamandla zikaJuda, inkundla yazo, iziphathamandla zikaZebuluni, iziphathamandla zikaNafithali bd. <b>28</b> UNkulunkulu wakho ulaye amandla akho; qinisa, Nkulunkulu, lokho osenzele khona be. <b>29</b> Ngenxa yethempeli lakho eJerusalema amakhosi bf azakulethela isipho bg. <b>30</b> Sikhuze bh isilo somhlanga bi, umhlambi wezinkunzi kanye lamankonyana abantu bj, kuze kuthobeke kulenhlamvu zesiliva; ubahlakaze abantu abathokoza ezimpini bk.

Sela* bp. <b>33</b> Kuyo egade bq phezu kwamazulu amazulu awendulo br; khangelani, iphumisa ilizwi layo, ilizwi elilamandla bs. <b>34</b> Phanini amandla kuNkulunkulu; ubukhosi bakhe buphezu kukaIsrayeli bt, lamandla akhe bu asemayezini bv. 
<b>35</b> Uyesabeka, Nkulunkulu bw, ezindaweni zakho ezingcwele bx; uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli nguye onika abantu bakhe amandla lengalo. Kabongwe uNkulunkulu. 
<b>Psalms 69</b><br><b>1</b> Ngisindisa, Nkulunkulu, ngoba amanzi asengene a kuwo umphefumulo b. <b>2</b> Ngiyatshona odakeni olujulileyo, lapho okungelakuma khona c , d; ngingene emanzini atshonayo, lapho impophoma e iyangicwilisa f. <b>3</b> Ngidiniwe yikukhala kwami, umphimbo wami womile qha g; amehlo ami h ayafiphala ekulindeleni uNkulunkulu wami i. <b>4</b> Abangizondayo ngeze j banengi okwedlula inwele zekhanda lami k; abangangibhubhisa, izitha zami ngaphandle kwecala, balamandla. Lokho engingakwebanga ngasengikubuyisela l. 
<b>5</b> Nkulunkulu, wena uyabazi ubuthutha bami, lezono zami kazifihlakalanga kuwe. <b>6</b> Kabangayangeki ngenxa yami labo abakulindeleyo, Nkosi Jehova wamabandla; bangadunyazwa ngenxa yami labo abakudingayo, Nkulunkulu kaIsrayeli. <b>7</b> Ngoba ngenxa yakho ngithwele ihlazo; inhloni zisibekele ubuso bami m. <b>8</b> Sengaba ngowemzini n kubafowethu o, lowezizweni kubantwana bakamama. <b>9</b> Ngoba ukutshisekela indlu yakho kungidlile p, lenhlamba yabakuhlambazayo iwele phezu kwami q. 
<b>10</b> Ngasengilila ekuzileni ukudla komphefumulo wami, njalo kwaba yikudunyazwa kimi r. <b>11</b> Ngasengisenza isaka isembatho sami s , t, ngaba yisiga kibo u. <b>12</b> Abahlezi esangweni bakhuluma ngami, njalo ngaba yingoma yabanatha okunathwayo okulamandla v. <b>13</b> Kodwa mina umkhuleko wami ukuwe, Nkosi w, ngesikhathi somusa, Nkulunkulu, ngobunengi besihawu sakho, ngiphendule, ngeqiniso losindiso lwakho x. <b>14</b> Ungophule odakeni y, ngingatshoni z; ngikhululwe kwabangizondayo aa lenzikini yamanzi ab. 
<b>15</b> Impophoma yamanzi kayingangicwilisi ac, lenziki ingangiginyi ad, lomgodi ae ungavali umlomo wawo phezu kwami af. <b>16</b> Ngiphendula, Nkosi, ngoba uthandolomusa wakho lulungile ag; ngobunengi besihawu sakho ah uphendukele kimi ai. <b>17</b> Njalo ungayifihleli ubuso bakho inceku yakho aj; ngoba ngiyahlupheka ak, phangisa ungiphendule al. <b>18</b> Sondela emphefumulweni wami, uwuhlenge, ungikhulule ngenxa yezitha zami. <b>19</b> Wena uyakwazi ukuthukwa kwami am lehlazo lami lokudunyazwa kwami; izitha zami zonke ziphambi kwakho an. 
<b>20</b> Ukuthukwa kuyidabule inhliziyo yami, njalo ngibuthakathaka ao. Bengilindele ongangihawukela, kodwa wayengekho ap, labaduduzayo, kodwa kangibatholanga aq. <b>21</b> Basebengipha inyongo ibe yikudla kwami ar, lekomeni kwami as banginathisa iviniga. at <b>22</b> Itafula labo eliphambi kwabo kalibe ngumjibila au , av, ibe yisifu sabonwabileyo. <b>23</b> Amehlo abo kawafiphale ukuze bangaboni aw, njalo wenze inkalo zabo zihlale zithuthumele ax. <b>24</b> Thela intukuthelo yakho phezu kwabo, lolaka lwakho oluvuthayo lubafice. 
<b>25</b> Indawo yabo yokuhlala kayibe lunxiwa ay , az, kungabi khona ohlala emathenteni abo. <b>26</b> Ngoba omtshayileyo wena ba bayamzingela bb, njalo baxoxa kube buhlungu kwabalinyazwe nguwe bc. <b>27</b> Yengezelela ukona ekoneni kwabo, ukuze bangangeni ekulungeni kwakho bd. <b>28</b> Kabesulwe ogwalweni lwabaphilayo be, bangabalwa kanye labalungileyo bf. <b>29</b> Kodwa mina ngilusizi, ngiyafuthelwa; usindiso lwakho, Nkulunkulu, kalungimise phezulu ngivikelekile bg. <b>30</b> Ngizadumisa ibizo likaNkulunkulu ngehubo, ngimkhulise bh ngokubonga bi.

<b>Psalms 70</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, ngophula, Nkosi, phangisa ungisize a. <b>2</b> Kabayangeke babe lenhloni bonke abadinga umphefumulo wami; kababuyiselwe emuva bayangiswe b abaloyisa ukulimala kwami c. <b>3</b> Kababuyele emuva kube ngumvuzo wehlazo labo abathi: Aha, aha d! <b>4</b> Kabathabe bajabule kuwe bonke abakudingayo; labathanda usindiso lwakho kabatsho njalo bathi: Kabe mkhulu uNkulunkulu! <b>5</b> Kodwa ngingumyanga njalo ngiyaswela; Nkulunkulu, phangisa uze kimi. Wena ungumsizi wami lomkhululi wami; Nkosi, ungaphuzi e. 
<b>Psalms 71</b><br><b>1</b> Kuwe, Nkosi, ngiyaphephela; kangingayangeki lanininini a. <b>2</b> Ngikhulula ngokulunga kwakho, ungophule; beka indlebe yakho kimi, ungisindise. <b>3</b> Woba lidwala lokuhlala kimi, ngiye kulo njalonjalo b; ulayile ukungisindisa c, ngoba ulidwala lami lenqaba yami d. <b>4</b> Nkulunkulu wami, ngophula esandleni somubi lesandleni songalunganga lolesihluku e. <b>5</b> Ngoba wena, ulithemba lami Nkosi, Jehova, ithemba lami kwasebutsheni bami f. 
<b>6</b> Ngeyame kuwe g kwasesizalweni h; nguwe owangikhupha esiswini sikamama i; indumiso yami ingawe njalonjalo. <b>7</b> Benginjengesimangaliso kwabanengi; kodwa wena uyisiphephelo sami esiqinileyo j. <b>8</b> Umlomo wami kawugcwaliswe ngendumiso yakho, usuku lonke ngodumo lwakho k. <b>9</b> Ungangilahli esikhathini sokuluphala l, ungangitshiyi ekupheleni kwamandla ami m. <b>10</b> Ngoba izitha zami zikhuluma zimelene lami, labacathamela umphefumulo wami bayacebisana n, 
<b>11</b> besithi: UNkulunkulu umtshiyile; zingelani, limbambe, ngoba kakho okhululayo o. <b>12</b> Nkulunkulu, ungabi khatshana lami p; Nkulunkulu wami, phangisela usizo lwami q. <b>13</b> Kabayangeke baphele r abayizitha zomphefumulo wami s, kabasitshekelwe yikuyangeka lehlazo t labo abadinga ukoniwa kwami u. <b>14</b> Kodwa mina ngizathemba njalonjalo, ngengeze ekudunyisweni kwakho konke. <b>15</b> Umlomo wami uzalandisa ukulunga kwakho, usuku lonke usindiso lwakho v, ngoba kangiwazi amanani akho w. 
<b>16</b> Ngizahamba emandleni eNkosi uJehova, ngiqambe ukulunga kwakho, okwakho kuphela x. <b>17</b> Nkulunkulu, ungifundisile kusukela ebutsheni bami, njalo kuze kube khathesi ngitshumayele izimangaliso zakho. <b>18</b> Lalapho sengiluphele sengiyimpunga y, Nkulunkulu, ungangitshiyi, ngize ngitshumayele ingalo yakho kuso isizukulwana, amandla akho kuye wonke ozakuza z. <b>19</b> Lokulunga kwakho, Nkulunkulu, kufika ezingqongeni aa, owenze izinto ezinkulu ab; Nkulunkulu, ngubani onjengawe ac? 
<b>20</b> Ongenze ngabona inhlupheko ezinengi lezibuhlungu ad, uzangivuselela futhi, ungikhuphule futhi ezinzikini zomhlaba ae. <b>21</b> Uzakwandisa ubukhulu bami, ungiduduze inhlangothi zonke. <b>22</b> Lami ngizakudumisa ngogubhu lwezintambo af, iqiniso lakho, Nkulunkulu wami; ngizahlabelela izibongo kuwe ngechacho ag, wena ongcwele kaIsrayeli ah. <b>23</b> Indebe zami zizamemeza ngentokozo lapho ngihlabelela kuwe, lomphefumulo wami owuhlengileyo ai. 
<b>24</b> Futhi ulimi lwami luzakhuluma ngokulunga kwakho usuku lonke aj, ngoba bayangekile, ngoba badumazeka, abadinga ukoniwa kwami ak. 
<b>Psalms 72</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, nika inkosi izahlulelo zakho lokulunga kwakho a endodaneni yenkosi b. <b>2</b> Izakwehlulela abantu bakho ngokulunga, labayanga bakho ngokuqonda c. <b>3</b> Izintaba zizalethela d abantu ukuthula e, lamaqaqa f, ngokulunga g. <b>4</b> Izakwahlulela abahluphekayo babantu, isindise abantwana babaswelayo, ifohloze umcindezeli h. <b>5</b> Bazakwesaba wena lisekhona ilanga, isekhona lenyanga, kuzizukulwana ngezizukulwana i. <b>6</b> Izakwehla njengezulu j etshanini obugundiweyo k, njengezihlambo ezithambisa umhlaba l. 
<b>7</b> Ensukwini zayo olungileyo uzahluma m; lokwanda kokuthula n, ize ingabi khona inyanga o. <b>8</b> Izabusa-ke kusukela elwandle kusiya elwandle p, lakusukela emfuleni q kusiya emikhawulweni yomhlaba r. <b>9</b> Abahlala enkangala s bazakhothama phambi kwayo t; izitha zayo zizakhotha uthuli u. <b>10</b> Amakhosi v eTarshishi w lawezihlengeni x azaletha izipho y, amakhosi eShebha z leSeba aa asondeze izipho ab. <b>11</b> Amakhosi wonke azayikhothamela, izizwe zonke ziyikhonze ac. 
<b>12</b> Ngoba izamkhulula oswelayo ekhala ad, longumyanga, longelamsizi ae. <b>13</b> Izahawukela ongumyanga loswelayo, isindise imiphefumulo yabaswelayo. <b>14</b> Izahlenga umphefumulo wabo ecindezelweni ledlakeleni af; legazi labo lizakuba ligugu emehlweni ayo ag. <b>15</b> Izaphila, inikwe okwegolide leShebha ah, ikhulekelwe njalonjalo, usuku lonke ibusiswe ai. <b>16</b> Kuzakuba khona inala yamabele elizweni ezingqongeni zezintaba; izithelo zawo zihatshazele njengeLebhanoni; labomuzi aj , ak bazahluma al njengotshani bomhlaba am. 
<b>17</b> Ibizo layo lizakuba khona kuze kube phakade, lebizo layo lizakwanda lisekhona ilanga. Njalo bazabusiswa kuyo an; izizwe zonke zizakuthi ibusisiwe ao. <b>18</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi uNkulunkulu, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli ap , aq, onguye yedwa ar owenza imimangaliso as. <b>19</b> Kalibusiswe ibizo lakhe elilodumo kuze kube nininini at; umhlaba wonke ugcwaliswe ngodumo lwakhe au. Ameni, loAmeni av! <b>20</b> Imikhuleko aw kaDavida indodana kaJese isiphelile ax. 
<b>Psalms 73</b><br><b>1</b> Qotho uNkulunkulu ulungile kuIsrayeli, kulabo abahlambulukileyo enhliziyweni a. <b>2</b> Kodwa mina inyawo zami zaphose zasuka, izinyathelo zami zaphose zatshelela b. <b>3</b> Ngoba c ngaba lomhawu d ngabazikhukhumezayo e, ngibona ukuphumelela kwababi f. <b>4</b> Ngoba kakulazibopho ekufeni kwabo, kodwa amandla abo aqinile g. <b>5</b> Kabakho enhluphekweni njengabanye abantu, njalo kabahlutshwa njengabanye abantu h. <b>6</b> Ngakho-ke ukuzigqaja kuyabazungeza i njengeketane j, udlame luyabasibekela njengesembatho k. 
<b>7</b> Amehlo abo aqumbile ngamafutha; iminakano yenhliziyo iyaphuphuma l. <b>8</b> Bayakloloda, bakhulume ngobubi ngocindezelo; bakhuluma m besekuphakameni n. <b>9</b> Bamisa umlomo wabo umelene lamazulu, lolimi lwabo luhamba emhlabeni. <b>10</b> Ngakho abantu bakhe bayaphendukela lapha o, lamanzi enkezo p egcweleyo akhanyelwa bona q. <b>11</b> Njalo bathi: UNkulunkulu wazi njani? Kukhona ulwazi yini koPhezukonke r? <b>12</b> Khangela, laba ngabangelaNkulunkulu, kanti bayaphumelela elizweni, bandisa inotho s. 
<b>13</b> Isibili ngihlambulule inhliziyo yami ngeze t, ngageza izandla zami ngokungelacala u. <b>14</b> Ngoba ngihlutshiwe usuku lonke v, lesijeziso sami sisekuseni yonke w. <b>15</b> Uba bengingathi: Ngizakhuluma ngokunje x, khangela, ngabe ngisikhohlisile isizukulwana sabantwana bakho y. <b>16</b> Lapho nganakana ukwazi lokhu, kwakubuhlungu emehlweni ami z, <b>17</b> ngaze ngangena ezindlini ezingcwele zikaNkulunkulu aa; ngasengikuqedisisa ab ukuphela kwabo ac. <b>18</b> Isibili ubamisa ad endaweni ezitshelelayo ae, ubawisela ekubhujisweni af. 
<b>19</b> Yeka ukuchitheka kwabo njengokucwayiza kwelihlo ag! Baphela baqedwe ah ngokwesabisayo ai. <b>20</b> Njengephupho ekuphaphameni aj, Nkosi, nxa uvuka uzadelela umfanekiso wabo ak. <b>21</b> Ngakho inhliziyo yami yaba buhlungu, ngahlatshwa ezinsweni zami. <b>22</b> Njalo ngaba yisiphukuphuku esingaziyo al, ngaba njengenyamazana kuwe am. <b>23</b> Kodwa mina ngihlezi ngilawe; ubambe isandla sami sokunene. <b>24</b> Uzangikhokhela ngeseluleko sakho an, lemva kwalokho ungemukele enkazimulweni ao. 
<b>25</b> Ngilobani emazulwini ngaphandle kwakho? Njalo kakukho engikufisayo emhlabeni ngaphandle kwakho ap. <b>26</b> Inyama yami lenhliziyo yami kuyaphela aq; uNkulunkulu ulidwala lenhliziyo yami lesabelo sami ar kuze kube nininini as. <b>27</b> Ngoba khangela, abakhatshana lawe bazabhubha at; ubachithile bonke abaphingayo ngokusuka kuwe au. <b>28</b> Kodwa mina kungilungele ukusondela kuNkulunkulu; ngibeke isiphephelo sami eNkosini uJehova, ukutshumayela zonke izenzo zakho av. 
<b>Psalms 74</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, usilahleleni kokuphela? Ulaka lwakho luvuthelani ngakuzo izimvu zedlelo lakho? <b>2</b> Khumbula ibandla lakho owalithenga endulo, intonga yelifa lakho owayihlengayo, le intaba yeZiyoni ohlala kuyo. <b>3</b> Phakamisa inyawo zakho ezincithakalweni eziqhubekayo, konke okubi izitha ezikwenzileyo endaweni engcwele. <b>4</b> Izitha zakho zibhongile phakathi kwezindawo zakho zokubuthanela, zamisa impawu zazo zibe zimpawu. <b>5</b> Umuntu waziwa njengophakamisa amahloka eguswini lezihlahla. 
<b>6</b> Kodwa khathesi zitshaye okubaziweyo kwayo ndawonye ngamahloka lezando. <b>7</b> Zinikele indlu yakho engcwele emlilweni, zangcolisa indawo yokuhlala yebizo lakho ngokuyiwisela emhlabathini. <b>8</b> Zathi enhliziyweni yazo: Kasibachithe ndawonye. Zatshisa zonke indawo zokubuthanela zikaNkulunkulu elizweni. <b>9</b> Impawu zethu kasiziboni; kakusekho mprofethi; kakho kithi owaziyo ukuthi koze kube nini. <b>10</b> Koze kube nini, Nkulunkulu, umcindezeli edelela? Isitha sizahlambaza ibizo lakho kokuphela yini? 
<b>11</b> Usifinyezeleni isandla sakho, yebo isandla sakho sokunene? Sikhuphe phakathi kwesifuba sakho. <b>12</b> Kanti uNkulunkulu uyiNkosi yami kusukela endulo, eyenza insindiso phakathi komhlaba. <b>13</b> Nguwe owehlukanisa ulwandle ngamandla akho; wafohloza amakhanda emigobho emanzini. <b>14</b> Nguwe owachoboza amakhanda kaLeviyathani*, wamnikela waba yikudla kwabantu enkangala. <b>15</b> Nguwe owaqhekeza umthombo lesifula, nguwe owomisa imifula egeleza njalo. <b>16</b> Usuku ngolwakho, lobusuku ngobakho; nguwe owamisa ukukhanya lelanga. 
<b>17</b> Nguwe owamisa yonke imikhawulo yomhlaba; nguwe owenza ihlobo lobusika. <b>18</b> Khumbula lokhu, isitha siyangisile, Nkosi, labantu abayizithutha bahlambazile ibizo lakho. <b>19</b> Ungawunikeli umphefumulo wejuba lakho ezinyamazaneni zendle; ungakhohlwa ixuku lezihlupheki zakho phakade. <b>20</b> Nanzelela isivumelwano; ngoba indawo ezimnyama zomhlaba zigcwele indawo zokuhlala zesihluku. <b>21</b> Kangabuyeli eyangekile ocindezelweyo; ohluphekayo loswelayo kabalidumise ibizo lakho. 
<b>22</b> Vuka, Nkulunkulu! Ulumele udaba lwakho, ukhumbule ihlazo lakho ngoyisiwula usuku lonke. <b>23</b> Ungakhohlwa ilizwi lezitha zakho, ukuxokozela kwabakuvukelayo kwenyuka njalonjalo. 
<b>Psalms 75</b><br><b>1</b> Siyakubonga, Nkulunkulu, siyabonga, ukuthi ibizo lakho liseduze, iyalandisa imisebenzi yakho emangalisayo a. <b>2</b> Lapho ngisemukela isikhathi esimisiweyo b ngizakwahlulela ngokuqonda c. <b>3</b> Umhlaba labo bonke abakhileyo kuwo kuncibilikile d; mina ngiqinisa insika zawo. Sela* e. <b>4</b> Ngathi kwabayiziwula: Lingabi yiziwula f. Lakwababi: Lingaphakamisi uphondo g. <b>5</b> Lingaphakamiseli phezulu uphondo lwenu, lingakhulumi ngentamo h elukhuni i. 
<b>6</b> Ngoba ukuphakanyiswa kakuveli empumalanga kumbe entshonalanga njalo hatshi enkangala. <b>7</b> Kodwa uNkulunkulu ungumahluleli j, uyehlisa omunye, aphakamise omunye k. <b>8</b> Ngoba esandleni seNkosi l kulenkezo m; lewayini liphuphuma, ligcwele inhlanganiso; iyathulula okwalo n; kodwa izibhidi zalo, ababi bonke bomhlaba bazazihluza bazinathe o. <b>9</b> Kodwa mina ngizatshumayela njalonjalo, ngihlabele indumiso kuNkulunkulu kaJakobe. <b>10</b> Njalo zonke impondo zababi ngizaziquma p; impondo zabalungileyo zizaphakanyiswa q. 
<b>Psalms 76</b><br><b>1</b> UNkulunkulu uyaziwa koJuda; ibizo lakhe likhulu koIsrayeli a. <b>2</b> Njalo idumba b lakhe liseSalema c, lendawo yakhe yokuhlala eZiyoni. <b>3</b> Lapho wephula d imitshoko evuthayo yedandili e, isihlangu, lenkemba, lempi. Sela*. <b>4</b> Uyakhazimula, ulobukhosi okwedlula izintaba zempango f. <b>5</b> Baphangiwe abaqinileyo ngenhliziyo g, balele ubuthongo babo h; njalo kakho emaqhaweni othole izandla zawo. <b>6</b> Ekukhalimeni kwakho, Nkulunkulu kaJakobe, kokubili inqola lebhiza i kulele ubuthongo obukhulu j. 
<b>7</b> Wena, wena uyesabeka k. Ngubani-ke ongema phambi kwakho kusukela esikhathini solaka lwakho l? <b>8</b> Wenza isahlulelo sizwakale usemazulwini; umhlaba wesaba wathula m, <b>9</b> mhla uNkulunkulu evukela isahlulelo, ukusindisa bonke abathobekileyo bomhlaba. Sela* n. <b>10</b> Qotho, ulaka lomuntu luzakudumisa o; uzazibhinca ngensalela yolaka p. <b>11</b> Fungani, likhokhe eNkosini uNkulunkulu wenu q, lonke eliyihanqileyo r lilethe izipho kuye ofanele esatshwe s. <b>12</b> Izaquma umoya weziphathamandla; iyesabeka emakhosini omhlaba t. 
<b>Psalms 77</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi lami likuNkulunkulu, njalo ngiyakhala, ilizwi lami likuNkulunkulu, wasebeka indlebe kimi a. <b>2</b> Osukwini lokuhlupheka kwami ngayidinga iNkosi b; isandla sami selulwa ebusuku, kasiphezanga c, umphefumulo wami wala ukududuzwa d. <b>3</b> Ngamkhumbula uNkulunkulu, ngabubula e; ngakhalaza, lomoya wami f waphela amandla. Sela* g. <b>4</b> Ubambe amehlo ami ukuthi angalali; ngakhathazeka, kangisakhulumanga h. <b>5</b> Ngakhumbula i insuku zasendulo j, iminyaka yekadeni k. 
<b>6</b> Ngakhumbula ingoma yami l, ebusuku, ngakhuluma lenhliziyo yami, lomoya wami wahlolisisa m. <b>7</b> INkosi izalahla kokuphela yini n? Njalo kayiselakuphinda ibe lomusa yini o? <b>8</b> Uthandolomusa wayo seluphelile kokuphela yini? Isithembiso sesiphelile kusukela kusizukulwana kusiya kusizukulwana na p? <b>9</b> UNkulunkulu usekhohliwe ukuba lomusa yini q? Usevalele isihawu sakhe ngolaka yini? Sela* r. <b>10</b> Ngasengisithi: Lobu yibuthakathaka bami; iminyaka yesandla sokunene soPhezukonke s. 
<b>11</b> Ngizakhumbula t izenzo zeNkosi. Isibili, ngizakhumbula u izimangaliso zakho ezindala v. <b>12</b> Ngizazindla w ngomsebenzi wakho wonke x, ngikhulume y ngezenzo zakho z. <b>13</b> Nkulunkulu, indlela yakho isendaweni engcwele aa. Ngubani unkulunkulu omkhulu njengoNkulunkulu ab? <b>14</b> Wena unguNkulunkulu owenza ac , ad isimangaliso ae; wazisa amandla akho phakathi kwezizwe af. <b>15</b> Ngengalo yakho ag uhlengile abantu bakho ah , ai, amadodana kaJakobe loJosefa. Sela* aj. 
<b>16</b> Amanzi akubona, Nkulunkulu, amanzi akubona ak , al, ethuka am; izinziki lazo zathuthumela an. <b>17</b> Amayezi athulula amanzi ao, lezibhakabhaka zaduma ap; imitshoko yakho layo yaya le lale aq. <b>18</b> Ilizwi lokuduma kwakho lalisesivunguvungwini ar; imibane yakhanyisa umhlaba as; lomhlaba at wagedezela au wazamazama av. <b>19</b> Indlela yakho iselwandle aw, lomkhondo wakho emanzini amanengi ax, lezinyathelo zakho ay zazingaziwa az. <b>20</b> Wakhokhela abantu bakho ba njengomhlambi bb ngesandla sikaMozisi loAroni bc. 
<b>Psalms 78</b><br><b>1</b> Lalelani, bantu bami, umthetho wami, libeke indlebe yenu emazwini omlomo wami a. <b>2</b> Ngizavula umlomo wami b ngomfanekiso c, ngikhuphe d izimfihlakalo zendulo e, <b>3</b> esizizwileyo lesizaziyo, obaba abasitshela zona f. <b>4</b> Kasiyikuzifihlela abantwana babo g, kusizukulwana esilandelayo, silandisa izindumiso zeNkosi, lamandla ayo h, lezimangaliso ezenzileyo i. <b>5</b> Ngoba wamisa ubufakazi j koJakobe k, wabeka umlayo koIsrayeli, awulaya obaba l, ukuze bawazise abantwana babo m, 
<b>6</b> ukuze isizukulwana esilandelayo sazi n, abantwana abazazalwa o, basukume batshele abantwana babo p, <b>7</b> ukuze babeke ithemba labo kuNkulunkulu, bangayikhohlwa imisebenzi kaNkulunkulu q, kodwa bagcine imithetho yakhe r; <b>8</b> njalo bangabi njengaboyise s, isizukulwana esilenkani t , u lesivukelayo v , w, isizukulwana esingaqondisanga inhliziyo yaso, lesimoya waso ungathembekanga kuNkulunkulu x. <b>9</b> Abantwana bakoEfrayimi, behlomile y betshoka ngamadandili z , aa, babuyela emuva mhla wempi ab. 
<b>10</b> Kabasilondolozanga isivumelwano sikaNkulunkulu, bala ukuhamba emlayweni wakhe. <b>11</b> Bakhohlwa izenzo zakhe ac lezimangaliso zakhe ayebatshengisa zona ad. <b>12</b> Phambi kwaboyise wenza isimangaliso ae elizweni leGibhithe, egangeni leZowani af. <b>13</b> Waqhekeza ulwandle, wabachaphisa ag, wenza amanzi ema njengenqumbi ah. <b>14</b> Wabakhokhela ngeyezi emini, lebusuku bonke ngokukhanya komlilo ai. <b>15</b> Waqhekeza amadwala enkangala, wabanathisa kungathi kuvela ezinzikini ezinkulu aj. 
<b>16</b> Waseveza izifula ak edwaleni al, wenza amanzi ehla njengemifula. <b>17</b> Kodwa baphinda futhi ukona kuye, ngokumvukela oPhezukonke am enkangala an. <b>18</b> Bamlinga uNkulunkulu enhliziyweni yabo ngokucela ukudla kwenkanuko yabo ao. <b>19</b> Basebekhuluma bemelene loNkulunkulu bathi: UNkulunkulu angalungisa itafula enkangala yini ap? <b>20</b> Khangela, watshaya idwala aq, kwampompoza amanzi, lezifula zagabha ar; anganika lesinkwa yini? angalungisela abantu bakhe inyama na as? 
<b>21</b> Ngakho iNkosi yezwa yathukuthela at; umlilo wasubaselwa au uJakobe av, njalo lolaka lwavukela aw uIsrayeli ax, <b>22</b> ngoba kabakholwanga kuNkulunkulu, kabathembanga esindisweni lwakhe ay. <b>23</b> Lanxa walaya amayezi ngaphezulu, wavula iminyango yamazulu az, <b>24</b> wabanisela imana ukuthi badle ba, wabapha amabele amazulu bb. <b>25</b> Umuntu wadla isinkwa sabalamandla bc; wabathumela ukudla ukuze basuthe bd. <b>26</b> Wenza umoya wempumalanga wavunguza emazulwini; langamandla akhe waqhuba umoya weningizimu be. 
<b>27</b> Wasenisela phezu kwabo inyama njengothuli bf, lenyoni ezilempiko njengetshebetshebe lolwandle bg; <b>28</b> wazenza zawela phakathi kwenkamba yabo, zagombolozela indawo zabo zokuhlala bh. <b>29</b> Ngakho badla basutha kakhulu bi, ngoba wabalethela isiloyiso sabo bj. <b>30</b> Kabehlukananga lenkanuko yabo bk; ukudla kusesemlonyeni wabo bl, <b>31</b> ulaka lukaNkulunkulu lwaselubavukela bm, wabulala kwabakhulupheleyo babo, watshaya abakhethiweyo bakoIsrayeli bn. 
<b>32</b> Kulokhu konke bajinga besona bo, njalo kabakholwanga ngenxa yezimangaliso zakhe bp. <b>33</b> Ngakho waziqeda izinsuku zabo bq ngeze br, leminyaka yabo ovalweni. <b>34</b> Lapho ebabulala, bamdinga bs, baphenduka bt, bamdingisisa uNkulunkulu bu , bv. <b>35</b> Bakhumbula ukuthi uNkulunkulu ulidwala labo bw, loNkulunkulu oPhezukonke ungumhlengi wabo bx. <b>36</b> Kodwa bamyenga ngomlomo wabo by, baqamba amanga kuye ngolimi lwabo bz. <b>37</b> Ngoba inhliziyo yabo ca yayingaqondanga kuye cb, njalo bengathembekanga esivumelwaneni sakhe cc. 
<b>38</b> Kodwa yena elesihawu cd wathethelela ububi babo, kababhubhisanga ce; yebo, kanengi walunqanda ulaka lwakhe, kakuvusanga konke ukuthukuthela kwakhe. <b>39</b> Ngoba wakhumbula cf ukuthi bayinyama cg, umoya odlulayo, ongabuyiyo ch , ci. <b>40</b> Kanengi kangakanani bemvukela ehlane, bemdabukisa cj enkangala ck. <b>41</b> Yebo baphenduka bamlinga uNkulunkulu cl, bamdabukisa cm oNgcwele kaIsrayeli cn. <b>42</b> Kabasikhumbulanga isandla sakhe, usuku abahlenga ngalo esitheni co. 
<b>43</b> Ukuthi wamisa njani cp izibonakaliso zakhe eGibhithe cq, lezimangaliso zakhe cr , cs emmangweni weZowani ct, <b>44</b> lokuthi waphendula imifula yabo yaba ligazi cu, lezifula zabo, ukuze banganathi cv. <b>45</b> Wathumela phakathi kwabo umtshitshi wezibawu, ezabadlayo cw, lamaxoxo ababhubhisayo cx. <b>46</b> Wasenika imihogoyi isivuno sabo cy, lomsebenzi wabo isikhonyane cz. <b>47</b> Wabulala ivini labo ngesiqhotho da, lemikhiwa yabo yesikhamore ngongqwaqwane db. <b>48</b> Wasenikela izifuyo zabo esiqhothweni, lemihlambi yabo kuyo imibane. 
<b>49</b> Waphosela phezu kwabo ukuvutha kolaka lwakhe, ukuthukuthela, lokucunuka, lokuhlupheka, ngokuthuma izithunywa zobubi dc. <b>50</b> Walungisela ulaka lwakhe indlela, kanqandanga umphefumulo wabo ekufeni, kodwa wanikela impilo yabo kumatshayabhuqe wesifo dd. <b>51</b> Wasetshaya wonke amazibulo eGibhithe de, okokuqala kwamandla emathenteni df kaHamu dg. <b>52</b> Wasebakhupha abantu bakhe dh njengezimvu di, wabakhokhela enkangala njengomhlambi dj. 
<b>53</b> Wasebakhokhela bephephile ukuze bangesabi dk , dl; kodwa ulwandle lwasibekela izitha zabo dm. <b>54</b> Wasebafikisa emkhawulweni wendawo yakhe engcwele dn, le intaba, isandla sakhe sokunene esayizuzayo do , dp. <b>55</b> Wasebaxotsha abezizwe phambi kwabo dq; wababela ilifa dr ngomzila ds, wahlalisa izizwe zakoIsrayeli emathenteni azo. <b>56</b> Kanti dt bamlinga du, bamvukela uNkulunkulu oPhezukonke dv, kabazigcinanga izifakazelo zakhe. 
<b>57</b> Kodwa babuyela emuva, benza ngokungathembeki njengaboyise dw, baphanjulwa njengedandili elikhohlisayo dx. <b>58</b> Ngoba bamthukuthelisa dy ngezindawo zabo eziphakemeyo dz, bavusa umona wakhe ea ngezithombe zabo ezibaziweyo eb. <b>59</b> UNkulunkulu esizwa wathukuthela ec, wamenyanya kakhulu uIsrayeli ed. <b>60</b> Ngakho walitshiya ee ithabhanekele ef leShilo eg, ithente eh alimisa phakathi kwabantu ei. <b>61</b> Wasenikela amandla akhe ej ekuthunjweni ek, lenkazimulo yakhe esandleni sesitha. 
<b>62</b> Wasenikela abantu bakhe el enkembeni em, waselithukuthelela ilifa lakhe en. <b>63</b> Umlilo waqeda amajaha abo, lezintombi zabo kazihlatshelelwanga. <b>64</b> Abapristi babo bawa ngenkemba eo, labafelokazi babo ep kabalilanga eq. <b>65</b> Khona iNkosi yavuka njengobelele er, njengeqhawe eliklabalala ngenxa yewayini es. <b>66</b> Yazitshaya izitha zayo emuva, yazithela ihlazo phakade et. <b>67</b> Yasilahla ithente eu likaJosefa, kayikhethanga isizwe sakoEfrayimi ev. <b>68</b> Kodwa yakhetha isizwe sakoJuda, intaba yeZiyoni eyithandayo ew. 
<b>69</b> Yayakha indlu yayo engcwele njengezingqonga, njengomhlaba ewusekeleyo kuze kube nininini. <b>70</b> Yasikhetha uDavida inceku yayo ex, yamthatha ezibayeni zezimvu ey; <b>71</b> ekulandeleni izimvukazi ezenyisayo ez, yamletha ukwelusa fa uJakobe abantu bayo fb, loIsrayeli fc ilifa layo fd. <b>72</b> Njalo wabelusa njengobuqotho benhliziyo yakhe fe, wabakhokhela ngobugabazi bezandla zakhe ff. 
<b>Psalms 79</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu, abezizwe a sebengenile elifeni lakho b; balingcolisile ithempeli lakho elingcwele c; iJerusalema bayenze yaba zinqumbi d. <b>2</b> Banikile izidumbu zezinceku zakho zaba yikudla kwenyoni zamazulu e, inyama yabangcwele bakho f kuzo izilo zomhlaba g. <b>3</b> Balichithile igazi labo njengamanzi h inhlangothi zonke zeJerusalema; njalo kakubanga khona ongcwabayo i. <b>4</b> Sesibe yinhlamba kubomakhelwane bethu j , k, inhlekisa loklolodelo l kwabasiphahlileyo m. 
<b>5</b> Koze kube nini, Nkosi n , o? Uzathukuthela phakade yini p? Ubukhwele bakho q buzavutha njengomlilo yini r? <b>6</b> Thulula ulaka lwakho phezu kwabezizwe s , t abangakwaziyo u, laphezu kwemibuso engabizi ibizo lakho v. <b>7</b> Ngoba bamdlile uJakobe, lomuzi wakhe sebewenze unxiwa w. <b>8</b> Ungakhumbuli umelene lathi izono zethu zendulo x; isihawu sakho kasize masinyane phambi kwethu y, ngoba sehliselwe phansi kakhulu z. 
<b>9</b> Sisize, Nkulunkulu wosindiso lwethu, ngenxa yodumo lwebizo lakho aa; sikhulule, uhlawulele izono zethu ab, ngenxa yebizo lakho ac. <b>10</b> Kungani abezizwe bezakuthi: Ungaphi uNkulunkulu wabo? Kawaziwe phakathi kwezizwe phambi kwamehlo ethu ad, impindiselo yegazi lenceku zakho elachithwayo. <b>11</b> Ukububula kwezibotshwa kakuze phambi kwakho ae; njengobukhulu bengalo yakho ulondoloze abantwana bokufa af. <b>12</b> Uphindisele omakhelwane bethu ag kasikhombisa ah esifubeni sabo ai ukuthuka kwabo, abakuthuka ngakho, Nkosi aj. 
<b>13</b> Khona thina abantu bakho ak, izimvu zedlelo lakho, sizakubonga kuze kube nininini al, silandise udumo lwakho esizukulwaneni ngesizukulwana am. 
<b>Psalms 80</b><br><b>1</b> Melusi kaIsrayeli, beka indlebe, okhokhela uJosefa a njengomhlambi b, ohlezi phakathi kwamakherubhi* c, khanyisa d. <b>2</b> Phambi kukaEfrayimi loBhenjamini loManase e vusa amandla akho, uze ube lusindiso lwethu f. <b>3</b> Siphendule, Nkulunkulu g, wenze ubuso bakho bukhanye, ngakho sizasindiswa h. <b>4</b> Nkosi Nkulunkulu wamabandla i, koze kube nini uthukuthelela umkhuleko wabantu bakho j? <b>5</b> Ubenze badla isinkwa sezinyembezi k, wabanathisa izinyembezi ngesilinganiso l. 
<b>6</b> Usenza sibe yingxabano kubomakhelwane bethu, lezitha zethu ziyahlekisana ngathi m. <b>7</b> Siphendule, Nkulunkulu wamabandla, wenze ubuso bakho bukhanye, ngakho sizasindiswa n. <b>8</b> Waliletha ivini livela eGibhithe o, wazixotsha izizwe p, walihlanyela lona q. <b>9</b> Walungisa indawo phambi kwalo r, wagxilisa impande zalo, laze lagcwala umhlaba. <b>10</b> Izintaba zembeswa ngomthunzi walo, lezingatsha zalo zinjengemisedari kaNkulunkulu. <b>11</b> Lanabisela ingatsha zalo elwandle s, lamahlumela alo t emfuleni u. 
<b>12</b> Uyibhobozeleni imiduli yalo v, ukuze balikhe bonke abadlula ngendlela w? <b>13</b> Ingulube yasehlathini iyalihlikiza x, lenyamazana yeganga iyalidla y. <b>14</b> Nkulunkulu wamabandla, akubuyele z, ukhangele phansi usemazulwini aa, ubone, wethekelele lelivini ab, <b>15</b> ngitsho isivini isandla sakho sokunene esasihlanyelayo, lendodana acowaziqinisela yona ad. <b>16</b> Sitshisiwe ngomlilo ae, saqunyelwa phansi af; bayabhubha ngokukhuza kobuso bakho ag. 
<b>17</b> Isandla sakho kasibe phezu komuntu ah wesandla sakho sokunene ai, phezu kwendodana yomuntu aj, oziqinisele yona ak , al. <b>18</b> Ngakho kasiyikubuyela emuva sisuke kuwe; sivuselele, khona sizabiza ibizo lakho am. <b>19</b> Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wamabandla, siphendule, wenze ubuso bakho bukhanye, ngakho sizasindiswa an. 
<b>Psalms 81</b><br><b>1</b> Hlabelani kakhulu kuNkulunkulu amandla ethu a, lihlokome ngentokozo kuNkulunkulu kaJakobe b. <b>2</b> Thathani ingoma, lilethe isigujana c, ichacho elimnandi d kanye logubhu lwezintambo e. <b>3</b> Vuthelani uphondo f ekuthwaseni kwenyanga g, esikhathini esimisiweyo, ngosuku lomkhosi wethu h. <b>4</b> Ngoba lokho kuyisimiso kuIsrayeli, umthetho kaNkulunkulu kaJakobe. <b>5</b> Wakumisa kwaba yibufakazi i kuJosefa j, ekuphumeni kwakhe emelene lelizwe leGibhithe k; ngezwa ulimi l engingalwaziyo m. 
<b>6</b> Ngasusa ihlombe lakhe n emthwalweni o; izandla zakhe zakhululwa ezimbizeni p. <b>7</b> Wakhala usenhluphekweni, ngakukhulula q; ngakuphendula ekusithekeni komdumo r; ngakuhlola emanzini eMeriba. Sela* s. <b>8</b> Zwana, sizwe sami t, ngizafakaza kuwe, Israyeli, aluba ungangilalela u. <b>9</b> Kakungabi khona phakathi kwakho unkulunkulu wezizweni v, njalo ungakhonzi unkulunkulu wezizweni w. <b>10</b> Mina ngiyiNkosi, uNkulunkulu wakho, owakwenyusa usuka elizweni leGibhithe x. Khamisa umlomo wakho ube banzi, njalo ngizawugcwalisa y. 
<b>11</b> Kodwa abantu bami kabalilalelanga ilizwi lami; loIsrayeli kavumelananga lami z. <b>12</b> Ngakho ngabanikela enkanini yenhliziyo yabo aa, ukuze bahambe kumacebo abo ab. <b>13</b> Kungathi abantu bami bebengangilalela ac, uIsrayeli ahambe endleleni zami ad! <b>14</b> Masinyane bengingazehlisela phansi izitha zabo, ngiphendulele isandla sami kwabamelene labo ae. <b>15</b> Abayizondayo iNkosi bangatshotshobala phambi kwayo ngokuzenzisa, kodwa isikhathi sabo besizakuba phakade af. 
<b>16</b> Wayezabondla ngamanono engqoloyi; njalo bengizakusuthisa ngoluju oluvela edwaleni ag.

<b>Psalms 84</b><br><b>1</b> Athandeka kangakanani a amathente akho, Nkosi yamabandla b! <b>2</b> Umphefumulo wami uyalangatha c, yebo ngitsho, uyaphela d yikunxwanela amaguma eNkosi e. <b>Psalms 86</b><br><b>1</b> Beka indlebe yakho, Nkosi, ungiphendule a, ngoba ngingumyanga, ngiyaswela b. <b>2</b> Londoloza umphefumulo wami, ngoba ngiyamesaba uNkulunkulu; wena Nkulunkulu wami, sindisa inceku yakho ekuthembayo c. <b>3</b> Woba lesihawu kimi, Nkosi d, ngoba ngikhala kuwe usuku lonke e. <b>4</b> Thokozisa umphefumulo wenceku yakho, ngoba kuwe, Nkosi, ngiyawuphakamisela umphefumulo wami f. <b>5</b> Ngoba wena, Nkosi, ulungile, ungothethelelayo g; umkhulu emuseni kubo bonke abakubizayo h.

<b>Psalms 89</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizahlabela a ngezisa zeNkosi, kuze kube nininini b , c; ngomlomo wami ngizakwazisa ukuthembeka kwakho kusizukulwana lesizukulwana d , e. <b>2</b> Ngoba ngithe: Umusa uzakwakhiwa kuze kube phakade f; emazulwini ngokwawo uzaqinisa g uthembeko lwakho h. <b>3</b> Ngenze isivumelwano lomkhethwa wami i, ngifungile kuDavida inceku yami j. <b>4</b> Ngizaqinisa inzalo yakho kuze kube nininini k, ngakhe l isihlalo sakho sobukhosi m esizukulwaneni lesizukulwana. <b>24</b> Kodwa uthembeko lwami bh lomusa wami kuzakuba laye bi, lebizweni lami luzaphakanyiswa uphondo lwakhe bj. <b>25</b> Njalo ngizabeka isandla sakhe olwandle bk, lesandla sakhe sokunene emifuleni bl. <b>26</b> Yena uzangibiza ngokuthi: Wena ungubaba wami bm, uNkulunkulu wami, ledwala losindiso lwami bn. <b>27</b> Njalo mina ngizammisa abe lizibulo bo, aphakame kakhulu kulamakhosi omhlaba bp. <b>28</b> Ngizaligcinela umusa wami kuze kube nininini bq, lesivumelwano sami sizaqina kuye br. 
<b>29</b> Njalo ngizamisa inzalo yakhe kuze kube nininini bs, lesihlalo sakhe sobukhosi bt njengensuku zamazulu bu. <b>30</b> Uba abantwana bakhe bv betshiya umlayo wami bw, bangahambi ngezahlulelo zami, <b>31</b> uba besephula izimiso zami, bengagcini imithetho yami, <b>32</b> ngizaphindisela iziphambeko zabo bx ngenduku by, lesono sabo ngenswazi bz. <b>33</b> Kodwa kangiyikulususa du uthandolomusa wami kuye ca, ngingakhohlisi ngothembeko lwami cb. <b>34</b> Kangiyikwephula cc isivumelwano sami cd, lalokho okuphuma endebeni zami kangiyikukuguqula.

Sela* ck. <b>38</b> Kodwa wena usulahlile cl wala cm , cn, wamthukuthelela co , cp ogcotshiweyo wakho cq. <b>39</b> Usenze ize isivumelwano senceku yakho cr; wangcolisa cs umqhele wakhe ct emhlabathini cu. 
<b>40</b> Uyibhobozile yonke imithangala yakhe, wazenza inqaba zakhe amanxiwa cv. <b>41</b> Bonke abadlula ngendlela bayamphanga cw; ulihlazo kubomakhelwane bakhe cx. <b>42</b> Usiphakamisile isandla sokunene sabamelene laye; wazithokozisa zonke izitha zakhe. <b>43</b> Futhi ubuyisele emuva ubukhali benkemba yakhe; kawumenzanga ame empini. <b>44</b> Uyiqedile inkazimulo yakhe, lesihlalo sakhe sobukhosi wasiphosela emhlabathini cy. <b>45</b> Ufinyezile insuku zobutsha bakhe, wamembesa ihlazo. Sela* cz.

Ulaka lwakho luvutha njengomlilo yini de? <b>47</b> Khumbula df ukuthi isikhathi sempilo yami singakanani dg; kungani wadalela ize bonke abantwana babantu dh? <b>48</b> Ngubani umuntu ophilayo di ongaboni ukufa dj? Akhulule umphefumulo wakhe esandleni sengcwaba na? Sela*. <b>49</b> Zingaphi izisa zakho zakuqala, Nkosi dk, owazifunga kuDavida dl ngothembeko lwakho dm , dn? 
<b>50</b> Khumbula, Nkosi, ihlazo lezinceku zakho do, engilithwele esifubeni sami, elazo zonke izizwe ezinengi dp, <b>51</b> izitha zakho ezithuke ngalo, Nkosi dq, ezithuke ngalo izinyathelo dr zogcotshiweyo wakho ds. <b>52</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi kuze kube nininini. Ameni, loAmeni dt. 
<b>Psalms 90</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, wena ubuyindawo yethu yokuhlala kusizukulwana lesizukulwana a. <b>2</b> Zingakazalwa intaba b , c , d, kumbe uveze umhlaba lelizwe e , f , g, ngitsho kusukela ephakadeni kuze kube sephakadeni wena unguNkulunkulu h. <b>3</b> Uyambuyisela umuntu encithakalweni, uthi: Buyelani, bantwana babantu i. <b>4</b> Ngoba iminyaka eyinkulungwane emehlweni akho j injengelanga lezolo selidlulile, lanjengomlindo ebusuku k. <b>5</b> Uyabakhukhula l, banjengobuthongo m, ekuseni banjengotshani n , o obuhlumayo p. 
<b>6</b> Ekuseni q buyakhahlela r , s, bukhule t, ntambama u buyasikwa v , w, bubune. <b>7</b> Ngoba siqedwa yikuthukuthela kwakho, langolaka lwakho sibhujiswe. <b>8</b> Ubekile iziphambeko zethu phambi kwakho x, izono zethu ezifihlakeleyo ekukhanyeni kobuso bakho y. <b>9</b> Ngoba insuku zethu zonke ziyedlula elakeni lwakho; sichitha iminyaka yethu njengokubeka iphika. 
<b>10</b> Insuku zeminyaka yethu, kuleminyaka engamatshumi ayisikhombisa kuzo; njalo uba ngenxa yamandla zingamatshumi ayisificaminwembili, kanti ukuzigqaja kwayo kuyinhlupheko losizi, ngoba aphangisa aqunywe, sibe sesiphapha sinyamalale z. <b>11</b> Ngubani owaziyo amandla okuthukuthela kwakho? Lanjengokwesabeka kwakho lunjalo ulaka lwakho. <b>12</b> Ngokunjalo sifundise ukubala insuku zethu, ukuze sizuze inhliziyo yenhlakanipho aa. <b>13</b> Phenduka, Nkosi ab! Koze kube nini ac? Hawukela izinceku zakho ad. 
<b>14</b> Sisuthise ekuseni ngomusa wakho ae, ukuze sithabe sijabule insuku zethu zonke af. <b>15</b> Sithokozise njengokwensuku owasihlupha ngazo ag, iminyaka esabona okubi kuyo ah. <b>16</b> Umsebenzi wakho kawubonakale ezincekwini zakho, lenkazimulo yakho ebantwaneni bazo ai. <b>17</b> Ubuhle beNkosi, uNkulunkulu wethu, kabube phezu kwethu aj; uqinise umsebenzi wezandla zethu kithi, yebo, umsebenzi wezandla zethu uwuqinise ak. 
<b>Psalms 91</b><br><b>1</b> Ohlezi ekusithekeni koPhezukonke a uzahlala emthunzini kaSomandla b. <b>2</b> Ngizakuthi eNkosini: Uyisiphephelo sami c lenqaba yami d, uNkulunkulu wami, engithemba kuye e. <b>3</b> Sibili yena uzakukhulula f emjibileni womthiyi g, kuye umatshayabhuqe wesifo obhubhisayo h. <b>4</b> Uzakusibekela ngensiba zakhe i, uzathembela langaphansi kwempiko zakhe j , k , l; iqiniso lakhe liyisihlangu lehawu m. <b>5</b> Kawuyikwesaba isesabiso sebusuku, umtshoko ophaphayo emini n, 
<b>6</b> umatshayabhuqe wesifo ohambayo emnyameni o, incithakalo ebhubhisayo emini enkulu p. <b>7</b> Bazakuwa abayinkulungwane eceleni kwakho, labayizinkulungwane ezilitshumi ngakwesokunene sakho; kuwe kakuyikusondela. <b>8</b> Uzakhangela nje ngamehlo akho, ubone impindiselo kwababi q. <b>9</b> Ngoba wena, Nkosi, uyisiphephelo sami r; umenzile oPhezukonke waba yindawo yakho yokuhlala s. <b>10</b> Kakulabubi obuzakwehlela t, lenhlupheko kayiyikusondela u ethenteni lakho v. 
<b>11</b> Ngoba uzalaya w ingilosi zakhe ngawe, ukuthi zikulondoloze endleleni zakho zonke x. <b>12</b> Zizakuphatha ezandleni, hlezi utshayeke ngonyawo lwakho elitsheni y. <b>13</b> Uzanyathela z isilwane lebululu aa, uzagxoba ibhongo lesilwane, lomgobho ab. <b>14</b> Ngoba uyangithanda kakhulu, ngakho ngizamkhulula ac; ngizambeka phezulu ad, ngoba uyalazi ibizo lami ae. <b>15</b> Uzangibiza, njalo ngimphendule; ngibe laye ekuhluphekeni af, ngimkhulule ag, ngimnike udumo ah , ai. 
<b>16</b> Ngizamsuthisa ngobude bezinsuku, ngimtshengise usindiso lwami aj. 
<b>Psalms 92</b><br><b>1</b> Kuhle a ukubonga iNkosi b, lokuhlabela indumiso ebizweni lakho, wena oPhezukonke c; <b>2</b> ukutshumayela uthandolomusa wakho ekuseni d, lothembeko lwakho ebusuku bonke e, <b>3</b> ngechacho elilentambo ezilitshumi f, langogubhu lwezintambo g , h, ngechacho i , j elikhala kamnandi. <b>4</b> Ngoba ungithokozisile, Nkosi, ngomsebenzi wakho k; ngizamemeza ngenjabulo ngemisebenzi yezandla zakho l. <b>5</b> Mikhulu kangakanani imisebenzi yakho, Nkosi m! <b>Psalms 94</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wempindiselo a, Nkulunkulu wempindiselo, khanyisa b! <b>2</b> Phakama c, Mahluleli womhlaba, uphindisele umvuzo kwabazigqajayo d. <b>3</b> Nkosi, koze kube nini ababi e, koze kube nini ababi begiya f? <b>4</b> Bepopota g, bekhuluma ngokuziphakamisa h, bonke abenzi bobubi bezikhukhumeza i? <b>5</b> Nkosi, bayabaphohloza abantu bakho, bayalihlupha ilifa lakho j. <b>6</b> Babulala umfelokazi lowezizweni, lentandane bazibulale. <b>7</b> Kanti bathi k: INkosi kayiboni, loNkulunkulu kaJakobe kananzi l.

<b>Psalms 96</b><br><b>1</b> Hlabelani eNkosini a , b ingoma entsha c; hlabelelani eNkosini, mhlaba wonke d. <b>2</b> Hlabelani eNkosini, lidumise ibizo layo, litshumayele usindiso lwayo insuku ngensuku. <b>3</b> Landisani udumo lwayo phakathi kwezizwe, izimangaliso zayo phakathi kwabantu bonke. <b>4</b> Ngoba yinkulu iNkosi, ifanele ukudunyiswa kakhulu, yona iyesabeka phezu kwabonkulunkulu bonke e. <b>5</b> Ngoba bonke onkulunkulu bezizwe bayizithombe; kodwa iNkosi yenza amazulu.

Khona izihlahla zonke zegusu zizahlabela ngentokozo t <b>13</b> phambi kweNkosi, ngoba iyeza, ngoba iyeza ukwahlulela umhlaba u; izakwahlulela umhlaba ngokulunga, labantu ngeqiniso layo v. 
<b>Psalms 97</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi iyabusa a; kawuthokoze umhlaba b, kazijabule izihlenge ezinengi c. <b>2</b> Amayezi d lomnyama kuyiphahlile e; ukulunga lesahlulelo f kuyisisekelo sesihlalo sayo sobukhosi g. <b>3</b> Umlilo uhamba phambi kwayo, utshisa izitha zayo inhlangothi zonke h. <b>4</b> Imibane yayo ikhanyisa umhlaba; umhlaba wabona, wathuthumela i. <b>5</b> Izintaba zancibilika j njengengcino phambi kweNkosi k, phambi kweNkosi yomhlaba wonke l. <b>6</b> Amazulu atshumayela ukulunga kwayo, labantu bonke babona inkazimulo yayo m.
