<b>Psalms 100</b><br><b>1</b> Hlabelelani iNkosi ngokuthaba a, mhlaba wonke b. <b>2</b> Ikhonzeni iNkosi ngenjabulo, wozani phambi kwayo ngokuhlabela ngentokozo c. <b>3</b> Yazini ukuthi iNkosi inguNkulunkulu. Yiyo eyasenzayo, njalo ayisithi d: singabantu bayo e lezimvu zedlelo layo f. <b>4</b> Ngenani emasangweni ayo ngokubonga g, emagumeni ayo ngokudumisa h. Ibongeni, lidumise ibizo layo. <b>5</b> Ngoba iNkosi ilungile i; umusa wayo uhlezi kuze kube nininini j, leqiniso layo esizukulwaneni lesizukulwana k. 
<b>Psalms 101</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizahlabela ngomusa lesahlulelo; ngizahlabela kuwe, Nkosi. <b>2</b> Ngizaziphatha ngenhlakanipho ngendlela eqotho a. Uzakuza nini kimi b , c? Ngizahamba ngobuqotho benhliziyo yami phakathi kwendlu yami d , e. <b>3</b> Kangiyikubeka phambi kwamehlo ami into embi. Ngiyakuzonda ukwenza kwabaphambukayo; kakuyikunamathela kimi f , g. <b>4</b> Inhliziyo ephambeneyo izasuka kimi h; kangiyikumazi omubi i. 
<b>5</b> Ohleba umakhelwane wakhe ensitha, yena ngizamquma j. Ophakeme ngamehlo k, lolenhliziyo ezikhukhumezayo l, yena kangiyikumvumela m. <b>6</b> Amehlo ami azakuba kwabathembekileyo belizwe, ukuze bahlale lami. Ohamba endleleni eqotho, yena uzangikhonza n. <b>7</b> Owenza inkohliso kayikuhlala phakathi kwendlu yami; okhuluma amanga kayikuma phambi kwamehlo ami o. <b>8</b> Insuku zonke ekuseni ngizabaquma bonke ababi belizwe p , q, ukuze ngibaqume basuke emzini weNkosi bonke abenzi bobubi r , s. 
<b>Psalms 102</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, zwana umkhuleko wami a, lokukhala kwami b kakuze kuwe c. <b>2</b> Ungangifihleli ubuso bakho d osukwini lohlupho lwami e; beka indlebe yakho kimi f; mhla ngibiza g ungiphendule masinyane h. <b>3</b> Ngoba insuku zami ziyanyamalala njengentuthu i, lamathambo ami ayatsha njengeziko j. <b>4</b> Inhliziyo yami itshayiwe, yabuna njengotshani k, ngaze ngakhohlwa ukudla isinkwa sami. <b>5</b> Ngenxa yelizwi lokububula kwami amathambo ami anamathela esikhumbeni sami l. 
<b>6</b> Ngifanana lewunkwe yenkangala m, senginjengesikhova sezindawo ezidilikileyo n. <b>7</b> Ngiyalinda, senginjengenyoni ehlezi yodwa ephahleni o. <b>8</b> Izitha zami ziyangithuka usuku lonke; abangihlanyelayo p bafunga ngami q. <b>9</b> Ngoba ngidle umlotha njengesinkwa, ngixubanise okunathwayo kwami lezinyembezi r, <b>10</b> ngenxa yentukuthelo yakho lolaka lwakho; ngoba ungiphakamisile s, wasungilahla phansi t. <b>11</b> Insuku zami zinjengesithunzi u eselulekayo v, njalo mina ngiyabuna njengotshani w. 
<b>12</b> Kodwa wena, Nkosi, uzahlala kuze kube nininini x, lesikhumbuzo sakho y kusizukulwana lesizukulwana z. <b>13</b> Wena uzavuka uhawukele iZiyoni aa; ngoba isikhathi sokuyitshengisa umusa, yebo, isikhathi esimisiweyo sesifikile ab. <b>14</b> Ngoba inceku zakho ziyawathanda amatshe ayo ac, zilesihawu ethulini lwayo ad. <b>15</b> Khona izizwe zizalesaba ibizo leNkosi ae, lamakhosi wonke omhlaba udumo lwayo af. <b>16</b> Lapho iNkosi izayakha iZiyoni ag, izabonakala enkazimulweni yayo ah. 
<b>17</b> Izaphendukela emkhulekweni ai wongelalutho aj, kayiyikudelela umthandazo wabo. <b>18</b> Lokhu kuzabhalelwa ak isizukulwana esilandelayo al, labantu abazadalwa am bazadumisa iNkosi an. <b>19</b> Ngoba yakhangela phansi isekuphakameni kwendawo yayo engcwele ao; iNkosi isemazulwini yakhangela emhlabeni ap, <b>20</b> ukuzwa ukububula kwesibotshwa, ukukhulula abamiselwa ukufa aq; <b>21</b> ukulandisa ibizo leNkosi eZiyoni, lendumiso yayo eJerusalema ar, <b>22</b> lapho izizwe zibuthana ndawonye, lemibuso, ukuyikhonza iNkosi as. 
<b>23</b> Yathoba amandla ami endleleni, yafinyeza insuku zami at. <b>24</b> Ngathi: Nkulunkulu wami au, ungangisusi av phakathi kwensuku zami aw, iminyaka yakho ax isesizukulwaneni lezizukulwana ay. <b>25</b> Endulo wawusekela umhlaba, lamazulu angumsebenzi wezandla zakho az. <b>26</b> Lokho kuzabhubha ba, kodwa wena uzakuma bb. Kuzaguga-ke konke njengesembatho, njengesigqoko uzakuguqula, njalo kuzaguqulwa. <b>27</b> Kodwa wena unguwe njalo, leminyaka yakho kayipheli bc. 
<b>28</b> Abantwana benceku zakho bd bazahlala be, lenzalo yazo bf izaqiniswa phambi kwakho bg. 
<b>Psalms 103</b><br><b>1</b> Bonga iNkosi, mphefumulo wami, lakho konke okuphakathi kwami kubonge ibizo layo elingcwele a. <b>2</b> Busisa iNkosi, mphefumulo wami b, ungakhohlwa zonke izinzuzo zayo c, <b>3</b> ethethelela zonke izono zakho d , e, eyelapha zonke izifo zakho f , g, <b>4</b> ehlenga h impilo yakho i ekubhujisweni j, ekwethwesa umqhele womusa lezihawu k, <b>5</b> esuthisa umlomo wakho ngokuhle l, ukuze ubutsha bakho buvuswe njengobokhozi m. <b>6</b> INkosi iyabenzela ukulunga n lezahlulelo bonke abacindezelweyo o. 
<b>7</b> Yamazisa uMozisi indlela zayo, abantwana bakoIsrayeli izenzo zayo p. <b>8</b> INkosi ilesihawu lomusa, iyaphuza ukuthukuthela, njalo ivame ububele q. <b>9</b> Kayiyikuphikisana njalonjalo r, kayiyikugcina intukuthelo kuze kube nininini s. <b>10</b> Kayisenzelanga njengokwezono zethu t, kayisiphindiselanga njengokweziphambeko zethu. u <b>11</b> Ngoba njengokuphakama kwamazulu ngaphezu komhlaba v, umkhulu umusa wayo w kubo abayesabayo x. 
<b>12</b> Njengobukhatshana bempumalanga kuyo intshonalanga, ngokunjalo izisusele khatshana lathi iziphambeko zethu y. <b>13</b> Njengoyise ehawukela abantwana z, iNkosi iyabahawukela labo abayesabayo aa. <b>14</b> Ngoba iyakwazi ukubunjwa kwethu ab, ikhumbula ac ukuthi siluthuli ad. <b>15</b> Umuntu, insuku zakhe zinjengotshani ae; njengeluba leganga af, ngokunjalo uyakhahlela ag. <b>16</b> Ngoba umoya wedlula phezu kwakhe, njalo angabikho ah, lendawo yakhe ingabe isamazi ai. 
<b>17</b> Kodwa umusa weNkosi usukela ephakadeni usiya ephakadeni aj phezu kwabayesabayo ak, lokulunga kwayo kuze kube sebantwaneni babantwana al, <b>18</b> kubo abagcina isivumelwano sayo am, lakubo abakhumbula imithetho yayo ukuyenza an. <b>19</b> INkosi imisile isihlalo sayo sobukhosi emazulwini ao, lombuso wayo ubusa phezu kwakho konke ap. <b>20</b> Busisani iNkosi, lina zingilosi zayo aq, maqhawe alamandla, ezenza ilizwi layo, lilalela ilizwi lelizwi layo ar. 
<b>21</b> Bongani iNkosi, lonke mabandla ayo as , at, zikhonzi zayo au, ezenza intando yayo av. <b>22</b> Bongani iNkosi, lonke misebenzi yayo, endaweni zonke zombuso wayo aw. Bonga iNkosi, mphefumulo wami ax. 
<b>Psalms 104</b><br><b>1</b> Bonga iNkosi, mphefumulo wami a. Nkosi Nkulunkulu wami, umkhulu kakhulu b; wembethe c ubukhosi lodumo d. <b>2</b> Ozembesa ngokukhanya njengesembatho e, owendlala amazulu f njengekhetheni g. <b>3</b> Owabeka imijabo h yamakamelo akhe i aphezulu emanzini j, owenza amayezi abe yinqola yakhe k, ohamba phezu kwempiko zomoya l. <b>4</b> Owenza izingilosi zakhe zibe yimimoya m, izinceku zakhe n zibe ngumlilo olamalangabi o. <b>5</b> Wamisa umhlaba phezu kwezisekelo zawo p, kawuyikuzanyazanyiswa phakade laphakade q. 
<b>6</b> Wawugubuzela ngokujula njengesembatho; amanzi ema phezu kwezintaba r. <b>7</b> Ekukhuzeni kwakho abaleka s; elizwini lokuduma kwakho t aphangisa asuka u; <b>8</b> - zenyuka izintaba, zehla izihotsha v - endaweni owawuwamisele wona w. <b>9</b> Wamisa x umngcele angewudlule y, kawayikubuya agubuzele umhlaba z. <b>10</b> Othumela imithombo ezihotsheni, yahamba phakathi kwezintaba. <b>11</b> Yanathisa yonke inyamazana yeganga aa, obabhemi beganga bacitsha ukoma kwabo ab. 
<b>12</b> Inyoni zamazulu zakhela emaceleni ayo, zizwakalisa ilizwi ziphakathi kwezingatsha ac. <b>13</b> Uthelela izintaba ad esemakamelweni akhe aphezulu ae; umhlaba af usuthiswa ag ngesithelo semisebenzi yakho ah. <b>14</b> Umilisela izifuyo utshani ai, lemibhida yokusiza umuntu aj, ukuveza ukudla emhlabathini ak , al, <b>15</b> lewayini am elithokozisa inhliziyo yomuntu an, ukwenza ubuso bukhanye ngamafutha ao, lesinkwa esiqinisa inhliziyo yomuntu ap. <b>16</b> Ziyasutha aq izihlahla zeNkosi ar, imisedari yeLebhanoni as eyayihlanyelayo at. 
<b>17</b> Lapho inyoni ezakhela khona izidleke; ingabuzane, izihlahla zefiri ziyindlu yalo au. <b>18</b> Intaba eziphakemeyo zingezamagogo av, amadwala ayisiphephelo sezimbila aw. <b>19</b> Inyanga wayenzela izikhathi ezimisiweyo ax; ilanga liyazi ukutshona kwalo ay. <b>20</b> Wenza umnyama, kube sebusuku az, phakathi kwabo kuphume yonke inyamazana yehlathi ba. <b>21</b> Izilwane ezintsha bb zibhongela impango bc, lokudinga ukudla kwazo kuNkulunkulu bd. <b>22</b> Ilanga liphuma, zibuthane, zilale ezikhundleni zazo be. 
<b>23</b> Umuntu uphuma bf aye emsebenzini wakhe lemtshikatshikeni wakhe kuze kuhlwe bg. <b>24</b> Minengi kangakanani imisebenzi yakho, Nkosi! Yonke uyenze ngenhlakanipho bh; umhlaba ugcwele inotho yakho bi. <b>25</b> Lolulwandle olukhulu lolubanzi bj ngezinhlangothi, lapho okulezihuquzelayo khona ezingelakubalwa, izinto eziphilayo ezincinyane kanye lezinkulu bk. <b>26</b> Kuhamba khona imikhumbi, uLeviyathani* bl owambumbela ukuthi adlale kulo bm. <b>27</b> Zonke lezi zilindele kuwe bn, ukuthi uzinike ukudla kwazo ngesikhathi sazo bo , bp. 
<b>28</b> Uyazinika, zibuthe; uvula isandla sakho, zisuthe okuhle bq. <b>29</b> Ufihla ubuso bakho, zikhathazeke br; ususa umoya wazo, zife bs, zibuyele ethulini lwazo bt , bu. <b>30</b> Uthumela umoya wakho, zidalwe; wenze bube butsha ubuso bomhlaba. <b>31</b> Udumo lweNkosi kalume kuze kube nininini; iNkosi kayithokoze ngemisebenzi yayo bv. <b>32</b> Ikhangela umhlaba, uthuthumele bw; ithinta izintaba, zithunqe bx. <b>33</b> Ngizahlabelela iNkosi ngisaphila by; ngizahlabelela indumiso kuNkulunkulu wami nxa ngisekhona bz. 
<b>34</b> Ukuzindla kwami ngaye kuzakuba mnandi; mina ngizathokoza eNkosini ca. <b>35</b> Izoni kaziqedwe emhlabeni, kungabe kusaba khona ababi cb. Bonga iNkosi, mphefumulo wami. Dumisani iNkosi cc! 
<b>Psalms 105</b><br><b>1</b> Bongani iNkosi, libize ibizo layo a , b, lazise phakathi kwezizwe izenzo zayo c. <b>2</b> Hlabelelani kuyo, lihlabelele amahubo kuyo, likhulume ngezenzo zayo zonke ezimangalisayo. <b>3</b> Zincomeni ebizweni layo elingcwele; kayithokoze inhliziyo yabadinga iNkosi. <b>4</b> Dingani iNkosi lamandla ayo, lidinge ubuso bayo njalonjalo d. <b>5</b> Khumbulani izenzo zayo ezimangalisayo eyazenzayo, izimangaliso zayo lezahlulelo zomlomo wayo e, <b>6</b> nzalo kaAbrahama inceku yayo f, bantwana bakaJakobe abakhethiweyo bayo g. 
<b>7</b> Yona iyiNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu; izahlulelo zayo zisemhlabeni wonke. <b>8</b> Iyakhumbula isivumelwano sayo kuze kube nininini h, ilizwi eyalilaya kuzizukulwana eziyinkulungwane i, <b>9</b> eyasenza loAbrahama, lesifungo sayo kuIsaka j; <b>10</b> eyasiqinisa kuJakobe saba yisimiso, kuIsrayeli saba yisivumelwano esilaphakade, <b>11</b> isithi: Ngizakunika wena ilizwe leKhanani, isabelo selifa lakho k. <b>12</b> Besebalutshwana ngenani l, yebo, bebalutshwana, labemzini kulo m; 
<b>13</b> basebezula besuka esizweni besiya esizweni, besuka komunye umbuso besiya kwabanye abantu; <b>14</b> kayivumelanga muntu ukubahlupha; yasikhuza amakhosi ngenxa yabo, <b>15</b> isithi: Lingathinti abagcotshiweyo bami n, lingoni abaprofethi bami o. <b>16</b> Yasibiza indlala phezu kwelizwe p, yephula q udondolo lonke lwesinkwa r. <b>17</b> Yathuma indoda phambi kwabo s: UJosefa wathengiswa waba yisigqili t. <b>18</b> Bazwisa ubuhlungu inyawo zakhe u ngamaketane, yena ngokwakhe wafika ensimbini v. 
<b>19</b> Kwaze kwaba yisikhathi sokufika kwelizwi lakhe w, ilizwi likaJehova x lamlinga y. <b>20</b> Inkosi yathuma z yamthukulula, umbusi wabantu wasemkhulula aa. <b>21</b> Yambeka waba yinkosi yendlu yayo, lombusi wemfuyo yayo yonke ab, <b>22</b> ukubopha iziphathamandla zayo ac ngokuthanda kwakhe, afundise abadala bakhe ukuhlakanipha ad. <b>23</b> UIsrayeli wasefika eGibhithe ae; uJakobe wahlala njengowezizwe elizweni lakoHamu af. <b>24</b> Wenza abantu bayo bazale kakhulu, yabenza baba lamandla kulezitha zabo ag. 
<b>25</b> Yaphendula inhliziyo yabo ukuzonda abantu bayo ah, ukuthi baphathe inceku zayo ngobuqili ai. <b>26</b> Yasithuma uMozisi inceku yayo aj, uAroni ak eyayimkhethile al. <b>27</b> Babeka phakathi kwabo izinto zezibonakaliso zayo am, lezimangaliso an elizweni lakoHamu ao. <b>28</b> Yathumela umnyama ap, yenza kwaba mnyama aq; njalo kabavukelanga ilizwi layo ar. <b>29</b> Yaphendula amanzi abo aba ligazi, yabulala inhlanzi zawo as. <b>30</b> Ilizwe labo at lanyakazela amaxoxo au, emakamelweni amakhosi abo. 
<b>31</b> Yakhuluma av, kwavela umtshitshi wezibawu aw, intwala emngceleni wabo wonke ax. <b>32</b> Yabanika isiqhotho saba lizulu ay, umlilo olamalangabi elizweni labo az. <b>33</b> Yasitshaya isivini sabo lomkhiwa wabo, yephula izihlahla zomngcele wabo ba. <b>34</b> Yakhuluma bb, kwasekuvela isikhonyane bc lemihogoyi, okungelakubalwa bd, <b>35</b> okwaqeda yonke imibhida elizweni labo, kwaqeda isithelo somhlabathi wabo. <b>36</b> Yasitshaya amazibulo wonke elizweni labo be, okokuqala kwamandla abo wonke bf. 
<b>37</b> Yasibakhupha belesiliva legolide bg, njalo kwakungekho owakhubekayo phakathi kwezizwe zabo bh. <b>38</b> IGibhithe yathokoza ekuphumeni kwabo bi, ngoba uvalo ngabo lwabehlela bj. <b>39</b> Yendlala iyezi laba yisisibekelo, lomlilo wokukhanyisa ebusuku bk , bl. <b>40</b> Bacela bm, yasiletha izagwaca bn, yabasuthisa ngesinkwa samazulu bo. <b>41</b> Yavula idwala bp, kwampompoza amanzi bq; ahamba endaweni ezomileyo njengomfula br. <b>42</b> Ngoba yakhumbula isithembiso sayo esingcwele, loAbrahama inceku yayo bs. 
<b>43</b> Yasikhupha abantu bayo ngentokozo, abakhethiweyo bayo ngenjabulo bt. <b>44</b> Yasibanika amazwe abezizwe bu, njalo badla ilifa lomtshikatshika wabantu bv, <b>45</b> ukuze bagcine izimiso zayo bw, balondoloze imilayo yayo. Dumisani iNkosi bx! 
<b>Psalms 106</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Ibongeni iNkosi a ngoba ilungile b, ngoba umusa wayo umi kuze kube nininini c. <b>2</b> Ngubani ongalandisa izenzo ezilamandla zeNkosi, azwakalise yonke indumiso yayo? <b>3</b> Babusisiwe abagcina izahlulelo, owenza ukulunga d ngaso sonke isikhathi e. <b>4</b> Ngikhumbule, Nkosi, ngothando olalo kubantu bakho; ngethekelele ngosindiso lwakho f; <b>5</b> ukuze ngibone okuhle kwabakhethiweyo bakho g, ukuze ngithokoze entokozweni yesizwe sakho, ukuze ngizincome kanye lelifa lakho h. 
<b>6</b> Sonile i kanye labobaba j, senze okubi k , l, senza ngenkohlakalo m , n. <b>7</b> Obaba eGibhithe kabaqedisisanga izimangaliso zakho o; kabakhumbulanga ubunengi bezisa zakho p; kodwa benza umvukela elwandle q, eLwandle oluBomvu r. <b>8</b> Loba kunjalo wabakhulula ngenxa yebizo lakhe s, ukuze azise amandla akhe t. <b>9</b> Wasekhuza uLwandle oluBomvu u , v, lwatsha w; wasebahambisa phakathi kwezinziki njengenkangala x. <b>10</b> Wasebasindisa esandleni somzondi y, wabahlenga esandleni sesitha z. 
<b>11</b> Amanzi asibekela izitha zabo aa; kakusalanga loyedwa kuzo ab. <b>12</b> Basebekholwa amazwi akhe ac, bahlabelela indumiso yakhe ad. <b>13</b> Baphanga bakhohlwa imisebenzi yakhe ae, kabalindelanga iseluleko sakhe af. <b>14</b> Kodwa bakhanuka lokukhanuka ehlane ag, bamlinga uNkulunkulu ah enkangala ai. <b>15</b> Wasebanika isicelo sabo aj, kodwa wathumela ukucaka emphefumulweni wabo ak. <b>16</b> Basebesiba lomhawu ngoMozisi enkambeni al, ngoAroni ongcwele weNkosi am. 
<b>17</b> Umhlaba wavuleka waginya uDathani an, wasibekela ao iqembu likaAbiramu ap. <b>18</b> Lomlilo wavutha eqenjini labo aq, ilangabi latshisa ar abakhohlakeleyo as. <b>19</b> Benza ithole at eHorebe au, bakhonza isithombe esibunjwe ngokuncibilikisa. <b>20</b> Basebeguqula inkazimulo yabo av yaba yisifaniso senkabi edla utshani aw. <b>21</b> Bamkhohlwa uNkulunkulu umsindisi wabo ax, owayenze izinto ezinkulu eGibhithe ay, <b>22</b> izimangaliso elizweni likaHamu az; izinto ezesabekayo ba eLwandle oluBomvu bb. 
<b>23</b> Ngakho wathi bc uzababhubhisa bd, ngaphandle kokuthi uMozisi okhethiweyo wakhe wayemile be ekufohleni phambi kwakhe bf ukuphendula ulaka lwakhe ukuze angababhubhisi bg. <b>24</b> Yebo, baleyisa ilizwe elihle bh, kabalikholwanga ilizwi lakhe bi. <b>25</b> Kodwa bakhonona emathenteni abo, kabalilalelanga ilizwi leNkosi bj , bk. <b>26</b> Ngakho waphakamisa isandla sakhe emelene labo, ukubawisa enkangala bl, <b>27</b> lokuwisa inzalo yabo phakathi kwezizwe, lokubahlakaza emazweni bm. 
<b>28</b> Basebezihlanganisa bn loBhali-Peyori bo, badla imihlatshelo bp yabafileyo bq. <b>29</b> Basebemthukuthelisa ngezenzo zabo, lenhlupheko yafohlela kibo br. <b>30</b> Kwasekusukuma uPhinehasi bs, wenza isigwebo bt, inhlupheko yasimiswa bu. <b>31</b> Lokhu kwasekubalelwa kuye kube yikulunga, esizukulwaneni ngesizukulwana kuze kube nininini bv. <b>32</b> Basebemthukuthelisa emanzini empikisano bw , bx, kwaze kwaba kubi kuMozisi ngenxa yabo by. <b>33</b> Ngoba bacunula umoya wakhe bz, waze wakhuluma ngamawala ngendebe zakhe ca , cb. 
<b>34</b> Kabazibhubhisanga izizwe cc, iNkosi eyayibatshele ngazo cd. <b>35</b> Kodwa bazihlanganisa lezizwe, bafunda imisebenzi yazo ce. <b>36</b> Basebekhonza izithombe zazo cf ezaba ngumjibila kubo cg. <b>37</b> Lamadodana abo lamadodakazi abo bawahlabela amadimoni ch , ci. <b>38</b> Bachitha igazi elingelacala, igazi lamadodana abo lamadodakazi abo, abawahlabela izithombe zeKhanani cj; lelizwe langcoliswa ngegazi elinengi ck. <b>39</b> Basebengcola ngemisebenzi yabo cl, baphinga ngezenzo zabo cm , cn , co. 
<b>40</b> Ngakho ulaka lweNkosi cp lwabavuthela abantu bayo cq, yanengwa yilifa layo cr. <b>41</b> Yabanikela esandleni sezizwe, lababazondayo bababusa cs , ct. <b>42</b> Lezitha zabo zabacindezela cu, basebethotshiswa ngaphansi kwesandla sazo cv. <b>43</b> Yabakhulula kanengi cw; kodwa bona bayivukela cx ngecebo labo, behliswa ngobubi babo cy. <b>44</b> Loba kunjalo yabona ukuhlupheka kwabo cz, lapho isizwa da ukukhala kwabo db. <b>45</b> Yabakhumbulela isivumelwano sayo dc, yazisola dd njengobunengi bezisa zayo de. 
<b>46</b> Yabanika izisa phambi kwabo bonke ababebathumbile df , dg. <b>47</b> Sisindise, Nkosi Nkulunkulu wethu dh, usibuthe sivela ezizweni, ukubonga ibizo lakho elingcwele, ukuzincoma endumisweni yakho di. <b>48</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, kusukela phakade kuze kube sephakadeni dj! Abantu bonke kabathi dk: Ameni dl. Dumisani iNkosi! 
<b>Psalms 107</b><br><b>1</b> Ibongeni iNkosi, ngoba ilungile, ngoba umusa wayo umi kuze kube phakade a. <b>2</b> Kabatsho njalo abahlengiweyo beNkosi b ebahlengileyo esandleni sesitha c. <b>3</b> Yababutha d bevela emazweni, empumalanga lentshonalanga, enyakatho lelwandle e. <b>4</b> Bazulazula enkangala f endleleni yenhlane g, kabatholanga umuzi wokuhlala h. <b>5</b> Belambile njalo bomile i, umphefumulo wabo waphela amandla phakathi kwabo j. <b>6</b> Khona bakhala eNkosini k ekuhluphekeni kwabo, yabakhulula ezinsizini zabo l. 
<b>7</b> Yabakhokhela ngendlela eqondileyo m, ukuze baye emzini wokuhlala n. <b>8</b> Kabayidumise iNkosingomusa wayo langemisebenzi yayo emangalisayo ebantwaneni babantu o! <b>9</b> Ngoba iyasuthisa p umphefumulo olangathayo q, lomphefumulo olambileyo iwugcwalisa ngokuhle r. <b>10</b> Abahlala emnyameni s lethunzini lokufa t, bebotshiwe ensizini lensimbini u, <b>11</b> ngoba bavukela amazwi kaNkulunkulu v, badelela w iseluleko soPhezukonke x. <b>12</b> Ngakho wathoba inhliziyo yabo ngokuhlupheka; bakhubeka, njalo kwakungekho umsizi y. 
<b>13</b> Khona bakhala eNkosini ekuhluphekeni kwabo, yabasindisa ezinsizini zabo z. <b>14</b> Yabakhupha aa emnyameni lethunzini lokufa ab, yaqamula izibopho zabo ac. <b>15</b> Kabayidumise iNkosingomusa wayo, langemisebenzi yayo emangalisayo ebantwaneni babantu ad! <b>16</b> Ngoba yephule izivalo zethusi, yaquma imigoqo yensimbi ae. <b>17</b> Abayizithutha, ngenxa yendlela yeziphambeko zabo langenxa yezono zabo, bahlutshiwe af. <b>18</b> Umphefumulo wabo wenyanya ukudla konke ag, basondela ah emasangweni okufa ai. 
<b>19</b> Khona bakhala eNkosini ekuhluphekeni kwabo, yabasindisa ezinsizini zabo aj. <b>20</b> Yathumela ak , al ilizwi layo am, yabasilisa an, yabakhulula ao emigodini yabo ap. <b>21</b> Kabayidumise iNkosingomusa wayo, langemisebenzi yayo emangalisayo ebantwaneni babantu aq! <b>22</b> Njalo kabahlabe imihlatshelo yokubonga ar, balandise izenzo zayo ngokuthaba as. <b>23</b> Abehlela olwandle ngemikhumbi at, abenza imisebenzi emanzini amakhulu au; <b>24</b> laba babona izenzo zeNkosi, lemisebenzi yayo emangalisayo ekujuleni. 
<b>25</b> Ngoba iyakhuluma av, ivuse isiphepho somoya, esiqubula amagagasi alo aw. <b>26</b> Benyukela emazulwini, behlele enzikini; umphefumulo wabo uncibilika ngosizi ax. <b>27</b> Bayadengezela bebhadazela ay njengesidakwa, lenhlakanipho yabo yonke iginyiwe az. <b>28</b> Khona bakhala eNkosini ekuhluphekeni kwabo, yabakhupha ezinsizini zabo ba. <b>29</b> Yenza isiphepho bb sachuma, lamagagasi aso athula bc. <b>30</b> Basebethokoza ngoba ayesethule, yasibaholela ethekwini lesifiso sabo bd. 
<b>31</b> Kabayibonge iNkosingomusa wayo, langemisebenzi yayo emangalisayo ebantwaneni babantu be! <b>32</b> Njalo kabayiphakamise bf ebandleni labantu bg, bayidumise esihlalweni sabadala bh! <b>33</b> Yenza imifula ibe yinkangala, lemithombo yamanzi ibe ngumhlabathi owomileyo bi, <b>34</b> umhlabathi ovundileyo ube lugwadule, ngenxa yobubi babahlezi kuwo bj. <b>35</b> Iphendula inkangala bk ibe yisiziba samanzi bl , bm, lelizwe elomileyo libe yimithombo yamanzi bn. <b>36</b> Ihlalise khona abalambileyo, njalo bamise umuzi wokuhlala bo; 
<b>37</b> bahlanyele amasimu, bagxumeke izivini, ezithela izithelo zesivuno. <b>38</b> Njalo iyababusisa ukuze bande kakhulu, lezifuyo zabo kayizinciphisi bp. <b>39</b> Babuye banciphe, bathotshiswe ngocindezelo, ububi, losizi. <b>40</b> Ithela ukudelelwa phezu kweziphathamandla bq, ibazulisa enkangala, engelandlela br. <b>41</b> Kube kanti ihlalisa phezulu umyanga ekuhluphekeni bs, enze insapho zibe njengomhlambi bt. <b>42</b> Abalungileyo bazabona bathokoze bu, lobubi bonke buzavala umlomo wabo bv. 
<b>43</b> Loba ngubani ohlakaniphileyo uzaqaphelisa lezizinto; labo bazaqedisisa izisa zeNkosi bw. 
<b>Psalms 108</b><br><b>1</b> Inhliziyo yami iqinile, Nkulunkulu. Ngizahlabelela ngitshotshe, ngitsho udumo lwami a! <b>2</b> Vukani gubhu lwezintambo lechacho! Ngizavusa ukusa. <b>3</b> Ngizakudumisa, Nkosi, phakathi kwabantu, ngihlabele indumiso kuwe phakathi kwezizwe. <b>4</b> Ngoba umusa wakho mkhulu phezu kwamazulu b, leqiniso lakho lifika emayezini c. <b>5</b> Phakama, Nkulunkulu, ngaphezu kwamazulu, lobukhosi bakho phezu komhlaba wonke d. <b>6</b> Ukuze abathandekayo bakho bakhululwe; sindisa ngesandla sakho sokunene, ungiphendule e. 
<b>7</b> UNkulunkulu ukhulumile ebungcweleni bakhe. Ngizathaba, ngiyabe iShekema, ngilinganise isihotsha seSukothi. <b>8</b> Ngeyami iGileyadi, ngowami uManase, loEfrayimi ungamandla ekhanda lami, uJuda ungumnikumthetho wami. <b>9</b> UMowabi ungumganu wami wokugezela; phezu kukaEdoma ngizaphosela inyathela lami; phezu kweFilisti f ngizamemeza ngokunqoba. <b>10</b> Ngubani ozangisa emzini oqinileyo? Ngubani ozangiholela eEdoma g , h? <b>11</b> Kawusilahlanga yini, Nkulunkulu; kawuphumanga, Nkulunkulu, lamabutho ethu i? 
<b>12</b> Siphe usizo ekuhlupheni, ngoba luyize uncedo lomuntu. <b>13</b> KuNkulunkulu sizakwenza ngobuqhawe, ngoba yena uzanyathelela phansi izitha zethu. 
<b>Psalms 109</b><br><b>1</b> Nkulunkulu wendumiso yami a, ungathuli b. <b>2</b> Ngoba umlomo wokhohlakeleyo lomlomo wenkohliso uvulekile umelene lami; bakhulume lami ngolimi lwamanga. <b>3</b> Bangihanqa ngamazwi enzondo, balwa bemelene lami kungelasizatho c. <b>4</b> Esikhundleni sothando lwami bayizitha zami d, kodwa mina e ngisemkhulekweni f. <b>5</b> Basebengibuyisela okubi esikhundleni sokuhle, lenzondo esikhundleni sothando lwami g. <b>6</b> Umise omubi h phezu kwakhe i, loSathane j kame ngakwesokunene sakhe k , l. 
<b>7</b> Ekwahlulelweni kwakhe kaphume elecala m; lomkhuleko wakhe ube yisono n. <b>8</b> Insuku zakhe kazibe nlutshwana; omunye athathe isikhundla sakhe o. <b>9</b> Abantwana bakhe kabangabi loyise, lomkakhe abe ngumfelokazi p. <b>10</b> Abantwana bakhe kabazulazule q bephanza isinkwa sabo r, bacele bevela emanxiweni abo s. <b>11</b> Umebolekisi kabambe konke alakho t, labezizweni baphange izithelo zomsebenzi wakhe u. <b>12</b> Kungabi khona omelulela umusa v, futhi kungabi khona ozwela intandane zakhe w. 
<b>13</b> Inzalo yakhe kayiqunywe x, lebizo labo lesulwe esizukulwaneni esilandelayo y. <b>14</b> Ububi baboyise bukhunjulwe eNkosini z, lesono sikanina singesulwa aa. <b>15</b> Kabube phambi kweNkosi njalonjalo, ukuze aqume ukukhunjulwa kwabo kusuke emhlabeni ab. <b>16</b> Ngenxa yokuthi wayengakhumbuli ukwenza umusa, kodwa wazingela umuntu ohluphekayo longumyanga ac, lodabukileyo enhliziyweni ukumbulala ad. <b>17</b> Njengoba wathanda ukuqalekisa, ngakho kakwehlele kuye; njengoba wayengathandi ukubusisa, ngakho kakube khatshana laye ae. 
<b>18</b> Njengoba wagqoka isiqalekiso njengesigqoko sakhe af, ngakho kakungene emibilini yakhe njengamanzi, lanjengamafutha emathanjeni akhe ag. <b>19</b> Kakube kuye njengesembatho azembesa ngaso, kube libhanti azibopha ngalo njalonjalo. <b>20</b> Lokhu kube ngumvuzo wezitha zami ovela eNkosini, lowalabo abakhuluma okubi ngomphefumulo wami ah. <b>21</b> Kodwa wena, Jehova, Nkosi, ngenzela ngenxa yebizo lakho ai; ngoba umusa wakho ulungile, ngikhulule aj. <b>22</b> Ngoba mina ngiyisihlupheki lomyanga, lenhliziyo yami ilimele phakathi kwami ak. 
<b>23</b> Ngedlula njengesithunzi sisiba side al; ngizanyazanyiswe am njengesikhonyane an. <b>24</b> Amadolo ami ayaxega ngokuzila ukudla ao, lenyama yami isicakile iswela ukukhuluphala ap. <b>25</b> Mina sengibe lihlazo kubo aq, bengibona banikina ikhanda labo ar. <b>26</b> Ngisize, Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wami, ungisindise ngokomusa wakho as. <b>27</b> Ukuze bazi ukuthi lesi yisandla sakho; wena, Nkosi, ukwenzile. <b>28</b> Kabathuke bona, kodwa wena ubusise at; nxa bevuka kabayangeke, kodwa inceku yakho kayithabe au. 
<b>29</b> Izitha zami av , aw kazigqokiswe ngehlazo ax, kazizigoqele ay ngenhloni zazo njengesembatho az. <b>30</b> Ngizayibonga kakhulu iNkosi ngomlomo wami; njalo ngizayidumisa phakathi kwabanengi ba. <b>31</b> Ngoba izakuma ngakwesokunene komyanga, ukumsindisa kulabo abalahla umphefumulo wakhe bb. 
<b>Psalms 110</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi yathi eNkosini yami a: Hlala b ngakwesokunene sami c, ngize ngenze izitha zakho d zibe yisenabelo senyawo zakho e. <b>2</b> INkosi izathuma intonga yamandla akho f ivela eZiyoni g. Busa phakathi kwezitha zakho h. <b>3</b> Abantu bakho bazavuma ngosuku lwamandla akho i, ebuhleni bobungcwele j, kusukela esizalweni semadabukakusa kuza kuwe amazolo obutsha bakho k. <b>4</b> INkosi l ifungile m, kayiyikuphenduka n: Wena o , p ungumpristi q kuze kube nininini r ngokwendlela kaMelkizedeki s. 
<b>5</b> INkosi ngakwesokunene sakho t izachoboza u amakhosi v osukwini w lokuthukuthela kwayo x. <b>6</b> Izakwehlulela phakathi kwezizwe, kugcwale izidumbu; izachoboza inhloko phezu kwelizwe elikhulu y. <b>7</b> Izanatha esifuleni endleleni; ngakho-ke izaphakamisa ikhanda z. 
<b>Psalms 111</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Ngizayibonga iNkosi ngenhliziyo yonke a, emhlanganweni wabaqotho b lebandleni c. <b>2</b> Mikhulu imisebenzi yeNkosi, idingwa yilabo abathokoza ngayo d. <b>3</b> Ulodumo lenkazimulo umsebenzi wayo e , f, lokulunga kwayo kumi kuze kube nininini g. <b>4</b> Yenzile izimangaliso zayo zikhunjulwe; iNkosi ilomusa lesihawu h. <b>5</b> Iyabapha ukudla labo abayesabayo; iyakhumbula kuze kube phakade isivumelwano sayo i. <b>6</b> Yazisile abantu bayo amandla emisebenzi yayo, ngokubanika ilifa lezizwe. 
<b>7</b> Imisebenzi yezandla zayo iliqiniso lokwehlulela j; imithetho yayo yonke iqinisekile k. <b>8</b> Isekelwe kuze kube phakade laphakade l, yenziwa ngeqiniso langobuqotho m. <b>9</b> Yathumela uhlengo ebantwini bayo n , o; ilaye isivumelwano sayo kuze kube nininini; lingcwele p liyesabeka ibizo layo q , r. <b>10</b> Ukuyesaba iNkosi kuyikuqala kokuhlakanipha s; balokuqedisisa okuhle bonke abayenzayo imilayo t. Indumiso yayo imi kuze kube nininini u. 
<b>Psalms 112</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Ubusisiwe umuntu oyesabayo iNkosi a, othokoza kakhulu ngemithetho yayo b. <b>2</b> Inzalo yakhe izakuba lamandla emhlabeni; isizukulwana sabaqotho sizabusiswa c. <b>3</b> Imfuyo d lenotho kuzakuba sendlini yakhe e, lokulunga kwakhe kuzakuma kuze kube nininini f. <b>4</b> Kuyabaphumela abaqotho ukukhanya emnyameni g; ilomusa lesihawu h lokulunga i. <b>5</b> Umuntu olungileyo ngolesisa lowebolekisayo j, wenza izindaba zakhe k ngokulunga l. 
<b>6</b> Ngoba kayikunyikinywa phakade m; olungileyo uzakuba sekukhunjulweni kuze kube nininini n. <b>7</b> Kayikwesaba umbiko omubi o, inhliziyo yakhe iqinile p, ithemba eNkosini q. <b>8</b> Isekelwe r inhliziyo yakhe, kayikwesaba s, aze abone isiloyiso sakhe phezu kwezitha zakhe t. <b>9</b> Uhlakazile, uyabanika abayanga u , v; ukulunga kwakhe kumi kuze kube nininini w; uphondo lwakhe luzaphakanyiswa ngodumo x. <b>10</b> Omubi uyakubona, athukuthele; agedle amazinyo akhe y, ancibilike z; isifiso aa sababi ab sizabhubha ac. 
<b>Psalms 113</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Dumisani a, zinceku zeNkosi, dumisani ibizo leNkosi b , c. <b>2</b> Kalibongwe ibizo leNkosi kusukela khathesi njalo kuze kube nininini d. <b>3</b> Kusukela ekuphumeni kwelanga kuze kube sekutshoneni kwalo e ibizo leNkosi f kalidunyiswe g. <b>4</b> INkosi iphakeme phezu kwezizwe zonke h, ubukhosi bayo buphezu kwamazulu i. <b>5</b> Ngubani onjengeNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu, ehlezi phezulu j, <b>6</b> ekhothama ukukhangela emazulwini lemhlabeni k? 
<b>7</b> Iyamvusa umyanga ethulini, imphakamise osweleyo enqunjini yomquba, <b>8</b> ukuze l imhlalise leziphathamandla, kanye leziphathamandla zabantu bayo m. <b>9</b> Ihlalisa inyumba emzini n, unina wabantwana othokozayo. Dumisani iNkosi o! 
<b>Psalms 114</b><br><b>1</b> Ekuphumeni kukaIsrayeli eGibhithe a, indlu kaJakobe ebantwini bolimi lwezizwe b, <b>2</b> uJuda waba yindlu yakhe engcwele c, uIsrayeli waba ngumbuso wakhe d. <b>3</b> Ulwandle lwakubona lwabaleka e, iJordani yabuyela emuva f. <b>4</b> Intaba zaqolotsha njengezinqama g, amaqaqa njengamawundlu h. <b>5</b> Ulani wena lwandle, ukuthi ubaleke? Wena Jordani, ukuthi ubuyele emuva i? <b>6</b> Lina zintaba, ukuthi liqolotshe njengezinqama j? Lina maqaqa, njengamawundlu k? 
<b>7</b> Thuthumela, mhlaba, phambi kweNkosi, phambi kukaNkulunkulu kaJakobe l, <b>8</b> owaphendula idwala mlibe lichibi lamanzi n, ilitshe elilukhuni libe ngumthombo wamanzi o. 
<b>Psalms 115</b><br><b>1</b> Kakungabi kithi, Nkosi, kakungabi kithi a, kodwa ebizweni lakho nika udumo, ngenxa yomusa wakho, ngenxa yeqiniso lakho b. <b>2</b> Kungani izizwe zizakuthi: Ungaphi pho uNkulunkulu wabo c? <b>3</b> Kodwa uNkulunkulu wethu usemazulwini d, wenza konke akuthandayo e. <b>4</b> Izithombe zabo yisiliva legolide f, umsebenzi wezandla zomuntu g. <b>5</b> Zilomlomo, kodwa kazikhulumi h; zilamehlo, kodwa kaziboni i; <b>6</b> zilendlebe, kodwa kazizwa; zilempumulo, kodwa kazinuki j; 
<b>7</b> zilezandla, kodwa kaziphathi; inyawo zilazo, kodwa kazihambi; kazikhulumi ngomphimbo wazo k. <b>8</b> Abazenzayo kabafanane lazo, wonke othembela kuzo l. <b>9</b> Israyeli m, themba eNkosini n. Ilusizo lwabo o lesihlangu sabo p. <b>10</b> Ndlu kaAroni q, themba eNkosini r. Ilusizo lwabo s lesihlangu sabo t. <b>11</b> Lina elesaba iNkosi u , v, thembani eNkosini w. Ilusizo lwabo x lesihlangu sabo y. <b>12</b> INkosi isikhumbule, izabusisa, ibusise indlu kaIsrayeli z, ibusise indlu kaAroni aa; 
<b>13</b> izabusisa labo abayesabayo iNkosi, abancinyane kanye labakhulu ab , ac. <b>14</b> INkosi izalandisa, lina labantwana benu ad. <b>15</b> Libusisiwe eNkosini ae, eyenza amazulu lomhlaba af. <b>16</b> Amazulu, amazulu ngaweNkosi, kodwa umhlaba iwunike abantwana babantu ag. <b>17</b> Abafileyo ah kabayidumisi iNkosi ai, hatshi-ke loba ngubani owehlela ekuthuleni aj. <b>18</b> Kodwa thina ak sizayibonga iNkosi al kusukela khathesi kuze kube nininini. Dumisani iNkosi am! 
<b>Psalms 116</b><br><b>1</b> Ngiyayithanda iNkosi a, ngoba izwile b ilizwi lami, ukunxusa kwami c. <b>2</b> Ngoba ibeke indlebe yayo kimi, ngakho ngizayibiza ensukwini zami d. <b>3</b> Izibopho zokufa e zangihanqa f, lenhlungu zesihogo g zangibamba; ngathola inhlupheko losizi. <b>4</b> Ngasengibiza ibizo leNkosi, ngathi: O Nkosi, khulula umphefumulo wami h. <b>5</b> Ilomusa iNkosi i, ilungile j; ye, uNkulunkulu wethu ulesihawu k. <b>6</b> INkosi iyabalondoloza abathobekileyo l; ngehliselwa phansi, yasingisindisa m. 
<b>7</b> Buyela, mphefumulo wami, ekuphumuleni kwakho n, ngoba iNkosi ikuphethe ngomusa o. <b>8</b> Ngoba ikhulule umphefumulo wami ekufeni p, amehlo ami ezinyembezini, unyawo lwami ekuweni q. <b>9</b> Ngizahamba phambi kweNkosi emazweni abaphilayo r. <b>10</b> Ngakholwa, ngakho ngikhulumile. Mina ngahlupheka kakhulu s. <b>11</b> Mina ngathi ekuphangiseni kwami t: Wonke umuntu ungumqambimanga u. <b>12</b> Ngizabuyiselani eNkosini vngazo zonke inzuzo zayo kimi w? <b>13</b> Ngizathatha inkezo yosindiso x, ngibize ibizo leNkosi y. 
<b>14</b> Ngizakhokha izifungo zami eNkosini, khathesi phambi kwabantu bayo bonke z. <b>15</b> Kuligugu emehlweni eNkosi aa ukufa kwabangcwele bayo ab. <b>16</b> Hawu Nkosi, isibili ngiyinceku yakho ac, ngiyinceku yakho, indodana yencekukazi yakho ad; uthukulule izibopho zami ae. <b>17</b> Ngizakuhlabela umhlatshelo wokubonga af, ngibize ibizo leNkosi ag. <b>18</b> Ngizakhokha izifungo zami eNkosini, khathesi phambi kwabantu bayo bonke ah, <b>19</b> emagumeni endlu yeNkosi, phakathi kwakho, Jerusalema ai. Dumisani iNkosi aj! 
<b>Psalms 117</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi, lina zizwe zonke, liyibabaze, lina bantu lonke a. <b>2</b> Ngoba uthandolomusa wayo mkhulu kithi b, leqiniso leNkosilimi kuze kube nininini c. Dumisani iNkosi d! 
<b>Psalms 118</b><br><b>1</b> Bongani iNkosi, ngoba ilungile, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini a. <b>2</b> Katsho uIsrayeli khathesi ukuthi isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini b. <b>3</b> Kayitsho indlu kaAroni khathesi ukuthi isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini c. <b>4</b> Kabatsho khathesi d abayesabayo iNkosi ukuthi isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini e. <b>5</b> Ngisesizini ngabiza iNkosi f , g , h; iNkosi yangiphendula i yangibeka j endaweni ebanzi k. <b>6</b> INkosi ingakimi l, kangiyikwesaba; umuntu angenzani kimi m? 
<b>7</b> INkosi ingakimi labangisizayo n; ngakho mina ngizabona isiloyiso sami phezu kwabangizondayo o. <b>8</b> Kungcono ukuphephela eNkosini kulokuthembela emuntwini p. <b>9</b> Kungcono ukuphephela eNkosini q kulokuthembela kuziphathamandla r. <b>10</b> Zonke izizwe zangihanqa; kodwa ebizweni leNkosi ngizaziquma s. <b>11</b> Zangihanqa, yebo zangihanqa; kodwa ebizweni leNkosi ngizaziquma t. <b>12</b> Zangihanqa u njengenyosi v, zacitshwa njengomlilo wameva; kodwa ebizweni leNkosi ngizaziquma w. 
<b>13</b> Wangigudlumeza lokugudlumeza ngize ngiwe; kodwa iNkosi yangisiza x. <b>14</b> INkosi ingamandla lengoma yami y , z, isibe lusindiso lwami aa , ab. <b>15</b> Ilizwi lentokozo losindiso lisemathenteni abalungileyo; esokunene seNkosi ac senza ngamandla ad. <b>16</b> Esokunene seNkosi ae siphakanyisiwe, esokunene seNkosi senza ngamandla af. <b>17</b> Kangiyikufa, kodwa ngizaphila ag, ngilandise ah imisebenzi yeNkosi ai. <b>18</b> INkosi aj ingijezisile kabuhlungu, kodwa kayinginikelanga ekufeni ak.

<b>176</b> Ngiduhile ji njengemvu elahlekileyo jj; dinga inceku yakho, ngoba kangikhohlwa imithetho yakho jk. 
<b>Psalms 120</b><br><b>1</b> Ekuhluphekeni kwami ngakhala eNkosini, yangiphendula a. <b>2</b> Nkosi, khulula umphefumulo wami kundebe zamanga, kulimi lwenkohliso. <b>3</b> Uzanikwani, njalo kuzakwengezelelwani kuwe, limi lwenkohliso? <b>4</b> Imitshoko ebukhali b yeqhawe c, lamalahle avuthayo esihlahla serotemu* d! <b>5</b> Maye kimi, ngoba ngihlala njengowezizwe koMesheki e, ngihlala emathenteni eKedari f! <b>6</b> Umphefumulo wami sekukade wahlala laye ozonda ukuthula g. <b>7</b> Ngingowokuthula, kodwa lapho ngikhuluma, bangabempi h. 
<b>Psalms 121</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizaphakamisela amehlo ami a ezintabeni, lapho usizo lwami oluzavela khona b. <b>2</b> Usizo lwami luvela eNkosini c, eyenza amazulu lomhlaba d. <b>3</b> Kayiyikuvuma ukuthi unyawo lwakho lutshelele e; umlondolozi wakho f kayikuwozela g. <b>4</b> Khangela, umlondolozi kaIsrayeli h kayikuwozela njalo kayikulala i , j. <b>5</b> INkosi ingumlondolozi wakho k; iNkosi ingumthunzi wakho l esandleni sakho sokunene m. <b>6</b> Ilanga kaliyikukutshaya emini n, lenyanga ebusuku o. 
<b>7</b> INkosi izakulondoloza kukho konke okubi p; izalondoloza umphefumulo wakho q , r. <b>8</b> INkosi izalondoloza s ukuphuma kwakho lokungena kwakho, kusukela khathesi kuze kube phakade t. 
<b>Psalms 122</b><br><b>1</b> Ngathokoza lapho besithi kimi: Kasiye endlini yeNkosi a , b. <b>2</b> Inyawo zethu zimi emasangweni akho, Jerusalema. <b>3</b> IJerusalema yakhiwe c njengomuzi ohlangeneyo ngokwawo d, <b>4</b> lapho izizwe ezenyukela khona e, izizwe zeNkosi f, kubufakazi bukaIsrayeli, ukuze zibonge ibizo leNkosi g. <b>5</b> Ngoba kumiswe khona izihlalo zobukhosi zesahlulelo, izihlalo zobukhosi zendlu kaDavida h. <b>6</b> Khulekelani ukuthula kweJerusalema; bazaphumelela abakuthandayo i. 
<b>7</b> Kakube khona ukuthula phakathi kwemiduli yakho j, inhlalakahle ezigodlweni zakho k. <b>8</b> Ngenxa yabafowethu labangane bami sengizakuthi: Ukuthula kakube phakathi kwakho l. <b>9</b> Ngenxa yendlu yeNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu ngizakudingela okuhle m. 
<b>Psalms 123</b><br><b>1</b> Ngiyaphakamisela kuwe amehlo ami a, wena ohlezi emazulwini b. <b>2</b> Khangela, njengalokhu amehlo ezinceku engasesandleni senkosi yazo, njengamehlo encekukazi engasesandleni senkosikazi yayo, ngokunjalo amehlo ethu angaseNkosini uNkulunkulu wethu, ize ibe lesihawu kithi. <b>3</b> Woba lesihawu kithi, Nkosi c, woba lesihawu kithi d, ngoba sigcwele kakhulu e yikudelelwa f. <b>4</b> Umphefumulo wethu ugcwele kakhulu yikukloloda g kwabonwabileyo h, yikudelela kwabazigqajayo i. 
<b>Psalms 124</b><br><b>1</b> Uba kwakungesiNkosi eyayilathi a, katsho-ke uIsrayeli b; <b>2</b> uba kwakungesiNkosi eyayilathi, lapho abantu besivukela c, <b>3</b> ngabe basiginya d siphila e , f, ekuvutheni kolaka lwabo kithi; <b>4</b> ngabe amanzi g agijima phezu kwethu, lempophoma yedlula phezu komphefumulo wethu h; <b>5</b> ngabe amanzi aqubukayo edlula phezu komphefumulo wethu. <b>6</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, engasinikelanga ukuthi sibe yimpango emazinyweni abo. 
<b>7</b> Umphefumulo wethu uphunyukile njengenyoni emjibileni wabathiyi; umjibila uqamukile, thina sasesiphunyuka i. <b>8</b> Usizo lwethu lusebizweni leNkosi j eyenza amazulu lomhlaba k. 
<b>Psalms 125</b><br><b>1</b> Labo abathembela eNkosini banjengentaba iZiyoni aenganyikinyekiyo b, emi kuze kube phakade c. <b>2</b> Njengezintaba ziyiphahlile iJerusalema, ngokunjalo iNkosi iyabaphahla abantu bayo kusukela kulesisikhathi kuze kube nininini d. <b>3</b> Ngoba intonga yobukhosi yobubi e kayiyikuhlala f phezu kwesabelo sabalungileyo g, ukuze abalungileyo bangeluleli izandla zabo kokubi. <b>4</b> Nkosi, yenza okuhle kubo abalungileyo h lakubo abaqotho enhliziyweni zabo i. 
<b>5</b> Kodwa labo abaphambukela j ezindleleni zabo ezitshekileyo k iNkosi izabenza bahambe labenzi bobubi l. Ukuthula phezu kukaIsrayeli m! 
<b>Psalms 126</b><br><b>1</b> Lapho iNkosi ibuyisa abathunjiweyo beZiyoni a, saba njengabaphuphayo b. <b>2</b> Umlomo wethu wasugcwala uhleko, lolimi lwethu ukuhlabelela c. Basebesithi phakathi kwezizwe: INkosi ibenzele izinto ezinkulu d. <b>3</b> INkosi isenzele izinto ezinkulu; siyathokoza. <b>4</b> Buyisa, Nkosi, ukuthunjwa kwethu, njengezifula e zeningizimu f. <b>5</b> Abahlanyela ngezinyembezi bazavuna ngentokozo g. <b>6</b> Yena ohamba ahambe ekhala inyembezi, ephethe inhlanyelo yokuhlanyela, isibili uzabuya ethokoza, ethwele inyanda zakhe h. 
<b>Psalms 127</b><br><b>1</b> Uba iNkosi ingayakhi indlu, basebenzela ize kiyo abayakhayo. Uba iNkosi ingawulondolozi umuzi a, umlindi ulindela ize b. <b>2</b> Kuyize kini c ukuthi livuke ngovivi d, lihlale e kuze kube sebusuku f , g, lidle ukudla h kosizi i; ngokunjalo iyamnika j ubuthongo othandiweyo wayo k. <b>3</b> Khangela, abantwana bayilifa leNkosi l, isithelo sesizalo singumvuzo m. <b>4</b> Njengemitshoko esandleni seqhawe n, banjalo abantwana bobutsha o. 
<b>5</b> Ibusisiwe indoda egcwalise umxhaka wayo ngabo; kabayikuyangeka ngoba bazakhuluma lezitha p esangweni q. 
<b>Psalms 128</b><br><b>1</b> Ubusisiwe wonke oyesabayo iNkosi a , b, ohamba endleleni zayo c. <b>2</b> Ngoba uzakudla umsebenzi wezandla zakho d; uzathokoza, njalo kuzakuba kuhle kuwe e. <b>3</b> Umkakho uzakuba njengevini elithelayo f emaceleni endlu yakho g, abantwana bakho bazakuba njengamahlumela omhlwathi bezingelezele itafula lakho h. <b>4</b> Ngoba khangela, ngokunjalo izabusiswa indoda eyesabayo iNkosi. <b>5</b> INkosi izakubusisa i iseZiyoni, njalo ubone okuhle kweJerusalema insuku zonke zempilo yakho j , k, 
<b>6</b> yebo, uzabona abantwana babantwana bakho l. Ukuthula kakube phezu kukaIsrayeli m! 
<b>Psalms 129</b><br><b>1</b> Kanengi bangihluphile a kusukela ebutsheni bami b, katsho-ke uIsrayeli c, <b>2</b> kanengi bangihluphile kusukela ebutsheni bami d, kanti kabangehlulanga e. <b>3</b> Abalimi balima emhlana wami f, bayenza yaba mide imifolo yabo g. <b>4</b> INkosi ilungile, iqumile intambo zababi h. <b>5</b> Kabayangeke i babuyiselwe emuva bonke abazonda iZiyoni j. <b>6</b> Kababe njengotshani ephahleni k , l, obubuna bungakakhuli m, <b>7</b> ovunayo angagcwalisi ngabo isandla sakhe, lalowo obopha izithungo isifuba sakhe n. 
<b>8</b> Labadlulayo bangatsho ukuthi: Isibusiso seNkosi kasibe phezu kwenu o; siyalibusisa ebizweni leNkosi p. 
<b>Psalms 130</b><br><b>1</b> Ngisenzikini ngakhalela kuwe, Nkosi a. <b>2</b> Nkosi, zwana ilizwi lami, indlebe zakho zilalele b ilizwi lokuncenga kwami c. <b>3</b> Uba wena, Nkosi d, uqaphela iziphambeko e, Nkosi, ngubani ongema f , g? <b>4</b> Kodwa kuwe kulothethelelo h, ukuze wesatshwe i. <b>5</b> Ngiyilindele iNkosi j, umphefumulo wami ulindile k, lelizwini layo ngiyathemba l. <b>6</b> Umphefumulo wami ulindela iNkosi okwedlula abalindele m ukusa, belindele ukusa n. 
<b>7</b> UIsrayeli kathembele eNkosini o; ngoba eNkosini kulothandolomusa, njalo kuyo kulohlengo olunengi p. <b>8</b> Yona-ke izahlenga uIsrayeli kuzo zonke iziphambeko zakhe q. 
<b>Psalms 131</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, inhliziyo yami kayikhukhumali a, lamehlo ami kawaphakami b, njalo kangihambi ezintweni ezinkulu lezintweni ezimangalisayo kakhulu kimi c. <b>2</b> Sibili ngichumisile ngathulisa umphefumulo wami, njengolunyuliweyo kunina; umphefumulo wami unjengolunyuliweyo phakathi kwami d. <b>3</b> UIsrayeli kathembele eNkosini kusukela khathesi kuze kube laphakade e. 
<b>Psalms 132</b><br><b>1</b> Khumbula, Nkosi, uDavida, izinhlupheko zakhe zonke a, <b>2</b> ukuthi wafunga b eNkosini, wathembisa kuSomandla kaJakobe c: <b>3</b> Qotho, kangiyikungena ethenteni lendlu yami, qotho, kangiyikwenyukela ecansini lombheda wami; <b>4</b> qotho, kangiyikunika amehlo ami ubuthongo d, ukuwozela ezinkopheni zami e, <b>5</b> ngize ngiyitholele iNkosi indawo f, indawo zokuhlala uSomandla kaJakobe g. <b>6</b> Khangela, sakuzwa h eEfratha i, sakuthola emasimini j egusu k. 
<b>7</b> Sizangena ezindlini zayo zokuhlala l, sikhonze esenabelweni m senyawo zayo n. <b>8</b> Sukuma, Nkosi o, uye ekuphumuleni kwakho p, wena lomtshokotsho wamandla akho q! <b>9</b> Abapristi bakho kabembeswe ukulunga r, labangcwele bakho bathabe kakhulu s. <b>10</b> Ngenxa yenceku yakho uDavida ungafulathelisi t ubuso bogcotshiweyo wakho u. <b>11</b> INkosi v yafunga ngeqiniso kuDavida w, kayiyikubuyela emuva kilo x: Owesithelo sesizalo sakho ngizambeka esihlalweni sakho sobukhosi y. 
<b>12</b> Uba abantwana bakho begcina isivumelwano sami lezifakazelo zami engizabafundisa zona z, labantwana babo bazahlala esihlalweni sakho sobukhosi kuze kube nini lanini aa. <b>13</b> Ngoba iNkosi iyikhethile iZiyoni, iyifisile ibe yindawo yayo yokuhlala ab. <b>14</b> Le yindawo yami yokuphumula kuze kube nini lanini ac; lapha ngizahlala, ngoba ngiyifisile ad. <b>15</b> Ngizabusisa kakhulu ukudla kwayo ae, ngisuthise abayanga bayo ngesinkwa af. 
<b>16</b> Labapristi bayo ngizabembathisa usindiso ag, labangcwele bayo bazahlabelela kakhulu ngenjabulo ah , ai. <b>17</b> Lapho ngizahlumisa uphondo lukaDavida aj; ngimisele ogcotshiweyo wami ak isibane al. <b>18</b> Izitha zakhe ngizazembathisa ihlazo, kodwa phezu kwakhe uzakhazimula umqhele wakhe wobukhosi am. 
<b>Psalms 133</b><br><b>1</b> Khangela, kuhle kangakanani njalo kumnandi kangakanani ukuhlala kwabazalwane ndawonye ngitsho babe munye a. <b>2</b> Kufanana lamafutha amahle b phezu kwekhanda c, ehlela endevini, indevu zikaAroni, ehlele emphethweni wezembatho zakhe d, <b>3</b> njengamazolo eHermoni e, ehlela entabeni f zeZiyoni g; ngoba lapho iNkosi yalaya isibusiso, impilo kuze kube phakade h. 
<b>Psalms 134</b><br><b>1</b> Khangelani, libonge iNkosi lina lonke zinceku zeNkosi a, elimi endlini yeNkosi b , c ebusuku d. <b>2</b> Phakamiselani izandla zenu e endaweni engcwele, libonge iNkosi f. <b>3</b> INkosi eyenza amazulu lomhlaba g kayilibusise iseZiyoni h. 
<b>Psalms 135</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi a! Dumisani ibizo leNkosi b, dumisani lina zinceku zeNkosi c , d, <b>2</b> elimi endlini yeNkosi e, emagumeni endlu kaNkulunkulu wethu f. <b>3</b> Dumisani iNkosi, ngoba iNkosi ilungile g; hlabelelani indumiso ebizweni layo, ngoba kumnandi h. <b>4</b> Ngoba iNkosi i izikhethele uJakobe j, uIsrayeli abe ngokuligugu kwakhe okukhethekileyo k. <b>5</b> Ngoba mina ngiyazi ukuthi iNkosi inkulu, ukuthi iNkosi yethu iphezu kwabo bonke onkulunkulu l. 
<b>6</b> Konke iNkosi ekuthandayo iyakwenza, emazulwini lemhlabeni, ezinlwandle lenzikini zonke m. <b>7</b> Yenza izinkungu zenyuke emikhawulweni yomhlaba n , o, yenzela izulu imibane p , q , r, ikhupha umoya s eziphaleni zayo t. <b>8</b> Eyatshaya izibulo leGibhithe, kusukela emuntwini kuze kube senyamazaneni u. <b>9</b> Yathumela izibonakaliso lezimangaliso phakathi kwakho, Gibhithe v, phezu kukaFaro laphezu kwenceku zakhe zonke w. <b>10</b> Eyatshaya izizwe ezinengi, yabulala amakhosi alamandla x, 
<b>11</b> uSihoni inkosi yamaAmori y, loOgi inkosi yeBashani z, layo yonke imibuso yeKhanani aa, <b>12</b> yanikela ilizwe labo laba yilifa, ilifa likaIsrayeli, isizwe sayo ab. <b>13</b> Nkosi, ibizo lakho limi kuze kube phakade ac, ukukhunjulwa kwakho, Nkosi, kusizukulwana ngesizukulwana ad , ae. <b>14</b> Ngoba iNkosi izakwehlulela abantu bayo af, izazisola mayelana lezinceku zayo ag , ah. <b>15</b> Izithombe zezizwe ziyisiliva legolide, umsebenzi wezandla zomuntu ai. <b>16</b> Zilemilomo, kodwa kazikhulumi; zilamehlo, kodwa kaziboni; 
<b>17</b> zilendlebe, kodwa kazizwa; lomoya kawukho emlonyeni wazo aj. <b>18</b> Abazenzayo bafanana lazo, laye wonke othembela kuzo. <b>19</b> Wena ndlu kaIsrayeli, bongani iNkosi; wena ndlu kaAroni, bongani iNkosi ak; <b>20</b> wena ndlu kaLevi, bongani iNkosi al. Lina eliyesabayo iNkosi, bongani iNkosi am. <b>21</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi iseZiyoni an, ehlala eJerusalema ao. Dumisani iNkosi ap! 
<b>Psalms 136</b><br><b>1</b> Bongani iNkosi a ngoba ilungile b, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini c. <b>2</b> Bongani uNkulunkulu wabonkulunkulu, ngoba isihawu sakhe simi kuze kube nininini d. <b>3</b> Bongani iNkosi yamakhosi, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini e. <b>4</b> Yona yodwa eyenza izimangaliso ezinkulu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini f; <b>5</b> eyenza amazulu g ngokuhlakanipha h, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; <b>6</b> eyendlala umhlaba i phezu kwamanzi j, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; 
<b>7</b> eyenza izikhanyiso ezinkulu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini k; <b>8</b> ilanga ukubusa emini, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini l; <b>9</b> inyanga lezinkanyezi ukubusa ebusuku, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini m. <b>10</b> Yona eyatshaya amaGibhithe kumazibulo awo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini n; <b>11</b> yakhupha uIsrayeli phakathi kwabo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini o; <b>12</b> ngesandla esilamandla langengalo eyeluliweyo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini p. 
<b>13</b> Yona eyehlukanisa q uLwandle oluBomvu r lwaba yiziqamu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini s; <b>14</b> yenza uIsrayeli adabule phakathi kwalo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini t; <b>15</b> kodwa yathintithela uFaro lebutho lakhe oLwandle oluBomvu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini u. <b>16</b> Yona eyakhokhela abantu bayo enkangala, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini v. <b>17</b> Yona eyatshaya amakhosi amakhulu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini w; 
<b>18</b> yabulala amakhosi adumileyo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; <b>19</b> uSihoni inkosi yamaAmori, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; <b>20</b> loOgi inkosi yeBashani, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; <b>21</b> yanika ilizwe labo laba yilifa, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; <b>22</b> ilifa kuIsrayeli inceku yayo, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini x. <b>23</b> Eyasikhumbula y ebuphansini bethu z, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini; 
<b>24</b> yasophula ezitheni zethu, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini aa. <b>25</b> Enika yonke inyama ukudla, ngoba isihawu sayo simi kuze kube nininini ab. <b>26</b> Bongani uNkulunkulu wamazulu, ngoba isihawu sakhe simi kuze kube nininini ac. 
<b>Psalms 137</b><br><b>1</b> Emifuleni yeBhabhiloni sahlala phansi khona, yebo sakhala inyembezi, lapho sikhumbula iZiyoni. <b>2</b> Amachacho ethu sawaphanyeka eminyezaneni phakathi kwayo a. <b>3</b> Ngoba khonapho abasithumbayo basicela amazwi engoma, labasimotshayo bacela injabulo besithi: Sihlabeleleni enye yezingoma zeZiyoni. <b>4</b> Singahlabela njani ingoma yeNkosi elizweni lemzini b? <b>5</b> Uba ngikukhohlwa, Jerusalema, isandla sami sokunene kasikhohlwe ubungcitshi c. 
<b>6</b> Ulimi lwami kalunamathele elwangeni lwami, uba ngingakukhumbuli, uba ngingaphakamisi iJerusalema phezu kwentokozo yami enkulu d. <b>7</b> Khumbula, Nkosi, ngabantwana bakoEdoma, ngosuku lweJerusalema, abathi: Dilizani, dilizani ▼▼ dilizani: Heb embulani., kuze kube sesisekelweni sayo f. <b>8</b> Ndodakazi yeBhabhiloni, ozabhujiswa g, ubusisiwe ozaphindisela kuwe isenzo osenze kithi h , i. <b>9</b> Ubusisiwe ozabamba asakaze abantwana bakho edwaleni j. 
<b>Psalms 138</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizakubonga ngenhliziyo yami yonke; phambi kwabonkulunkulu ngizakuhlabelela indumiso a. <b>2</b> Ngizakhonza ngikhangele b ngasethempelini lakho elingcwele c, ngidumise ibizo lakho ngenxa yothando lwakho langenxa yobuqotho bakho; ngoba ukhulisile d ilizwi lakho phezu kwalo lonke ibizo lakho e. <b>3</b> Ngosuku engakhala ngalo wangiphendula, wangiqinisa ngamandla emphefumulweni wami. <b>4</b> Wonke amakhosi omhlaba azakudumisa, Nkosi, ngoba azwile amazwi omlomo wakho f. 
<b>5</b> Yebo, azahlabelela ngezindlela zeNkosi, ngoba lukhulu udumo lweNkosi. <b>6</b> Loba iNkosi iphakeme g, kube kanti iyabona othobekileyo h , i; kodwa ozigqajayo iyamazi ekhatshana j. <b>7</b> Loba ngihamba phakathi kwenhlupheko k, uzangiphilisa l; uzakwelula isandla sakho umelana lolaka lezitha zami, lesandla sakho sokunene sizangisindisa m. <b>8</b> INkosi izaphelelisa okumayelana lami n; uthando lwakho, Nkosi, lumi kuze kube nininini o. Ungatshiyi imisebenzi yezandla zakho p. 
<b>Psalms 139</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, ungihlolile, uyangazi a. <b>2</b> Wena uyakwazi ukuhlala kwami b , c lokusukuma kwami d , e; uyaqedisisa f imicabango yami ukhatshana g. <b>3</b> Uyahlungula ukuhamba kwami lokulala kwami h, uzejwayele indlela zami zonke i. <b>4</b> Ngoba kalikho ilizwi elimini lwami, khangela Nkosi, usulazi lonke j. <b>5</b> Ungizingelezile emuva langaphambili k, ubeke isandla sakho phezu kwami l. <b>6</b> Lolulwazi m lumangalisa kakhulu kimi; luphakeme, kangilakho ukufinyelela kulo n. 
<b>7</b> Ngizakuya ngaphi ngisuke kuMoya wakho o? Kumbe ngizabalekela ngaphi ngisuke ebusweni bakho p? <b>8</b> Uba ngisenyukela emazulwini, lapho ukhona q; uba ngisendlala icansi lami esihogweni, khangela, ukhona r , s. <b>9</b> Uba ngithatha amaphiko okusa, ngihlale emikhawulweni yolwandle t, <b>10</b> lalapho isandla sakho sizangikhokhela, lesokunene sakho singibambe u. <b>11</b> Uba ngisithi: Isibili umnyama uzangifihla, ngitsho ubusuku buzakuba yikukhanya kimi inhlangothi zonke v. 
<b>12</b> Ngitsho umnyama kawufihli kuwe, kodwa ubusuku bukhanya njengemini; njengomnyama kunjalo ukukhanya kuwe w. <b>13</b> Ngoba wena ungumnini wezinso zami x, wangeluka esiswini sikamama y. <b>14</b> Ngizakudumisa, ngenxa yokuthi ngenziwe ngokwesabekayo, ngokumangalisayo. Imisebenzi yakho iyamangalisa; lomphefumulo wami uyakwazi kuhle kakhulu z. <b>15</b> Amathambo ami ayengafihlekanga kuwe, lapho ngangisenziwa ekusithekeni aa, ngenziwa ngobuciko ezindaweni ezizikileyo zomhlaba ab , ac. 
<b>16</b> Amehlo akho abona ibumba lami; legwalweni lwakho zonke lezi zabhalwa, izinsuku okwabunjwa ngazo, kungakabikho lakunye kwakho ad. <b>17</b> Iligugu kangakanani-ke ae kimi imicabango yakho, O Nkulunkulu af; bukhulu kangakanani ubunengi bayo ag! <b>18</b> Uba ngingayibala ah, minengi kuletshebetshebe ai. Lapho ngivuka, ngilokhu ngilawe. <b>19</b> Isibili aj uzambulala omubi, Nkulunkulu ak! Ngakho lina bantu abomele igazi al sukani kimi am; <b>20</b> abakhuluma ngenkohliso ngawe an, ziphatha ngeze ibizo lakho ao, izitha zakho ap. 
<b>21</b> Kangibazondi yini, Nkosi, abakuzondayo aq, nginengwe ar ngabakuvukelayo as? <b>22</b> Ngiyabazonda ngenzondo epheleleyo; sebeyizitha kimi. <b>23</b> Ngihlola, Nkulunkulu, uyazi inhliziyo yami; ngilinga at, uyazi imicabango yami au, <b>24</b> ubone ukuthi ikhona indlela embi kimi yini av, ungikhokhele aw endleleni ax ephakade ay. 
<b>Psalms 140</b><br><b>1</b> Ngikhulula, Nkosi a, emuntwini omubi b, ungilondoloze emuntwini olesihluku c, <b>2</b> abaceba izinto ezimbi enhliziyweni, usuku lonke babuthanela izimpi d. <b>3</b> Balola ulimi lwabo e njengenyoka f, ubuhlungu g bebululu h bungaphansi kwendebe zabo. Sela* i. <b>4</b> Ngilondoloze, Nkosi, ezandleni zomubi, ungivikele emuntwini olesihluku j; abaceba ukuwisa inyawo zami k. <b>5</b> Abazigqajayo bangifihlele l umjibila m, lezintambo n, bendlala imbule eceleni kwendlela o; bangibekela izithiyo. Sela* p. 
<b>6</b> Ngithe eNkosini: Wena unguNkulunkulu wami q. Beka indlebe elizwini lokuncenga kwami, Nkosi r. <b>7</b> Jehova, Nkosi, mandla osindiso lwami s, wembese ikhanda lami t osukwini lwezikhali u. <b>8</b> Unganiki, Nkosi, izifiso zomubi, ungaphumelelisi icebo lakhe elibi v, hlezi baziphakamise. Sela* w. <b>9</b> Ngekhanda labangihanqileyo, ububi bendebe zabo kabubembese x. <b>10</b> Kakuthi amalahle atshisayo akhithikele phezu kwabo, ubawisela emlilweni, emigodini etshonayo, ukuze bangavuki y. 
<b>11</b> Umuntu wolimi kangamiswa elizweni; umuntu olesihluku, ububi buzamzingela kube yikuwiswa kwakhe. <b>12</b> Ngiyazi ukuthi iNkosi izasebenza udaba lohluphekayo z, ilungelo labayanga aa , ab. <b>13</b> Isibili abalungileyo bazalidumisa ibizo lakho; abaqotho bazahlala ebusweni bakho ac. 
<b>Psalms 141</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, ngiyakhala kuwe; phangisa uze kimi; beka indlebe elizwini lami lapho ngikhala kuwe a. <b>2</b> Umkhuleko wami kawumiswe njengempepha b phambi kwakho c, ukuphakanyiswa kwezandla zami d njengomnikelo wakusihlwa e. <b>3</b> Nkosi, bekela umlomo wami umlindi f, gcina umnyango wendebe zami g. <b>4</b> Ungaphenduleli inhliziyo yami entweni embi h, ukuthi ngenze izenzo ezimbi kanye labantu abenza ububi i, ngingadli ezibilibocweni zabo j. 
<b>5</b> Olungileyo kangitshaye ethandwenilomusa k, angisole; kuzakuba ngamafutha ekhanda, ikhanda lami kalingawali, ngoba lomkhuleko wami usezakuba sezinhluphekweni zabo l. <b>6</b> Abahluleli babo baphoselwa emaceleni edwala m, bazakuzwa amazwi ami ngoba amnandi n. <b>7</b> Amathambo ethu ahlakazekile o emlonyeni wengcwaba njengobandayo adabule emhlabathini p. <b>8</b> Kodwa amehlo ami akuwe, Jehova, Nkosi q; ngithembele kuwe; ungawuchayi umphefumulo wami r. <b>9</b> Ngigcina emandleni omjibila abangibekele wona s, lezithiyo t zabenzi bobubi u. 
<b>10</b> Ababi kabawele emambuleni abo kanye, ngize ngidlule mina v. 
<b>Psalms 142</b><br><b>1</b> Ngakhalela eNkosini ngelizwi lami a, ngelizwi lami eNkosini ngancenga b. <b>2</b> Ngathulula c isikhalazo sami phambi kwayo, ngatshengisa ukuhlupheka kwami phambi kwayo d. <b>3</b> Lapho umoya wami uphela amandla phakathi kwami, wena wayazi indlela yami e. Endleleni engihamba ngayo bangifihlele umjibila f. <b>4</b> Khangela g ngakwesokunene, ubone h, kodwa wayengekho ongaziyo; isiphephelo sangehlula, kakho owakhathalela umphefumulo wami i. 
<b>5</b> Ngakhalela kuwe, Nkosi, ngathi j: Wena uyisiphephelo sami k, isabelo sami l elizweni labaphilayo m. <b>6</b> Lalela ukukhala kwami n, ngoba ngehliselwe phansi kakhulu o. Ngikhulula kwabangizingelayo, ngoba balamandla kulami p. <b>7</b> Khupha umphefumulo wami entolongweni, ukuze ngidumise ibizo lakho q. Abalungileyo bazangibuthanela, ngoba uzangiphatha kuhle kakhulu r. 
<b>Psalms 143</b><br><b>1</b> Nkosi, zwana umkhuleko wami, ubeke indlebe ekuncengeni kwami a. Ungiphendule ngothembeko lwakho b, ngokulunga kwakho c. <b>2</b> Njalo ungangeni kusahlulelo lenceku yakho d, ngoba phambi kobuso bakho kakho ophilayo ozalungisiswa e. <b>3</b> Ngoba isitha sizingele umphefumulo wami, sacindezelela impilo yami emhlabathini, sangenza ngahlala emnyameni omkhulu, njengabakade bafa f. <b>4</b> Ngakho umoya wami uphela amandla phakathi kwami g, lenhliziyo yami ithithibele phakathi kwami h. 
<b>5</b> Ngikhumbula i insuku zekadeni j, ngizindla ngazo zonke izenzo zakho k , l, nginakane ngomsebenzi wezandla zakho m. <b>6</b> Ngelulela izandla zami kuwe n; umphefumulo wami o womele wena p njengomhlaba owomileyo. Sela* q. <b>7</b> Phangisa ungiphendule, Nkosi r; umoya wami uyaphela s. Ungangifihleli ubuso bakho t , u, hlezi ngibe njengalabo abehlela emgodini v. <b>8</b> Ngizwisa uthandolomusa wakho ekuseni w, ngoba ngiyathemba kuwe x. Ungazise indlela engingahamba ngayo y, ngoba ngiyaphakamisela kuwe umphefumulo wami z. 
<b>9</b> Ngikhulula ezitheni zami, Nkosi; ngizifihlile kuwe aa. <b>10</b> Ngifundisa ukwenza ab intando yakho, ngoba wena unguNkulunkulu wami ac. UMoya wakho muhle ad, ngikhokhelele ae elizweni lokuqonda af. <b>11</b> Ngenxa yebizo lakho ag, Nkosi, ngiphilisa ah. Ngokulunga kwakho khupha umphefumulo wami ekuhluphekeni ai. <b>12</b> Langomusa wakho chitha izitha zami aj, ubhubhise bonke abahlupha umphefumulo wami, ngoba ngiyinceku yakho ak. 
<b>Psalms 144</b><br><b>1</b> Kayibongwe iNkosi, idwala lami, efundisela izandla zami impi a, iminwe yami ukulwa b; <b>2</b> umusa wami c, lenqaba yami d, umphotshongo wami ophakemeyo, lomkhululi wami e, isihlangu sami, lengiphephela kuyo f, ethobisa abantu bami ngaphansi kwami g. <b>3</b> Nkosi, umuntu uyini h ukuthi umazi? Indodana yomuntu ukuthi uyinanze i? <b>4</b> Umuntu unjengeze j, insuku zakhe zinjengesithunzi esidlulayo k. <b>5</b> Nkosi, khothamisa amazulu akho, wehle l; thinta izintaba ukuze zithunqe m. 
<b>6</b> Phazimisa umbane, ubachithe n, uthumele imitshoko yakho, ubahlakaze o. <b>7</b> Yelula isandla sakho p uphezulu q, ungikhulule, ungophule r emanzini amanengi s, esandleni sabantwana besizwe t, <b>8</b> abamlomo wabo ukhuluma amanga u, lesandla sabo sokunene singesokunene senkohliso v. <b>9</b> Ngizahlabelela kuwe ingoma entsha, Nkulunkulu w; ngizahlabelela indumiso kuwe ngogubhu x olulentambo ezilitshumi y , z. <b>10</b> Wena onika amakhosi usindiso ▼▼ usindiso: kumbe, ukunqoba., okhulula uDavida inceku yakho enkembeni embi ab. 
<b>11</b> Ngophula, ungikhulule esandleni sabantwana besizwe ac, abamlomo wabo ukhuluma amanga, lesandla sabo sokunene singesokunene senkohliso ad. <b>12</b> Ukuthi amadodana ethu abe njengezilimo ezikhula ebutsheni bazo ae, amadodakazi ethu njengamatshe engonsi abazwe njengomfanekiso wesigodlo af! <b>13</b> Ukuthi iziphala zethu zigcwale ziveze uhlobo ngohlobo ag; izimvu zethu zizale izinkulungwane, yebo, izinkulungwane ezilitshumi emadlelweni ethu ah! 
<b>14</b> Ukuthi inkabi zethu zingathwaliswa, kungabi lokufohlela phakathi ai, njalo kungabi lokuphuma aj, kumbe kungabi lesililo ezitaladeni zethu ak! <b>15</b> Bayathaba abantu okunjalo kubo al; bayathaba abantu abaNkulunkulu wabo yiNkosi am. 
<b>Psalms 145</b><br><b>1</b> Ngizakuphakamisa, Nkulunkulu wami, Nkosi, ngibonge ibizo lakho kuze kube nini lanini a. <b>2</b> Ngensuku zonke ngizakubonga, ngidumise ibizo lakho kuze kube nini lanini. <b>3</b> Yinkulu iNkosi, ifanele ukudunyiswa kakhulu, lobukhulu bayo kabuhloleki b. <b>4</b> Isizukulwana ngesizukulwana sizababaza imisebenzi yakho c, sitshumayele izenzo zakho ezilamandla d. <b>5</b> Ngizakhuluma e ngodumo lwakho olukhazimulayo lobukhosi bakho, langendaba zezimangaliso zakho f. 
<b>6</b> Njalo bazakhuluma ngamandla ezenzo zakho ezesabekayo, lobukhulu bakho ngibulandise. <b>7</b> Bazathulula ngamazwi isikhumbuzo g sokulunga kwakho okukhulu, bahlabele ngentokozo ngokulunga kwakho h. <b>8</b> Ilomusa lesihawu iNkosi, iyaphuza ukuthukuthela, inkulu ngothando i. <b>9</b> INkosi ilungile kubo bonke, lezihawu zayo ziphezu kwayo yonke imisebenzi yayo j. <b>10</b> Imisebenzi yakho yonke izakudumisa, Nkosi k, labangcwele bakho bazakubusisa l. <b>11</b> Bazakhuluma ngenkazimulo yombuso wakho, balandise ngamandla akho. 
<b>12</b> Ukuze bazise abantwana babantu m izenzo zayo ezilamandla n, lenkazimulo yobukhosi bombuso wayo o. <b>13</b> Umbuso wakho ungumbuso wamaphakade wonke p, lokubusa kwakho kumi kuso sonke isizukulwana ngesizukulwana q. <b>14</b> INkosi isekela bonke abawayo r, iphakamise bonke s abakhothemeyo t , u. <b>15</b> Amehlo abo bonke alindele kuwe, wena-ke uyabanika ukudla kwabo v ngesikhathi sakho w. <b>16</b> Uyavula isandla sakho x, usuthise isifiso sakho konke okuphilayo y. 
<b>17</b> INkosi ilungile endleleni zayo zonke z, njalo ilomusa emisebenzini yayo yonke aa. <b>18</b> INkosi iseduze labo bonke abayibizayo ab, labo bonke abayibiza ngeqiniso ac. <b>19</b> Yenza isifiso sabayesabayo ad, iyezwa ukukhala kwabo, ibasindise ae. <b>20</b> INkosi iyabalondoloza bonke af abayithandayo ag; kodwa bonke ababi izababhubhisa. <b>21</b> Umlomo wami uzakhuluma indumiso yeNkosi, layo yonke inyama ibonge ibizo layo ah elingcwele kuze kube nini lanini ai. 
<b>Psalms 146</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Dumisa iNkosi, mphefumulo wami a. <b>2</b> Ngizayidumisa iNkosi ngisaphila b; ngizahlabelela indumiso kuNkulunkulu wami ngisesekhona c. <b>3</b> Lingabeki ithemba lenu eziphathamandleni d, endodaneni yomuntu okungekho kuyo usindiso e. <b>4</b> Umoya wakhe uyaphuma, uyabuyela emhlabathini wakhe f; ngalolosuku amacebo akhe ayaphela g. <b>5</b> Ubusisiwe yena oloNkulunkulu kaJakobe h ukuthi abe lusizo lwakhe i, othemba lakhe liseNkosini uNkulunkulu wakhe j, 
<b>6</b> owenza amazulu, lomhlaba k , l, ulwandle, lakho konke okukukho, ogcina iqiniso kuze kube nininini m, <b>7</b> owenzela abacindezelweyo isahlulelo n, obaphayo ukudla abalambileyo o. INkosi p iyakhulula izibotshwa q, <b>8</b> INkosi iyavula amehlo eziphofu r; iNkosi iyabalulamisa abakhothemeyo s , t; iNkosi iyabathanda abalungileyo u. <b>9</b> INkosi ilondoloza abemzini v, isekele intandane lomfelokazi w , x , y; kodwa indlela yababi z iyayigenqula aa. 
<b>10</b> INkosi izabusa kuze kube nininini ab, uNkulunkulu wakho, Ziyoni, kuze kube sesizukulwaneni ngesizukulwana ac. Dumisani iNkosi ad! 
<b>Psalms 147</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi a, ngoba kuhle ukuhlabelela indumiso kuNkulunkulu wethu b, ngoba kumnandi c, indumiso ifanele d. <b>2</b> INkosi iyayakha iJerusalema e, iqoqe f abahlakaziweyo bakoIsrayeli g. <b>3</b> Yelapha h abadabukileyo enhliziyweni i, ibophe izinsizi zabo j. <b>4</b> Ibala inani lezinkanyezi k, izibize zonke ngamabizo l. <b>5</b> Yinkulu iNkosi yethu m, njalo ngeyamandla amakhulu n; ukuqedisisa kwayo kakulakunaniswa o. <b>6</b> INkosi p iyaphakamisa abamnene, iyabawisela emhlabathini ababi q. 
<b>7</b> Hlabelelani eNkosini ngokubonga r; lihlabele indumiso kuNkulunkulu wethu ngechacho s. <b>8</b> Eyembesa amazulu ngamayezi, elungisela umhlaba izulu t, ekhulisa utshani ezintabeni u, <b>9</b> enika inyamazana ukudla kwazo v, amaphuphu ewabayi akhalayo w. <b>10</b> Kayithokozi ngamandla ebhiza, kayijabuleli imilenze yomuntu x. <b>11</b> INkosi ijabulela abayesabayo y, labo abathembela emuseni wayo z. <b>12</b> Babaza iNkosi, Jerusalema. Dumisa uNkulunkulu wakho, Ziyoni. 
<b>13</b> Ngoba iqinise imigoqo yamasango akho; ibusise abantwana bakho phakathi kwakho aa. <b>14</b> Iyenza ukuthula emingceleni yakho ab; ikusuthise ngamanono engqoloyi ac. <b>15</b> Iyathumela umthetho wayo emhlabeni; ilizwi layo ligijima ngesiqubu esikhulu ad. <b>16</b> Enika iliqhwa elikhithikileyo njengoboya bezimvu ae; ihaze af ungqwaqwane njengomlotha ag. <b>17</b> Iyaphosa isiqhotho sayo njengezigaqa ah , ai; ngubani ongema phambi komqando wayo aj? <b>18</b> Iyathumela ilizwi layo, ikuncibilikise; ivunguzise umoya wayo, amanzi ageleze ak. 
<b>19</b> Iyamemezela ilizwi layo kuJakobe al, izimiso zayo lezahlulelo zayo kuIsrayeli am. <b>20</b> Kayenzanga njalo lakusiphi isizwe an; lezahlulelo zayo kazizazi ao. Dumisani iNkosi ap! 
<b>Psalms 148</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Dumisani iNkosi lisemazulwini a, idumiseni endaweni eziphezulu b. <b>2</b> Idumiseni lonke zingilosi zayo c. Idumiseni lonke mabandla ayo d. <b>3</b> Idumiseni langa lenyanga. Idumiseni lonke zinkanyezi ezikhanyayo. <b>4</b> Idumiseni mazulu amazulu e, lani manzi aphezu kwamazulu f. <b>5</b> Kakudumise ibizo leNkosi g; ngoba yona yalaya, kwasekudalwa h. <b>6</b> Yakumisa kuze kube nini lanini i; yakhupha isimiso esingayikwedlula j. 
<b>7</b> Dumisani iNkosi lisemhlabeni k, migobho yolwandle, lani zinziki zonke l, <b>8</b> mlilo m lesiqhotho, liqhwa elikhithikileyo lenkungu n, siphepho o esenza ilizwi layo p; <b>9</b> zintaba lamaqaqa wonke q, zihlahla zezithelo r , s lemisedari yonke, <b>10</b> zinyamazana zeganga t lezifuyo zonke u, zinto ezihuquzelayo lenyoni eziphaphayo ▼▼ eziphaphayo: Heb ezilempiko.. <b>11</b> Makhosi omhlaba, lani zizwe zonke, ziphathamandla, lani babusi bomhlaba w, <b>12</b> majaha, lani-ke zintombi, maxhegu labatsha x. 
<b>13</b> Kabadumise ibizo leNkosi y, ngoba ibizo layo lodwa liphakeme z; ubukhosi bayo buphezu komhlaba lamazulu aa. <b>14</b> Njalo iphakamisile uphondo lwabantu bayo ab, indumiso yabo bonke ac abangcwele bayo ad, eyabantwana bakoIsrayeli, abantu abaseduze layo ae. Dumisani iNkosi af! 
<b>Psalms 149</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! HIabelelani iNkosi ingoma entsha a, indumiso yayo ebandleni b labangcwele c. <b>2</b> UIsrayeli kathokoze d ngoMenzi wakhe e, abantwana beZiyoni kabathabe eNkosini yabo f. <b>3</b> Kabadumise ibizo layo g ngokusina h , i, kabayihlabelele indumiso ngesigujana j lechacho k. <b>4</b> Ngoba iNkosi iyathokoza ngabantu bayo l; izacecisa abamnene m ngosindiso n. <b>5</b> Abangcwele kabathokoze ngendumiso o, kabahlabelele p , q ngenjabulo emibhedeni yabo r. 
<b>6</b> Indumiso zikaNkulunkulu zisemphinjeni wabo s, lenkemba esika nhlangothi mbili t esandleni sabo u, <b>7</b> ukuze benze impindiselo phezu kwabahedeni, izijeziso phezu kwezizwe, <b>8</b> ukuze babophe amakhosi azo ngamaketane v, lezikhulu zazo w ngezibopho zensimbi x, <b>9</b> ukuze benze isahlulelo y esibhaliweyo phezu kwazo z. Loludumo aa abangcwele bayo bonke balalo ab. Dumisani iNkosi ac! 
<b>Psalms 150</b><br><b>1</b> Dumisani iNkosi! Dumisani uNkulunkulu endlini yakhe engcwele a; mdumiseni emkhathini wamandla akhe b. <b>2</b> Mdumiseni ngenxa yezenzo zakhe ezilamandla c , d, limdumise ngenxa yobunengi bobukhulu bakhe e. <b>3</b> Mdumiseni ngokuvuthelwa kophondo, limdumise f ngogubhu lwezintambo g lechacho h, <b>4</b> limdumise i ngesigujana langokusina j, limdumise ngezinto zokuhlabelela ezilentambo k langomhlanga l, <b>5</b> limdumise ngensimbi ezincencethayo ezihlokomayo m, limdumise ngensimbi ezincencethayo ezikhala kakhulu n. 
<b>6</b> Konke okuphefumulayo o kakudumise iNkosi p. Dumisani iNkosi q!
